*Mohammad Amin Saeidi,Hina Tabassum*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对大孔径阵列在超5G/6G多频段下的波束斜视与波束分裂问题，为基于均匀平面阵列的RIS建立了统一解析框架，覆盖连续与有限分辨率相位控制，并给出峰值条件、闭式峰值族、可行性条件及基于微扰的峰值位置修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超5G与6G系统的多频操作使大孔径阵列的波束方向随频率变化，RIS的相位配置频率与照射频率不一致时会产生波束失准；反射场出现主瓣偏移与额外主导瓣，严重降低波束成形性能，亟需对这些效应进行统一的理论刻画。

Method: 面向UPA结构RIS，在连续相位与b比特量化相位两种情形下建立解析框架。连续情形：推导峰值存在的充要条件，得出仰角-方位角峰值族的闭式表达，并给出以频率比、入射角、配置角及阵元间距为变量的可行性条件。量化情形：通过对量化误差相量的傅里叶展开，将量化方向图表示为谐波阵列响应的叠加，得到逐谐波峰值族闭式解、主导性排序与可行性条件；进一步提出基于主导谐波曲率与残余谐波梯度的微扰修正，以估计谐波叠加引起的峰值位移，避免二维穷举搜索。

Result: 数值仿真验证了解析结论，量化了峰值偏移与波束分裂概率；在多RIS、多用户、多频段网络中，将解析预测的斜视/分裂及谐波候选纳入波束选择后，展示了和速率增益。

Conclusion: 该统一解析框架可准确表征频率失配下RIS的波束斜视与波束分裂机理，并兼容有限相位分辨率，为多频多RIS系统的波束配置与候选选择提供低复杂度、可闭式计算的指导。

Abstract: Multi-frequency operation in beyond-5G and 6G systems renders the beam directions of large-aperture arrays frequency-dependent, giving rise to beam misalignment effects critical for reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs). When an RIS phase profile is configured at one frequency but illuminated at another, the reflected field may exhibit main-lobe deviation and additional dominant lobes, degrading beamforming performance. In this paper, we develop a unified analytical framework to characterize these effects for uniform planar array-based RISs under both continuous and finite-resolution phase control. We formalize beam-squint as the deviation of the maximum-gain beam from the desired elevation-azimuth angle pair and beam-split as the emergence of additional maximum-gain beams under frequency mismatch and quantization. For continuous-phase RISs, we derive necessary and sufficient peak conditions, obtain closed-form elevation-azimuth peak families, and establish feasibility conditions as functions of the frequency ratio, incidence and configuration angles, and element spacing. Extending the analysis to b-bit phase quantization, we express the quantized beampattern as a superposition of harmonic array responses via Fourier expansion of the quantization-error phasor, leading to closed-form per-harmonic peak families, dominance ordering, and feasibility conditions. To capture peak displacement induced by harmonic superposition, we further develop a perturbation-based correction leveraging dominant-harmonic curvature and residual-harmonic gradients, improving peak-location accuracy without exhaustive two-dimensional searches. Numerical simulations validate the analysis, quantify peak deviations and beam-split probabilities, and demonstrate sum-rate gains by including analytically predicted squinted/split and harmonic candidates in multi-RIS, multi-user, multi-band networks.

</details>


### [31] [Performance Analysis of RSMA-Enabled Bistatic ISAC in LEO Networks with Holographic Apertures and Fluid-Antenna Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08006)
*Wali Ullah Khan,Chandan Kumar Sheemar,Muhammad Adil,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对LEO卫星网络中幅度约束RHS与FAS用户支持的RSMA双站ISAC，建立了遍历性能分析框架，推导速率下界与感知SNR闭式解，并量化RHS方向效应与双站感知优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星ISAC需在有限星载孔径与幅度约束硬件下同时保障通信与感知性能，而RSMA、RHS、FAS的联合建模会引入共享幅度状态、端口选择耦合与方向相关增益等复杂因素，现有标量RHS效率模型与单站感知基准难以准确刻画其真实性能。

Method: 采用共享多馈源RHS单一幅度状态实现基于确定角度的公共参考波束与迫零私有参考波束，配合流特定馈源域预编码器，显式保留自增益、泄漏增益与目标方向增益；分别针对参考端口接收与best-of-P FAS接收推导保守的私有/公共速率下界并保持同端口选择耦合；感知侧在最近接收机关联与有限目标-接收机保护距离下给出平均双站感知SNR闭式解，并扩展至角度条件化足迹平均与角度调度。

Result: 蒙特卡洛结果验证了解析速率下界的紧致性与感知表达式的正确性；基准对比表明标量RHS效率模型会遗漏强方向相关效应；在N_RHS=16384、LEO高度400–1000 km条件下，最近地面接收机双站感知相较有利单站LEO参考可获得17.7–25.7 dB的平均SNR优势；FAS增益在富散射场景最大，而共享状态RHS实际目标增益并不随孔径单调变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为RHS/FAS赋能的RSMA双站ISAC提供可解析的遍历性能评估工具，揭示方向相关RHS建模必要性与双站感知的显著SNR收益，并指出FAS与孔径设计的适用条件。

Abstract: This paper develops an ergodic performance framework for rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA)-enabled bistatic integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in a low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite network with an amplitude-constrained reconfigurable holographic surface (RHS) and fluid-antenna-system (FAS) users. Deterministic angle-based common and zero-forcing private reference beams are realized through one shared multi-feed RHS amplitude state and stream-specific feed-domain precoders, and the resulting self-, leakage-, and target-direction gains are retained explicitly. Conservative private- and common-rate lower bounds are derived for both reference-port and best-of-$P$ FAS reception while preserving the same-port selection coupling. For sensing, a closed-form average bistatic sensing signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is obtained under nearest-receiver association and a finite target--receiver guard distance, with extensions to angle-conditioned footprint averaging and angular scheduling. Monte Carlo results confirm the tightness of the analytical rate bounds and validate the sensing expressions. Benchmarks show that scalar RHS-efficiency models can miss strong direction-dependent effects and that nearest-ground-receiver bistatic sensing provides a $17.7$--$25.7$~dB mean SNR advantage over a favorable monostatic LEO reference for $N_{\rm RHS}=16384$ over LEO altitudes of $400$--$1000$~km. FAS gains are largest in scattering-rich regimes, while the realized shared-state RHS target gain need not vary monotonically with aperture size.

</details>


### [32] [BeamFocusNet: Beamforming-based Explicit Spatial Signal Focusing for Robust DoA Estimation under Low-SNR and Single-Snapshot Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08187)
*Xuyao Deng,Qisheng Xu,Shuo Liu,Yong Dou,Wen Zhang,Kele Xu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出 BeamFocusNet，通过显式信号聚焦生成滤波器，提升低 SNR 和小快拍下 DoA 估计的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的 DoA 估计方法借鉴波束形成，隐式地同时聚焦信号与抑制噪声来生成滤波器，二者目标耦合导致训练难以平衡，在低信噪比和有限快拍下鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出 BeamFocusNet，以显式信号聚焦方式生成滤波器，为基于神经网络的滤波器生成引入新范式。

Result: 在多种挑战性条件下的实验表明该方法在 DoA 估计中具有优越性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式信号聚焦可解耦原有耦合目标，缓解训练平衡问题并增强低 SNR、小快拍场景下的鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: DoA estimation plays a crucial role in signal processing. Inspired by beamforming, recent works employ neural networks to estimate filters for filtering received signals to achieve DoA estimation. These methods typically generate filters by implicitly focusing signals and suppressing noise. However, this couples the two objectives, making training difficult to balance and resulting in poor robustness, especially under low SNR and limited snapshots. To address this issue, we propose the BeamFocusNet method, which generates filters through explicit signal focusing, introducing a new paradigm for neural network-based filter generation. Extensive experiments under various challenging conditions demonstrate the superiority and robustness of the proposed method in DoA estimation. Code is available at https://github.com/colaudiolab/BeamFocusNet.

</details>


### [33] [Understanding emotional and behavioural responses through physiological signal analysis recorded during theatrical performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08321)
*Aditi Site,Annariina Lohiranta,Tarmo Lipping*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究通过多模态生理传感（心率HR与皮肤电导SCL）记录现场戏剧演出中的观众反应，结合人口统计、信号可视化、场景与强度（z-score）分析框架，发现戏剧性、惊异、强烈场景引发最强激活（HR与SCL同增），共情场景反应较低且稳定，幽默场景呈中高激活，并识别出跨被试的共享响应模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 戏剧作为一种文化干预手段，可促进情绪表达、反思与社会联结，现场演出对心理健康有积极影响；然而在自然情境（如现场演出）中缺乏显式情绪标签，使得生理信号分析困难，因此需要探索如何在真实剧场环境中识别与情绪状态相关的生理模式。

Method: 在现场戏剧演出中采集多名被试的多模态生理数据；构建综合分析框架，包括人口统计分析、生理信号可视化探索、被试层面观察、逐场景生理分析，以及基于z-score归一化的强度分析，用以归纳情感与行为响应规律。

Result: 戏剧性、惊异和强烈场景产生最强生理激活，常伴随HR与SCL同步升高；共情驱动场景反应较低且更稳定；幽默场景引起中高程度激活，尤其在突发反应或响亮声音出现时；分析还发现被试间存在共享的生理响应模式，激活集中于关键情绪事件周围。

Conclusion: 联合使用HR与SCL测量可有效捕捉现场戏剧演出中观众参与度的变化，为理解剧场、情绪体验与幸福感之间的关系提供了生理层面的实证依据。

Abstract: Among various cultural interventions, theatre offers a powerful medium for emotional expression, reflection, and social connection. Live performances can positively influence mental well-being. Therefore, studying audience responses during theatrical performances can provide valuable insights into the relationship between theatre, emotional experiences, and well-being. Physiological responses have emerged as an important modality for identifying patterns associated with different emotional states. However, in naturalistic environments such as live performances, analysing these signals is challenging due to the absence of explicit labels. In this study, we focus on understanding emotional and behavioural responses using physiological data collected during a live theatrical performance. Data were recorded from participants using multiple sensing modalities. In addition, a comprehensive analytical framework was employed, which included demographic analysis, visual exploration of physiological signals, participant-level observations, scene-wise physiological analysis, and intensity-based analysis using z-score normalization to summarize the findings. The findings revealed that dramatic, surprising, and intense scenes produced the strongest activation, often with concurrent increases in HR and SCL, whereas empathy-driven scenes showed lower and more stable responses. Humorous scenes elicited moderate-to-high activation, particularly in the presence of sudden reactions or loud sounds. Analyses also identified shared physiological response patterns across participants, with activation concentrated around key emotional events. These results indicate that combined HR and SCL measures can effectively capture variations in audience engagement during live theatrical performances.

</details>


### [34] [Map-Free Single-Anchor Position Localization Using Multipath Uncertainty at Upper Mid-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08370)
*Xingchen Liu,Mingjun Ying,Xinquan Wang,Homa Nikbakht,Daniel Abraham,Theodore S. Rappaport*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的无地图单锚点定位框架，利用16.95 GHz室内方向性信道测量中相邻PDP观测的局部一致性估计各MPC角度协方差，并加权融合点/线约束，实现优于功率加权与文献基线的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G-Advanced与6G将利用方向性宽带信道进行基于路径的定位，但无地图室内定位受限于提取的多径分量（MPC）定位可靠性差异大；仅依赖接收功率难以刻画MPC可靠性，需要额外的可靠性信息来源。

Method: 基于纽约布鲁克林NYU WIRELESS采集的16.95 GHz室内方向性信道测量数据，利用邻近的、嵌入方向图信息的功率时延谱（PDP）观测的局部一致性估计每个MPC的角度协方差，并将该协方差通过点型与线型约束的加权融合传播到几何定位中，形成协方差加权定位框架，并与功率加权、无权变体及文献基线对比。

Result: 在20条链路（TX-RX间距11 m至97 m）上，保留5个MPC时，所提协方差加权方法的平均定位误差为3.86 m，中位数为2.66 m，70%链路误差在5 m以内；在相同点/线约束下优于功率加权与无权变体，也优于文献基线。

Conclusion: 局部方向一致性提供了接收功率之外的可靠性信息，表明协方差加权融合可有效提升无地图单锚点室内定位性能，为未来室内方向性系统的实用化无地图定位提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Future 5G-Advanced and 6G systems will exploit directional wideband channels for path-based localization, yet map-free indoor positioning remains challenging because extracted multipath components (MPCs) vary widely in localization reliability. This paper presents an uncertainty-aware map-free single-anchor localization framework using 16.95~GHz indoor directional channel measurements collected at NYU WIRELESS in Brooklyn, New York. Per-MPC angular covariance is estimated from the local consistency of neighboring pattern-de-embedded power delay profile (PDP) observations and propagated into geometric localization through weighted fusion of point-type and line-type constraints. Measurements across 20 links with TX--RX separations from 11~m to 97~m show that the proposed covariance-weighted method achieves a mean localization error of 3.86~m, a median of 2.66~m, and 70\% of links within 5~m using five retained MPCs. It outperforms the power-weighted and unweighted variants under identical point/line constraints, as well as the literature baselines. These results indicate that local directional consistency provides reliability information beyond received power alone and demonstrate a practical map-free approach for future indoor directional systems.

</details>


### [35] [PAPR-Aware Multimodal Token Transmission in MLLM-Based Multiuser Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08464)
*Molka Trabelsi,Rafik Zayani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出MAPS框架，通过两阶段训练与PAPR感知设计，在多用户OFDM信道下高效传输多模态token嵌入，兼顾模态一致性、任务相关性与PAPR抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLLM的任务导向语义通信需在OFDM信道上传输token嵌入，但硬件损伤（尤其高峰均比PAPR）在非线性功放下严重降低能效，且需同时保证跨模态一致性与任务相关性。

Method: MAPS采用两阶段训练：先联合跨模态对齐与PAPR抑制，再进行任务导向微调。文本分支引入可训练线性投影以利于梯度传播；设计基于方差的跨用户均衡多模态PAPR损失；将重建损失锚定到固定语义目标以保持跨模态对齐。在Modified Rapp功放下的OFDM信道传输多模态token嵌入。

Result: 仿真显示MAPS在各模态上实现均衡的PAPR抑制并保持强跨模态一致性；在3 dB IBO下，功放非线性场景中AVQA准确率提升64.5%，任务导向能效提升64.4%，优于基线方案。

Conclusion: MAPS验证了PAPR感知的多模态token传输在能效与任务性能上的有效性，为能效多模态语义通信提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Task-oriented semantic communication (SemCom) empowered by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for efficiently transmitting multimodal information, yet transmitting token embeddings over OFDM channels faces critical challenges due to hardware impairments, particularly the high peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) that severely degrades energy efficiency under nonlinear power amplifiers. In this paper, we propose a novel PAPR-aware task-oriented multimodal token transmission framework. Specifically, we propose MAPS (Multimodal AI-driven PAPR-aware Token Transmission) scheme for energy-efficient multiuser wireless communications. The key challenge is to jointly ensure inter-modal consistency, task-relevant token transmission, and PAPR reduction. To address this, we adopt a two-stage training strategy that integrates joint cross-modal alignment and PAPR reduction, followed by task-oriented fine-tuning. MAPS transmits multimodal token embeddings over an OFDM channel under a realistic Modified Rapp power amplifier. Specifically, we incorporate a trainable linear projection in the text branch for effective gradient propagation, design a balanced multimodal PAPR loss with variance-based equalization across users, and anchor the reconstruction loss to a fixed semantic target to preserve cross-modal alignment. Simulation results show that MAPS achieves balanced PAPR reduction across all modalities while maintaining strong inter-modal consistency. Under power amplifier nonlinearity, MAPS outperforms baseline schemes in both audio-visual question answering (AVQA) accuracy (64.5% gain) and task-oriented energy efficiency (64.4% gain) at an input back-off (IBO) of 3 dB, highlighting its effectiveness for energy-efficient multimodal semantic communication.

</details>


### [36] [A Foundation Model for Large-Scale Wireless Network Planning , Operation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08482)
*Xinyu Qin,Wenqiang Pu,Hongcheng Dong,Bingsheng Peng,Ye Xue,Linzhi Shen,Qi Yan,Tsung-Hui Chang,Zhi-Quan Luo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基础模型 ChaRT，从运营蜂窝网络 routinely 产生的测量报告学习可迁移的无线射频表征，单套权重即可跨城重建无线环境并支持多类下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠连接依赖精确的无线环境模型，其支撑基站部署、网络运维与参数优化；但城市级无线环境难以刻画：基于物理的工具需详尽的站点描述与大量计算，任务专用模型则依赖专门测量且跨部署迁移能力差。

Method: ChaRT 从运营蜂窝网络的测量报告学习可迁移射频表征；测量报告天然提供跨多小区、多波束的联合观测，无需额外测量活动。架构中融入波束级角度结构、网络层级与传播环境多样性，并采用上下文感知掩码波束建模与自蒸馏进行预训练。

Result: 在单城市 3,503 个小区、超 10 亿条报告、182 亿波束级观测上训练。单套权重即可在未见城市重建无线环境，并迁移到新站点预测、无线电地图构建与网络参数调优；仅用 1% 标注数据即可支持用户定位、波束预测、传播场景分类与 SINR 估计；所学表征还可支持波束空间聚类以构建可复用无线栅格。

Conclusion: 运营测量报告可作为可扩展的数据基础，支撑可迁移的蜂窝网络智能，为城市级无线环境建模提供了不依赖专门测量活动的新范式。

Abstract: Wireless cellular networks provide critical infrastructure for communication, transportation and industry, making reliable connectivity essential to modern society. Delivering this connectivity requires accurate models of the radio environment shaped jointly by network infrastructure and their surroundings. Such models underpin base-station deployment, network operation and parameter optimization, yet city-scale radio environments remain difficult to capture. Physics-based tools require detailed site descriptions and computation, whereas task-specific models need dedicated measurements and transfer poorly across deployments. Here we introduce ChaRT, a foundation model that learns transferable radio representations from measurement reports routinely generated by operational cellular networks. These reports provide abundant joint observations across multiple cells and beams without additional measurement campaigns. ChaRT incorporates beam-level angular structure, network hierarchy and propagation-regime diversity into its architecture, and is pretrained through context-aware masked beam modelling and self-distillation. We train ChaRT on more than one billion reports comprising 18.2 billion beam-level observations from 3,503 cells in one city. With a single set of weights, ChaRT reconstructs radio environments in unseen cities and transfers to new-site prediction, radio map construction and network parameter tuning. With only 1% of labelled data, it supports user localization, beam prediction, propagation scenario classification and estimation of the signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio. The learned representation further enables beamspace clustering for reusable radio-grid construction. These results establish operational measurement reports as a scalable data foundation for transferable cellular-network intelligence.

</details>


### [37] [SCALE-X: A Systematic Complexity-Aware Low-Precision Approach for Digital Predistortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08489)
*Anees Rehman,Mohd Tasleem Khan,George Goussetis,Yuan Ding,João F. C. Mota,Jaiyu Hou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: SCALE-X：面向功率放大器的系统性、复杂度感知、低精度、基于模型的数字预失真技术，通过复杂度感知系数剪枝与优化定点建模，实现计算开销大幅降低而精度损失很小，并经FPGA实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率放大器数字预失真（DPD）在提升线性度的同时带来显著计算开销，硬件实现中精度与复杂度难以兼顾，需要一种复杂度感知、低精度且能准确刻画量化效应的方法来优化实现权衡。

Method: 提出新颖的复杂度感知系数剪枝方法，并与优化的定点建模相结合；对模型阶数、记忆深度和系数字宽进行针对性配置，以更准确捕获量化效应并支持更合理的实现决策。

Result: 在不大幅牺牲精度的前提下显著削减计算开销；FPGA实现展示了实际权衡关系并提升了整体效率。

Conclusion: SCALE-X为PA的DPD提供了一种系统性、复杂度感知的低精度建模方案，能够在精度与硬件开销之间取得良好平衡，具备实际实现价值。

Abstract: This letter presents SCALE-X, a systematic, complexity-aware, low-precision, model-based digital predistortion (DPD) technique for power amplifiers with enhanced capabilities. The proposed approach employs a novel complexity-aware coefficient pruning method, which, when combined with optimized fixed-point modeling, enables more accurate capture of quantization effects and supports better informed decisions for implementation. Targeted configuration of model order, memory depth, and coefficient bitwidth delivers dramatic cuts in computational overhead without much sacrificing accuracy. Field-programmable gate array implementation demonstrates practical trade-offs with improved overall efficiency.

</details>


### [38] [Masked Latent Prediction of CSI for Indoor Localization in Integrated Sensing and Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08509)
*Ibtissam Labriji,Mahdi Maleki,Pavan Koteshwar Srinath*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于JEPA自监督框架的CSI室内定位方法，预训练编码器后冻结，仅训练轻量回归头，在DICHASUS-005x数据集上将平均定位误差从0.90 m降至0.42 m（降低53%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSI指纹定位面临域偏移、标注数据有限以及多径丰富环境下性能退化的问题，需要减少对标签的依赖并提升鲁棒性。

Method: 基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的自监督定位框架：将每个CSI时间快照视为一个token，通过从可见快照预测被掩码快照的潜在嵌入来预训练编码器，无需任何标签；随后冻结编码器，仅在小规模有标签位置集上训练轻量回归头估计用户位置。

Result: 在实测DICHASUS-005x数据集（单天线发射、32天线阵列接收、多径丰富室内环境）上评估，采用50%掩码比例时，平均定位误差由监督CNN基线的0.90 m降至0.42 m，降幅53%（0.48 m）。

Conclusion: 该框架凭借标签高效、冻结编码器的设计，契合通感一体化（ISAC）目标，能以极少的标注与计算开销实现可靠感知。

Abstract: Channel State Information (CSI)-based fingerprinting can enable accurate indoor localization but suffers from domain shift, limited labeled data, and degraded performance in multipath-rich environments. To address these challenges, we propose a self-supervised localization framework built on a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA). Each CSI time snapshot is treated as a token, and the encoder is pre-trained by predicting the latent embeddings of masked snapshots from the visible ones, learning robust channel representations without any labels. The encoder is then frozen, and only a lightweight regression head is trained on a small set of labeled positions to estimate the user location. We evaluate the framework on the measured DICHASUS-005x dataset, a single-antenna transmitter received by a 32-antenna array in a multipath-rich indoor environment. With a 50% masking ratio, the proposed method reduces the mean localization error from 0.90 m to 0.42 m (a 53% reduction, or 0.48 m) relative to a supervised Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) baseline trained on raw CSI. Owing to its label-efficient, frozen-encoder design, the framework aligns with integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) objectives, enabling reliable sensing with minimal labeling and compute overhead.

</details>


### [39] [FedGenSC: Federated Generative Semantic Communication with Channel-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08593)
*Rita Abou Fares,Razan Al Kakoun,Maher Nouiehed,Hadi Sarieddeen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出FedGenSC，将GAN引入联邦语义通信，解决鉴别器聚合不稳定、语义漂移与信道无关生成三大失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信道失真下，生成式先验比判别式解码器更能恢复语义保真度，因此将GAN与联邦语义通信结合是自然演进。但朴素的GAN联邦会引入三个此前未被识别和解决的失效模式：非IID数据下的鉴别器聚合不稳定、局部嵌入空间差异导致的语义漂移、以及无法适应异构链路条件的信道无关生成。

Method: FedGenSC采用全局生成器与仅本地鉴别器的架构以稳定聚合；通过语义原型库在客户端之间提供跨客户端语义信息；并将生成过程以瞬时信噪比（SNR）为条件以适配异构信道。

Result: 在Europarl数据集、瑞利衰落信道（K=10客户端，Dirichlet α=0.5）下，非IID条件下FedGenSC在测试SNR范围内优于FedDeepSC基线，18 dB时BLEU-1相对提升最高达58.2%。消融实验证实各组件均有独立贡献。

Conclusion: FedGenSC通过本地鉴别器、语义原型库与SNR条件生成，系统性缓解了GAN联邦语义通信的三类失效模式，并验证了各模块的有效性与独立贡献。

Abstract: Integrating generative adversarial networks (GANs) into federated semantic communication (SemCom) is a natural progression, as generative priors can recover semantic fidelity under channel distortion that discriminative decoders cannot. However, naive GAN federation introduces three failure modes that prior work has, to the best of our knowledge, neither identified nor resolved: discriminator aggregation instability under non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data, semantic drift caused by divergent local embedding spaces, and channel-agnostic generation that cannot adapt to heterogeneous link conditions. We propose federated generative semantic communication (FedGenSC), which mitigates all three by employing a global generator with local-only discriminators, providing cross-client semantic information through a semantic prototype bank, and conditioning generation on the instantaneous signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Experiments on the Europarl dataset over Rayleigh fading channels (K=10 clients, Dirichlet α=0.5) show that FedGenSC under non-IID data outperforms the FedDeepSC baseline across the tested SNR range, achieving up to a 58.2% relative improvement in bilingual evaluation understudy (BLEU)-1 at 18 dB. Ablation studies confirm the independent contribution of each component.

</details>


### [40] [Efficient Deep Learning Adaptation for Cross-Environment RSS-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08629)
*Cien Zhang,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于主干复用与高效适配的场景自适应RSS室内定位框架，通过轻量提取器与共享主干的解耦设计提升跨环境泛化能力与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSS室内定位成本低且兼容现有无线基础设施，但RSS测量对环境变化高度敏感，导致深度学习定位模型难以在不同物理配置间泛化，跨场景迁移时需重新训练且效率低。

Method: 设计"轻量提取器+共享主干"架构：提取器将异构RSS输入投影至统一特征空间，主干捕捉可迁移的定位知识。适配阶段复用源数据集训练的主干，仅优化提取器以适配新数据集配置，并引入短优化阶段以较低学习率对主干进行微调。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，该训练策略相比从头训练收敛更快、适配效果更好；同时在不同数据集配置下均降低了训练时间。

Conclusion: 主干复用与高效适配机制可有效且高效地实现场景自适应的RSS室内定位，验证了框架的有效性与效率。

Abstract: Received signal strength (RSS)-based indoor localization has attracted increasing attention due to its low cost and compatibility with existing wireless infrastructures. However, RSS measurements are highly sensitive to environmental variations, making it challenging for deep learning-based localization models to generalize across different physical configurations. Driven by these challenges, this paper proposes a scenario-adaptive RSS localization framework based on backbone reuse and efficient adaptation. The proposed architecture consists of a lightweight extractor and a shared backbone, where the extractor projects heterogeneous RSS inputs into a unified feature space and the backbone captures transferable localization knowledge. During adaptation, the backbone trained from the source dataset is reused, while the extractor is optimized to adapt to the new dataset configuration. A short optimization stage is further introduced to slightly refine the backbone with a lower learning rate. Experimental results on four datasets demonstrate that the proposed training strategy enables faster convergence and improved adaptation compared with training from scratch. In addition, the proposed framework reduces training time under different dataset configurations, verifying its effectiveness and efficiency for adaptive RSS-based indoor localization.

</details>


### [41] [CrossRAFT: Cross-Domain Complex-Valued Feature Extraction for Ultrasound Motion Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08631)
*Yang Leng,Yuchen Tang,Kai-Hang Yiu,Yik-Chung Wu,Wei-Ning Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出CrossRAFT，一种基于复值神经网络的端到端亚采样超声运动估计方法，显式利用相位信息，在多种数据集上优于实值方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维运动估计是超声应用的基础。传统实值RF、解析和IQ信号方法计算量大，且受帧间去相关、分辨率折衷、横向缺乏载波与采样不足等问题困扰。现有深度学习模型虽推理更快，但均为实值架构，将复分量视为独立通道，破坏了相位耦合，影响运动估计精度。

Method: 基于RAFT架构提出CrossRAFT，采用复值编码器和自定义相关模块，直接从复数超声信号中提取并利用相位信息，实现端到端可训练的亚采样运动估计。在真实RF、解析和IQ三种信号表示下，于仿真体模、合成超声心动图及私有在体超声心动图数据集上进行评估。

Result: CrossRAFT在复杂组织运动中及困难的横向方向上均能准确估计位移。与基于实值RF的RAFT相比，基于解析信号的CrossRAFT在合成超声心动图数据上将垂直和水平位移误差分别降低69%和51%。

Conclusion: 通过复值神经网络利用相位信息为超声运动估计提供了强大框架，在临床弹性成像和功能成像中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate multi-dimensional motion estimation is fundamental to broad biomedical ultrasound applications, primarily performed using real-valued radio-frequency (RF), analytic, and in-phase and quadrature (IQ) signals. Real-valued RF and analytic signals offer high subsample accuracy but are computationally intensive. Regardless of the signal domain used, traditional algorithms suffer from interframe decorrelation, resolution tradeoffs, and a lack of a carrier and low sampling in the lateral direction. Emerging deep learning (DL) models demonstrate improved performance and faster inference. However, existing architectures are real-valued and disrupt inherent phase coupling crucial for accurate motion estimation by treating complex components as independent channels. To address this, we propose CrossRAFT, an end-to-end trainable network designed for subsample motion estimation directly from complex-valued ultrasound signals. Built on the RAFT architecture, CrossRAFT features complex-valued encoders and a custom correlation module to explicitly extract and use phase information. We evaluated CrossRAFT across real RF, analytic, and IQ signal presentations on simulated phantoms, synthetic echocardiographic, and private in vivo echocardiographic datasets. Experimental results show that CrossRAFT estimates displacements accurately under complex tissue motion and in the challenging lateral direction. Compared to real RF-based RAFT, analytic-based CrossRAFT reduces vertical and horizontal displacement errors by 69% and 51% on the synthetic echocardiographic data, respectively. These findings demonstrate that leveraging phase information via complex-valued neural networks provides a powerful framework for ultrasound motion estimation, showing potential for clinical elastography and functional imaging.

</details>


### [42] [Partial Superimposed Pilot-Aided Sparse Vector Transmission for High-Mobility URLLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08661)
*Yanfeng Zhang,Jinkai Zheng,Hui Liang,Wen Wu,Tom H. Luan,Di Zhang,Xu Zhu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出部分叠加导频辅助稀疏向量传输（PSP-SVT）方案，用于高移动性场景下短包超可靠低时延通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高移动性场景下短包URLLC需要高效的信道跟踪与数据检测，而传统全叠加导频辅助SVT方案存在严重的导频-数据干扰问题。

Method: 在部分子载波上部署少量导频，形成稀疏导频结构以支撑基于基扩展模型（BEM）的信道跟踪；基于PSP模式设计迭代接收机，联合进行信道估计与数据解码。

Result: PSP-SVT降低了导频干扰，获得更准确的初始信道估计与数据检测，改善了后续迭代精化并抑制误差传播；仿真表明其BLER性能优于现有全叠加导频辅助SVT方案，且收敛速度快。同时分析了导频数量与功率分配比对BLER的影响，揭示了近最优导频配置。

Conclusion: PSP-SVT是一种适用于高移动短包URLLC的高效传输方案，能够在BLER与收敛速度上超越现有全叠加导频方案。

Abstract: A partial superimposed pilot-aided sparse vector transmission (PSP-SVT) scheme is proposed for short-packet ultra-reliable and low-latency communications in high-mobility scenarios. Unlike conventional full superimposed pilot-aided SVT schemes, the proposed PSP-SVT scheme deploys only a few pilots over a subset of subcarriers. This sparse pilot structure is sufficient for basis expansion model based channel tracking while effectively reducing pilot-data interference. Based on the PSP pattern, an iterative receiver is developed to jointly perform channel estimation and data decoding. The reduced pilot interference in PSP-SVT provides more accurate initial channel estimation and data detection, thereby improving the subsequent iterative refinement and mitigating their error propagation. Moreover, the impacts of the number of PSPs and power allocation ratio on block error rate (BLER) performance are investigated to reveal the near-optimal pilot configuration. Simulation results show that the proposed PSP-SVT scheme outperforms existing full superimposed pilot-aided SVT schemes in terms of BLER with fast convergence speed.

</details>


### [43] [A Material-Aware Channel Model for Efficient CKM Generation via Environment Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08859)
*Xuancheng Zhu,Zhenjun Dong,Yong Zeng,Cheng-Xiang Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种材料感知信道模型，显式刻画散射体材料对无线信道的影响，并基于此设计迭代梯度下降的材料重建算法，通过全波仿真验证了模型与算法的有效性，为高效生成信道知识地图（CKM）提供支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中信道知识地图（CKM）的精准生成依赖于对环境的高精度重建，包括3D几何结构与散射体材料。几何结构可较易从LiDAR点云等多模态观测中获取，但材料属性难以直接由稀疏信道测量获得，原因在于缺乏显式关联材料与信道的信道模型。因此亟需填补这一空白，建立材料与无线信道之间的显式联系。

Method: 提出材料感知信道模型（material-aware channel model），显式表征散射体材料对无线信道的影响；在该模型基础上，设计了一种基于迭代梯度下降（iterative gradient descent）的材料重建算法，从稀疏信道测量中反演材料属性。

Result: 全波仿真（full wave simulation）结果验证了所提出的材料感知信道模型的正确性以及材料重建算法的有效性，展示了其通过环境重建实现高效CKM生成的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作为从稀疏信道测量中重建散射体材料提供了理论与算法基础，打通了材料属性与无线信道之间的显式建模链路，有望推动环境感知无线通信、感知与定位中高效CKM生成的发展。

Abstract: Channel knowledge map (CKM) is a promising technology for environment-aware wireless communication, sensing, and localization in 6G networks. Accurate CKM generation requires precise reconstruction of the environment, including 3D geometries and scatterer materials, typically from multi-modal sensory observations such as LiDAR point clouds and sparse channel measurements. While the former is relatively easy to acquire, materials remain difficult to obtain directly from sparse channel measurements due to the lack of an explicit channel model linking them. To fill this gap, this paper proposes a material-aware channel model that explicitly characterizes the influence of scatterer materials on the wireless channel. Based on this model, an iterative gradient descent based material reconstruction algorithm is proposed. Full wave simulation results validate the developed model and the proposed algorithm, demonstrating their potentials for efficient CKM generation via environment reconstruction.

</details>


### [44] [Omnidirectional UMi Path Loss Models for 3GPP Extension Above 100 GHz Using Extensive Empirical Data Sets from 6.75 to 142 GHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09092)
*Xingchen Liu,Homa Nikbakht,Mingjun Ying,Dipankar Shakya,Hitesh Poddar,Art Ishii,Daniel Abraham,Theodore S. Rappaport*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 基于6.75–142 GHz的实测数据，论文推导了UMi场景的单频与多频全向路损模型，认为CI/CIF模型在跨频段扩展中参数更稳定，可支撑0.5–150 GHz的3GPP UMi路损建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将标准化UMi路损模型从FR3扩展至sub-THz频段，需要在统一框架下开展跨宽频谱的实测对比，以验证模型的可扩展性。

Method: 基于NYU WIRELESS在6.75、16.95、28、73、142 GHz的LOS与NLOS实测数据，建立单频CI与FI模型，并在7–24 GHz、0.5–100 GHz、0.5–150 GHz范围内对比CI、CIF与ABG多频模型。

Result: 单频下CI参数更稳定且物理可解释，阴影衰落标准差与FI相差不超过0.98 dB；在逐步扩宽的频段中，CI/CIF的距离指数保持稳定并与3GPP UMi解释一致，而ABG仅以很小的拟合误差改善换取更高的参数敏感性。

Conclusion: 支持采用带1 m近距自由空间锚点的CI/CIF公式，将面向3GPP的UMi路损模型扩展至0.5–150 GHz全频段。

Abstract: Extending standardized urban microcell (UMi) path loss (PL) models from the upper mid-band (FR3) to sub-terahertz (sub-THz) frequencies requires measurement-based comparisons spanning a wide spectral range within a unified framework. In this paper, we derive single- and multi-frequency omnidirectional PL models for UMi environments based on extensive NYU WIRELESS measurements at 6.75, 16.95, 28, 73, and 142 GHz in line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions. Single-frequency close-in (CI) and floating-intercept (FI) models for each band show that CI yields more stable and physically interpretable parameters, with shadow fading standard deviations within 0.98 dB of FI across all five frequencies. We then extend the multi-frequency PL analysis to the 7-24 GHz, 0.5-100 GHz, and 0.5-150 GHz frequency ranges using CI, close-in with frequency weighting (CIF), and alpha-beta-gamma (ABG) models. Across progressively wider frequency spans, CI and CIF maintain stable distance exponents that remain closely aligned with the 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) UMi interpretations, whereas ABG offers only very modest reductions in fitting error at the cost of much greater parameter sensitivity. These results support physically anchored CI/CIF formulations with a close-in free space path loss anchor point at 1 m in order to extend 3GPP-oriented UMi PL models over the entire 0.5 to 150 GHz frequency range.

</details>
