### [45] [WIP: Energy-Efficient LLM-Based Serving Cluster Formulation in Cell-Free Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05725)
*Marcin Hoffmann,Paweł Kryszkiewicz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI智能体来解决6G无蜂窝大规模MIMO网络中的服务簇形成（SCF）问题，以提升能量效率（EE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升6G无线网络能量效率的一种途径是更高效地利用现有网络基础设施，这可通过引入以用户为中心的无蜂窝大规模MIMO（UCCF MMMIMO）实现，即允许多个基站同时服务单个用户。其关键挑战在于确定哪些基站应服务某个给定用户，即服务簇形成（SCF）问题。

Method: 提出使用基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI智能体来处理SCF问题，并以提升能量效率为目标；采用复杂的、基于3D射线追踪的蜂窝网络仿真器进行评估，对比了若干GPT模型与当前最先进算法。

Result: 与基线相比，所提出的AI智能体在能量效率上最高取得32%的增益。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的AI智能体可有效用于UCCF MMMIMO中的服务簇形成决策，并在能量效率上优于基线方案。

Abstract: One way to increase the Energy Efficiency (EE) of 6G wireless networks is to utilize existing network infrastructure more efficiently. This can be achieved by introducing User-Centric Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input-Multiple-Output (UCCF MMMIMO), which allows for simultaneously serving a single user by multiple Base Stations (BSs). From this perspective, the key challenge is to decide which BSs should serve a given user, known as the Serving Cluster Formulation (SCF). In this paper, we propose to deal with this problem by using an Artificial Intelligence (AI) agent based on a Large Language Model (LLM), targeting improvement of EE. We evaluated the proposed AI agent using a complex, 3D Ray Tracer-based, cellular network simulator, comparing a few GPT models and state-of-the-art algorithms. The results show up to 32% gain in EE of the proposed AI agent compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [46] [SDN-Orchestrated Dual-Path 5G/SATCOM Maritime Communications for Carrier Strike Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05729)
*Avinash Srinivasan,Dalibor Španjević,Kevin H. Nguyen,Bannon Ireton,Christopher B. Landis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SDN/SDR集成框架统一5G与卫星链路，实现舰载打击群双路径故障切换，仿真显示其可靠性显著优于单链路方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 舰载打击群通信需在距离、天气、衰落与间歇中断导致质量波动的异构链路上维持任务流量，单链路方案可靠性不足，需要跨层统一控制与故障切换机制。

Method: 构建集中式SDN控制器的SDN/SDR框架，耦合AMC下的Rician衰落5G信道、含ITU-R P.618/P.838雨衰的Ku波段SATCOM信道及两链路的Gilbert-Elliott突发可用性过程，控制器按业务类别与链路状态逐包路由；采用随机仿真，在Norfolk、挪威海、菲律宾海、北太平洋风暴带四区域评估，每组参数60次独立试验、每次5万数据包，使用共同随机数、配对置信区间及收敛诊断。

Result: Norfolk天气基线中SDN路由可靠性93.09%，高于5G-only的36.22%与SATCOM-only的89.87%，配对增益分别为+56.87与+3.22个百分点；在低Rician-K与高突发故障联合压力下优势扩大，AMC在SINR恶化时提升5G鲁棒性；SDN在四区域均为最高可靠性模式。

Conclusion: 跨层SDN控制的故障切换无需依赖确定性链路抽象即可提升海上通信韧性，是异构链路下维持舰载打击群任务流量的有效方案。

Abstract: Carrier Strike Group (CSG) communications must sustain mission traffic across heterogeneous links whose quality varies with distance, weather, fading, and intermittent outages. We present an integrated Software-Defined Networking (SDN)/Software-Defined Radio (SDR) framework that unifies 5G and satellite communications (SATCOM) under a single control plane for dual-path failover in CSG communications. The framework is evaluated via stochastic simulation across four operational regions: Norfolk, the Norwegian Sea, the Philippine Sea, and the North Pacific storm track. The framework couples a centralized SDN controller with a Rician-faded 5G channel under Adaptive Modulation and Coding (AMC), a Ku-band SATCOM channel with ITU-R P.618/P.838 rain attenuation, and Gilbert-Elliott burst-availability processes on both links. The controller routes each packet by traffic class and current link state. Experiments use 60 independent trials of 50,000 packets per parameter setting with common random numbers, paired confidence intervals, and convergence diagnostics across fault, fading, and region sweeps. For the Norfolk weather baseline, SDN routing reaches 93.09% reliability versus 36.22% (5G-only) and 89.87% (SATCOM-only), with paired gains of +56.87 and +3.22 percentage points. The advantage grows under joint low-Rician-K/high-burst-fault stress, and AMC improves 5G robustness as Signal-to-Interference-plus-Noise Ratio degrades. SDN remains the highest-reliability mode across all four regions, indicating that cross-layer SDN-controlled failover improves maritime resilience without relying on deterministic link abstractions.

</details>


### [47] [Semantic Communication for Distributed Spectrum Monitoring over Unreliable Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05791)
*Samer Lahoud,Kinda Khawam*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文将分布式辐射源计数与定位建模为不可靠上报链路上的语义通信问题：各接收机将本地时频观测编码为紧凑潜表示并发送，融合节点将存活潜表示与接收机位置配对后以集合方式处理，端到端训练中嵌入上报擦除与有限速率量化，实验证明该方案在擦除下更鲁棒、跨接收机数量泛化且低速率即可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式频谱监测需由空间分离的接收机推断无线电环境状态，核心任务是确定活跃辐射源数量与位置。但共享链路存在竞争、衰落或能量受限，导致整条上报被擦除，将所有接收机的原始I/Q样本回传至融合节点不现实，因此需要低速率、抗擦除的上报机制。

Method: 将任务形式化为不可靠上报链路下的语义通信：每个接收机把本地时频观测映射为紧凑潜表示并传输；融合节点将每个存活潜表示与其对应的接收机位置配对，在无序集合上运算，使融合规则兼容集合成员变动与接收机数量变化；编码器与解码器以任务目标为导向端到端训练，训练中显式引入上报擦除，并可选地引入有限速率量化。

Result: 在合成多辐射源场景中：信道感知训练提升了擦除条件下的鲁棒性；集合融合比固定顺序拼接更鲁棒；单一训练模型无需重训即可适配不同接收机数量；速率研究表明所测设置下每个潜分量仅需少数比特，且速率拐点在不同擦除水平下大致保持稳定。

Conclusion: 结果支持在不可靠链路上采用低速率语义上报实现分布式频谱监测的可行性。

Abstract: Distributed spectrum monitoring relies on spatially separated receivers to infer the state of the radio environment. A central task is to determine how many emitters are active and where they are located. Sensors typically report over shared unreliable links, where contention, fading, or energy limits erase whole reports, so forwarding raw I/Q samples from all receivers to a fusion node becomes impractical. This paper formulates distributed emitter counting and localization as a semantic communication problem over unreliable reporting links. Each receiver maps its local time-frequency observation to a compact latent representation and transmits it. The fusion node pairs each surviving latent with the corresponding receiver position and operates on the resulting unordered set, which keeps the fusion rule compatible with variable membership and receiver-count changes. The encoder and decoder are trained end-to-end with report erasure, and optionally finite-rate quantization, inside the task objective. Experiments on synthetic multi-emitter scenes show that channel-aware training improves robustness under erasure, that set fusion is more robust than fixed-order concatenation, and that one trained model operates across receiver counts without retraining. The rate study shows that a few bits per latent component suffice in the tested setting, and that the rate knee remains roughly stable across erasure levels. These results support the feasibility of low-rate semantic reporting for distributed spectrum monitoring over unreliable links.

</details>


### [48] [HalifaxDT: A Wireless Digital Twin from Open Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05793)
*Alexis Delplace,Xinyi Li,Samer Lahoud,Nur Zincir-Heywood*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出HalifaxDT——基于异构开放地理空间与频谱数据构建的哈利法克斯半岛城市级无线数字孪生，并集成Sionna RT进行仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级无线数字孪生对站点特定分析、规划与实验有重要价值，但在缺乏精确3D城市模型时构建困难，亟需利用开放、异构数据源的可复现构建方法。

Method: 整合建筑轮廓、LiDAR高程产品、建筑元数据与频谱许可记录，通过调和多源建筑高度信息并保留几何决策溯源的工作流，构建地形、建筑与网关元数据，并导入Sionna RT进行无线仿真。评估含两个用例：一是与实地测量参考图及解析基线对比覆盖预测；二是预测正常接收信号并检测干扰。

Result: 覆盖预测结果显示，HalifaxDT比解析基线更好地保持了实测无线电地图的空间结构；干扰研究表明，相对预测参考的偏差可揭示仅凭接收功率难以检测的干扰。

Conclusion: HalifaxDT验证了以开放异构数据构建城市级无线数字孪生的可行性；当前保真度局限包括简化的材料表示、植被缺失，以及需要更广泛的RF校准与实时测量同步。

Abstract: Wireless digital twins can support site-specific analysis, planning, and experimentation for future wireless networks. However, constructing them at city scale remains challenging when accurate 3D city models are unavailable. This paper presents HalifaxDT, a wireless digital twin of the Halifax Peninsula in Nova Scotia, Canada, constructed from heterogeneous open geospatial and spectrum data. HalifaxDT combines building footprints, LiDAR elevation products, building metadata, and spectrum licensing records through a workflow that reconciles multiple sources of building-height information while preserving the provenance of geometry decisions. The resulting terrain, buildings, and gateway metadata are integrated into Sionna RT for wireless simulation. We evaluate HalifaxDT through two use cases. The first compares its coverage predictions with a reference derived from field measurements and with an analytical baseline. The second uses the digital twin to predict the received signal under normal operating conditions and detect interference. The coverage results show that HalifaxDT better preserves the spatial structure of the measured radio map than the analytical baseline. The interference study shows that deviations from the predicted reference can reveal interference that is difficult to detect from received power alone. We also identify current fidelity limitations, including simplified material representation, missing vegetation, and the need for broader RF calibration and synchronization with live measurements.

</details>


### [49] [Comparative Performance of Graphene-Enabled Transmitarray Antenna and Reflectors for Wide-Angle Terahertz Beam Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05803)
*Somayeh Komeylian,Truong Nguyen,Christopher Paolini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文比较了半球形石墨烯透射阵列天线与平面反射阵列天线在太赫兹波段宽角波束扫描中的性能，证明透射阵列在扫描范围、波束宽度稳定性及方向性等方面更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向太赫兹波段宽角波束扫描应用，需要评估不同天线构型（半球形透射阵列与平面反射阵列）在波束扫描范围、波束宽度稳定性与方向性等关键指标上的差异，以明确更优的天线方案。

Method: 建立平面反射阵列的理论框架并进行数值评估，与基于石墨烯的半球形透射阵列进行对比分析；通过方位角/俯仰角扫描范围、HPBW变化、方向功率密度、方向性和增益等指标进行量化比较，并与文献中报道的平面反射阵列及天线的实验结果进行对照。

Result: 平面反射阵列的俯仰角波束扫描范围为±60°，而透射阵列可扩展至±78°并保持360°方位角全覆盖；平面反射阵列的HPBW变化较大（26.48°），透射阵列仅为13.1°。因此平面反射阵列反射波束分布更宽，方向功率密度、方向性和增益降低，而透射阵列在宽扫描范围内保持更稳定可控的波束宽度与更高的方向功率集中度。

Conclusion: 半球形石墨烯透射阵列在太赫兹宽角波束扫描中相较平面反射阵列具有显著性能优势，且该优势通过与文献实验结果的对比以及其余指标的全面评估得到进一步验证。

Abstract: This work compares a hemispherical graphene-based transmitarray antenna with its planar reflector counterpart for wide-angle beam steering in the THz regime. The theoretical framework of the planar reflector is formulated and numerically evaluated, yielding an elevation beam-steering range of \pm60°. In contrast, the transmitarray extends the elevation steering range to \pm78° while maintaining full 360° azimuthal coverage. The planar reflector exhibits a larger HPBW variation of 26.48°, compared with 13.1° for the transmitarray, resulting in a broader reflected-beam distribution and reduced directional power density, directivity, and gain. Meanwhile, the transmitarray maintains a more stable and controllable beamwidth response with greater directional power concentration over a wide steering range. The performance advantages of the transmitarray are demonstrated through comparisons with experimental results reported in the literature for planar reflectors and antennas. We further provide a comprehensive assessment of the performance advantages of the transmitarray over planar configurations across the remaining metrics.

</details>


### [50] [Biased Backpressure Routing for Multihop Wireless Networks with Heterogeneous Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05844)
*Yujun Ming,Zhongyuan Zhao,Fikadu Dagefu,Justin Kong,Terrence Moore,Kevin Chan,Ananthram Swami,Santiago Segarra*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将异构多跳无线网络中的分组路由、链路调度与接口分配联合优化，通过虚拟子节点建模把接口分配转化为设备内虚拟路由，并用多层SP-BP方案联合求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多跳无线网络（Het-MuNets）在战术网络、车联网、无线回传及非地面连接等场景中具有前景，但异构技术在天线穿透、干扰与带宽等方面差异显著，需逐跳决定分组到接口的分配，使路由与调度变得高度复杂，现有方法难以同时兼顾。

Method: 将分组到接口的分配建模为虚拟子节点之间的传输，从而把接口分配转化为设备内虚拟路由；在此基础上，将其与物理路由、链路调度在统一的多层最短路径偏置Backpressure（SP-BP）方案下联合求解，支持多并发流。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提框架在吞吐量（goodput）、时延和分组投递率方面均优于在其他基线图模型上运行的SP-BP以及非Backpressure路由方案。

Conclusion: 通过虚拟子节点建模将接口分配纳入统一的路由与调度框架，可有效解决Het-MuNets中跨层联合优化问题，并在多并发流场景下取得优于基线的性能。

Abstract: Heterogeneous-interface multihop wireless networks (Het-MuNets) are emerging as a promising paradigm for tactical networks and for infrastructure-light applications such as vehicular communications, wireless backhaul, and non-terrestrial connectivity. To exploit the diverse profiles of heterogeneous communication technologies in penetration, interference, and bandwidth, packet-to-interface assignment must be determined on a per-hop basis, making routing and scheduling highly complex. In this work, we develop a unified framework for joint packet routing, link scheduling, and interface assignment in Het-MuNets with multiple concurrent flows. By modeling packet-to-interface assignment as transmission between virtual subnodes, we transform interface assignment into intra-device virtual routing, which is solved jointly with physical routing and scheduling under a unified multi-layer shortest path-biased Backpressure (SP-BP) scheme. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms SP-BP operating on other baseline graph models and non-backpressure routing schemes in goodput, latency, and packet delivery rate.

</details>


### [51] [SLA-Safe Energy Control for AI-Native NG-RAN Using Stability-Aware Constrained PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05861)
*Dharmendra Kumar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稳定性感知的约束强化学习框架，用于5G NG-RAN中满足SLA的节能控制，基于带自适应拉格朗日惩罚的约束PPO，在标称、压力和未见压力流量下分别节能约41.4%、10.5%和22.9%，且保持零SLA违规与零吞吐损失，并降低模式切换振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-for-RAN的重要用例之一是节能，需要在满足用户QoS与SLA的前提下动态控制无线资源与小区能量模式。但激进的休眠或去激活决策虽能降低能耗，却可能导致吞吐下降、时延增大、SLA违规以及模式切换不稳定，尤其在时变和突发流量下更为突出。因此需要一个兼顾节能、服务保障与切换稳定性的控制框架。

Method: 将问题建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），AI原生控制器依据小区负载、队列状态、活跃用户信息、当前能量模式及SLA相关指标选择闭环节能动作。采用带自适应拉格朗日惩罚的约束近端策略优化（Constrained PPO）处理吞吐、时延和SLA约束。为应对流量分布漂移，使用标称与压力混合流量机制进行训练，并引入切换稳定性惩罚以减少活跃与低功耗模式间的振荡转换。

Result: 在七小区NG-RAN环境的仿真中，相对于Always-On基线，所提控制器在标称流量下降低能耗约41.4%，压力流量下约10.5%，未见压力流量下约22.9%。在压力和未见压力流量下，控制器保持零SLA违规和零吞吐损失，表明在挑战性条件下仍能维持服务。相比基础阈值式节能方法，所提方法还减少了切换活动。

Conclusion: 该稳定性感知约束强化学习框架能够在5G NG-RAN中实现SLA安全的节能控制，在多种流量条件下显著降低能耗，同时保障服务质量并抑制模式切换振荡，优于基础阈值式节能方案。

Abstract: One important AI-for-RAN use case is energy saving, in which radio resources and cell energy modes must be dynamically controlled without violating user quality-of-service (QoS) or service-level agreement (SLA) requirements. However, aggressive sleep-state or deactivation decisions may reduce energy consumption at the cost of throughput degradation, delay increase, SLA violations, and unstable mode switching, especially under time-varying and bursty traffic conditions. This paper proposes a stability-aware constrained reinforcement learning framework for SLA-safe energy control in 5G NG-RAN. The problem is formulated as a constrained Markov decision process in which an AI-native controller selects closed-loop energy-saving actions based on cell load, queue status, active-user information, current energy mode, and SLA-related indicators. The proposed framework uses constrained proximal policy optimization with adaptive Lagrangian penalties to account for throughput, delay, and SLA constraints. To improve operation under traffic distribution shift, the controller is trained using mixed nominal and stress traffic regimes, while a switching-stability penalty is introduced to reduce oscillatory transitions between active and low-power modes. Simulation results in a seven-cell NG-RAN environment show that the proposed controller reduces energy consumption by approximately 41.4% under nominal traffic, 10.5% under stress traffic, and 22.9% under unseen-stress traffic relative to the Always-On baseline. Under stress and unseen-stress traffic, the controller preserves zero SLA violation and zero throughput loss, indicating service-preserving operation under challenging conditions. The proposed method also reduces switching activity compared with basic threshold-based energy saving.

</details>


### [52] [Programmable Data Plane Switch based Heavy Hitter Flow Detection using Packet-Count and Packet-Size features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05998)
*Adarsha K. Sasidhar,Krishna M. Sivalingam,Gauravdeep Shami,Marc Lyonnais,Rodney Wilson*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文在可编程数据平面中利用流的中期包数（PC）特征，并辅以中期包大小（PS）特征，解决哈希冲突导致的HH漏检问题，显著降低漏检率（False Negative）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在承载大量流的数据网络中，超过预设阈值（如包数或字节数）的重流（Heavy Hitters, HHs）需被区别处理，以减少对小型流的影响。可编程数据平面交换机上实现HH检测更有效。已有工作利用包间隔（inter-packet gap）识别重流，但使用有限大小的哈希表存储流状态，当发生哈希冲突时，表中合法的HH流可能被非HH流替换，导致HH漏检（False Negative）。本文旨在解决该冲突导致的漏检问题。

Method: 引入流的中期包数（Packet Count, PC）特征；为限制哈希表中包数字段的大小，仅在发生哈希冲突前进行计数，从而缩小需存储的数值范围、减少所需比特数；同时独立引入另一个中期特征——包大小（Packet Size, PS）。方案用P4语言实现，并在Intel Tofino硬件上测试，使用CAIDA和MAWI真实流量轨迹进行性能评估。

Result: 在多个场景下，通过有效且高效地利用包数数据，能够显著降低HH检测的漏检率（False Negatives）。

Conclusion: 将流的中期包数特征（辅以中期包大小特征）融入数据平面HH检测，可缓解哈希冲突引起的HH漏检问题；该方案在P4/Tofino上可行，并经真实流量验证能显著减少漏检。

Abstract: In data networks carrying large numbers of flows, Heavy Hitters (HHs) or Elephant flows are the flows exceeding pre-determined thresholds (e.g. number of packets or bytes) in a given time window. Such HH flows need to be handled differently in order to minimize their impact on other smaller flows. HH detection techniques have been shown to be more effective when implemented in programmable data plane switches. In recent work, it was shown that the inter-packet gap can be used to identify heavy hitters. Such schemes use a limited-size hash table for storing flow state information and using this for the detection. However, when hash collisions occur, it is possible that a valid HH flow in the table can be replaced by a non-HH flow resulting in missing detection of HH flows. To address this problem, this paper incorporates a flow's medium-term Packet Count (PC) feature. In order to limit the packet count field size in the hash table, counting is done only till hash collision occurs so as to reduce the range of values to be stored and thus, the required number of bits. Also, another flow's medium-term feature, Packet Size (PS) is incorporated independently. The proposed scheme has been implemented in the P4 language and tested on Intel Tofino hardware. Performance evaluation has been performed using CAIDA and MAWI-based real-life traffic traces. The results show that in several scenarios cases, we can significantly reduce the False Negatives for HHs by using the packet count data effectively and efficiently.

</details>


### [53] [Delay and Throughput Analysis of Computation Offloading in Mobile Edge Computing: A Queueing Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06021)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Farid Ashtiani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对多依赖子任务（有根有向树）构成的移动边缘计算任务，提出乘积形式排队网络（PFQN）近似模型，推导闭式利用率与等待时间并给出递归延迟算法，进而将静态卸载设计建模为路由概率上的最小化速率加权平均延迟问题，采用可微优化求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEC 可将计算卸载至边缘服务器与云端以降低 AR/VR、实时推理、传感驱动分析等应用的端到端时延。既有研究多忽略任务内部子任务依赖结构，或缺乏可解析的排队性能刻画，因此需要面向树状依赖任务的多层 MEC 静态卸载建模与优化方法。

Method: 1) 建立乘积形式排队网络（PFQN）模型并引入近似，刻画多层 MEC 中树状任务执行的计算与通信动态；2) 基于该模型推导有效服务器利用率与等待时间的闭式表达式；3) 构造递归算法计算一般树结构任务的平均延迟；4) 将静态卸载设计表述为路由概率上速率加权平均任务延迟的最小化问题；5) 通过基于 softmax 参数化、log-sum-exp 平滑以及服务器利用率稳定性壁垒的可微优化框架求解。

Result: 数值结果表明：所提 PFQN 近似能给出准确的延迟估计；在平均任务延迟与经验最大稳定吞吐量两项指标上，延迟优化的静态策略均持续优于所比较的基线算法。

Conclusion: 面向树状依赖任务的多层 MEC 静态卸载问题，可通过 PFQN 近似获得准确的性能解析，并借助可微优化有效求解，从而在延迟与稳定吞吐量方面优于基线策略。

Abstract: Mobile edge computing (MEC) enables mobile devices to offload computation to nearby edge servers and to the cloud in order to reduce end-to-end delay for applications such as AR/VR, real-time inference, and sensor-driven analytics. In this paper, we study static computation offloading when each task consists of multiple dependent subtasks represented by a rooted directed tree. We develop a product-form queueing-network (PFQN) model with an approximation to capture the computation and communication dynamics of tree-structured task execution in a multi-tier MEC system. Based on this model, we derive closed-form expressions for effective server utilizations and waiting times, and then construct a recursive algorithm for evaluating the average delay of general tree-structured tasks. We formulate the static offloading design problem as the minimization of the rate-weighted average task delay over the routing probabilities, and solve it through a differentiable optimization framework based on softmax parameterization, log-sum-exp smoothing, and a stability barrier on server utilizations. Numerical results show that the proposed PFQN approximation provides accurate delay estimates and that the delay-optimized static policy consistently outperforms the considered baseline algorithms in terms of both average task delay and empirical maximum stable throughput.

</details>


### [54] [SESO-ISAC: Service-Aware End-to-End Sensing Orchestration for 6G ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06089)
*Youbin Jeon,Laeyoung Kim,Myungjune Youn,Sangheon Pack*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出SESO-ISAC框架，将感知服务请求映射为端到端感知配置，捕获配置间相互依赖，并根据QoS需求与执行上下文变化动态评估和重选配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G集成感知与通信（ISAC）服务不同于传统通信服务，需要覆盖数据采集、处理、结果生成与暴露的完整端到端感知操作，因此需要面向服务的端到端感知编排机制。

Method: SESO-ISAC：服务感知的端到端感知编排框架。将感知服务请求映射为感知执行上下文下的完整感知配置；不独立处理各项感知配置决策，而是捕获其在端到端感知操作中的相互依赖；依据适用的QoS需求评估候选配置；随感知执行上下文变化对已选配置进行重新评估。

Result: 通过两个代表性案例研究显示，最优感知配置取决于适用QoS维度上的整体性能，并可能在运行过程中发生变化。

Conclusion: 面向端到端感知的配置决策需整体考虑跨QoS维度的性能与配置间依赖，并支持随执行上下文变化的自适应重配置。

Abstract: Unlike conventional communication services, 6G Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) services require complete end-to-end sensing operations, from data collection to processing, result generation, and exposure. In this article, we propose SESO-ISAC, a service-aware end-to-end sensing orchestration framework that maps a sensing service request to a complete sensing configuration under the sensing execution context. Rather than treating sensing configuration decisions independently, SESO-ISAC captures their interdependence across the end-to-end sensing operation, evaluates candidate configurations according to the applicable QoS requirements, and re- evaluates the selected configuration as the sensing execution context changes. Through two representative case studies, we show that the preferred sensing configuration depends on the overall performance across the applicable QoS dimensions and can change during operation.

</details>


### [55] [Rethinking Channel Charting: A Graph Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06204)
*Yifei Jin,Yuxin Zhao,Dandan Hao,Gabor Fodor*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文从图论视角重新审视信道图（channel charting），将位置扩散目标等价为图拉普拉斯平滑泛函，并提出图神经网络（GNN）公式，用 ADP 相似图上的线性代价消息传递替代孪生网络的二次代价自相关编码，在参数减少 512 倍的情况下达到相当定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道图依赖高维信道状态信息（CSI）学习低维空间表示，但原方法基于孪生网络的全局测地线不相似度目标，计算代价高、参数冗余。作者希望从图论角度重新表述该目标，以更高效的架构实现同等定位性能，并揭示障碍物与环境语义对表示学习的影响。

Method: 1) 从图论视角将位置扩散目标等价为图拉普拉斯平滑度泛函；2) 提出 GNN 公式，用基于角度-时延剖面（ADP）相似图的图平滑目标替代孪生网络的全局测地线不相似度目标；3) 用线性代价的消息传递替代二次代价的自相关编码，且不偏离原优化目标；4) 用拉普拉斯谱分析研究障碍物与嵌入空间对谱的影响。

Result: 在参数减少 512 倍的情况下，GNN 达到与原始孪生网络相当的定位精度。谱分析显示：障碍物压缩 ADP 图谱；GNN 相对于障碍物及其他环境语义充当谱解压器，而相对于过大的 ADP 嵌入空间充当压缩器。此外，学习到的嵌入的第二特征向量编码的是视距/非视距边界，而非空间坐标。

Conclusion: 信道图可被重新表述为图拉普拉斯平滑问题，GNN 消息传递是更高效的实现方式；谱视角揭示了障碍物与环境语义如何塑造嵌入结构，且嵌入中主导非空间语义（如 LOS/NLOS 边界）而非坐标本身，对信道图表示的可解释性与设计具有启示。

Abstract: Channel charting is a self-supervised framework that learns low-dimensional spatial representations from high-dimensional channel state information. We revisit channel charting from a graph-theoretic perspective, and show that the position-diffusion objective is equivalent to a graph Laplacian smoothness functional. We propose a Graph Neural Network (GNN) formulation that replaces the Siamese network's global geodesic dissimilarity objective with a graph smoothness objective. We consider the reformulated objective to be the position diffusion objective. Without diverging from the original optimization objective, the GNN replaces the quadratic-cost self-correlation encoding with linear-cost message passing over an Angle-Delay Profile (ADP)-similarity graph, achieving comparable positioning accuracy with 512 times fewer parameters. Using Laplacian spectral analysis, we demonstrate that obstacles compress the ADP graph spectrum, while the GNN acts as a spectral decompressor against obstacles and other environmental semantics, but as a compressor against excessive ADP embedding space. Beyond this, the second eigenvector of the learned embedding encodes the line-of-sight/non-line-of-sight boundary rather than spatial coordinates.

</details>


### [56] [Task-Oriented Semantic Communication for Hazard Warning and Remote Operation in Connected Vehicle Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06242)
*S M Sabit Bananee,Shahriar Hasan,Muhammad Mahbub Alam,Nafiul Rashid*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自动网联车队协同危险响应与远程操作的一体化语义与任务导向通信框架，结合车载实例分割与单目深度估计进行任务导向危险决策，并采用SD-VAE进行语义场景压缩，通过广播式紧急语义消息（ESM）在现有V2X协议栈上传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动网联车队需要可靠传播任务相关危险信息以支持下流行驶响应及必要时的远程人工干预，但V2X通信带宽有限，难以传输大容量原始传感器数据，因此需仅交换任务相关信息。

Method: 框架融合车载实例分割与单目深度估计实现任务导向的危险决策，并利用基于Stable Diffusion变分自编码器（SD-VAE）的语义场景压缩；任务相关危险信息与紧凑语义表示通过广播式紧急语义消息（ESM）在现有V2X协议栈上联合传输。

Result: SD-VAE在任务相关重建指标上持续优于JPEG与JPEG 2000，且在低SINR条件下呈现平滑退化而非陡峭崩溃效应；7,290次仿真显示整体车间碰撞率为0.99%，碰撞主要发生于极端高速与短车距场景。

Conclusion: 结果表明该语义与任务导向通信框架在远程场景重建与车队协同危险响应方面具有可行性，能够有效支撑远程人工干预需求。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicle platoons require reliable dissemination of task-relevant hazard information to enable appropriate downstream responses, with unresolved situations additionally requiring remote human intervention. However, the limited channel bandwidth of V2X communication makes high-volume sensor data transmission challenging, motivating the exchange of only task-relevant information rather than raw sensor data. This paper proposes an integrated semantic and task-oriented communication framework for cooperative hazard response and remote operation in automated vehicle platoons. The framework combines onboard instance segmentation and monocular depth estimation for task-oriented hazard decision-making with Stable Diffusion Variational Autoencoder (SD-VAE)-based semantic scene compression. Task-relevant hazard information and the compact semantic representation are jointly disseminated through a broadcast-based Emergency Semantic Message (ESM) over the existing V2X protocol stack. Evaluation demonstrates that SD-VAE consistently outperforms JPEG and JPEG 2000 on task-relevant reconstruction metrics, exhibiting graceful degradation rather than a sharp cliff effect under low-SINR conditions, demonstrating its feasibility for remote scene reconstruction when human intervention is required. Moreover, an extensive campaign of 7,290 simulation runs shows a low overall inter-vehicle collision rate of 0.99%, with collisions occurring mainly under extreme high-speed and short-gap conditions.

</details>


### [57] [Exploring the Initial Performance of NB-IoT NTN over GEO: Measurement and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06280)
*Xiong Wang,Yumin Du,Bin Gu,Zheng Lin,Xiaoyang Li,Yi Gong,Wei Gong,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对基于GEO的NB-IoT NTN进行了首次全面深入的实测（涵盖Skylo与天通两大真实测试床，时长超六个月），揭示了接入过程的高时延与高能耗问题，以及PSM机制对流量模式的根本性重塑，并提出相应优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着NTN标准化使卫星能够直接连接海量低功耗IoT设备，3GPP IoT-NTN融合了蜂窝与卫星通信。然而，由于地面终端到GEO卫星的超长传输距离，IoT-NTN在端到端时延和能耗等系统性能维度上仍存在诸多疑虑，亟需实测数据加以验证。

Method: 搭建真实NB-IoT NTN测试床（Skylo与天通），开展为期超过六个月的首次综合且深入的GEO场景测量，覆盖接入过程、端到端时延与能耗等维度，并分析PSM机制的影响。

Result: 实测表明当前NB-IoT NTN实现仍处于初期阶段；受超长RTT影响，接入过程中存在大量时间与能耗开销；PSM作为节能机制，从根本上将NB-IoT NTN流量重塑为突发性、接入驱动的通信模式，对端到端时延与能耗产生显著影响。

Conclusion: 作者基于测量发现提出相应的时延与能耗优化方案，为近期NB-IoT NTN的落地部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: With the standardization of Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) to provide direct satellite connectivity to massive, low-power Internet of Things (IoT) devices, 3GPP IoT-NTN bridges the worlds of cellular and satellite communications. While holding great potential for global connectivity with IoT devices, there exist several concerns about the system performance of IoT-NTN over Geostationary Earth Orbit (GEO), which covers multiple dimensions such as end-to-end delay and energy consumption considering the ultra-long transmission distance from ground IoT terminals to GEO satellite. To answer these concerns, we have conducted the first comprehensive and in depth measurement for NB-IoT NTN over GEO. Based on real NB-IoT NTN testbeds including both Skylo and Tiantong, measurements covering more than six months confirm that the available implementation of NB-IoT NTN remains in the initial stage. Amplified by ultra-long Round-Trip Time (RTT) between ground IoT terminals and GEO satellite, there exists plenty of time and energy consumption during the access process. Interestingly, it also reveals that as an energy-saving mechanism, Power Saving Mode (PSM) fundamentally reshapes NB-IoT NTN traffic into a bursty and access-driven communication pattern and thus has a considerable influence on the end-to-end delay and energy consumption. Finally, we propose corresponding optimization schemes to reduce the delay and energy, which lays a good foundation for the implementation of NB-IoT NTN in the near future.

</details>


### [58] [The LQ Digital Mode Family: Protocol Architecture and Reference Specification for Weak-Signal Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06427)
*Luis Quesada*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出开源弱信号数字模式 LQ8，通过变长前缀码替代固定消息头，将结构化双向通联压缩至 4 次传输（60 s），并支持单载波恒包络下的双站同时确认，兼顾灵敏度与高速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业余无线电弱信号通联普遍采用固定 6 次传输（90 s）或 5 次（RR73，75 s）的交换流程，效率受限；同时多站堆积（pileup）场景下的通联吞吐能力不足。作者希望在不损失完整双向信息交换（网格定位、信号报告、互相确认）与 -21.0 dB 灵敏度的前提下，缩短通联时延并提升单位时间通联数。

Method: 用与物理层比特预算相匹配的变长前缀码替换固定宽度的消息类型头，将应答台的 Maidenhead 网格定位与实测信号报告压缩进单条 77 bit 回复；在单载波、恒包络、50.0 Hz 带宽的传输中同时确认最多两个应答站，且射频功率分配损失为 0.0 dB；底层沿用连续相位 8-GFSK 传输。协议族另定义 LQ16、LQ4、LQ2 三个扩展档位，分别面向极限灵敏度、VHF/UHF 竞赛与快速突发信道。

Result: LQ8 可在 4 次传输（60 s）内完成结构化双向通联，较经典 6 次传输（90 s）提速 1.50x，较 5 次 RR73 交换（75 s）提速 1.25x；多站堆积下理论峰值速率可达 160 QSOs/h，双载波运行可扩展至 320 QSOs/h，并保持 -21.0 dB SNR 灵敏度。

Conclusion: LQ8 是一种开源弱信号数字模式，通过变长前缀编码与单载波双站确认机制，在保持完整双向信息交换与既有灵敏度的条件下显著提升通联效率，并提供面向不同信道与频段需求的多个扩展档位。

Abstract: This paper introduces LQ8, an open-source weak-signal digital mode for amateur radio that completes structured two-way contacts in 4 transmissions (60 s), achieving a 1.50x speedup over canonical 6-transmission exchanges (90 s) and 1.25x over 5-transmission RR73 exchanges (75 s) while preserving complete bidirectional exchange of Maidenhead grid locators, signal reports, and mutual acknowledgments. By replacing fixed-width message-type headers with variable-length prefix codes matched to physical-layer bit budgets, LQ8 packs the answering station's grid locator and measured signal report into a single 77-bit reply. In multi-station pileup operations, LQ8 confirms up to two answering stations simultaneously within a single-carrier, constant-envelope 50.0 Hz transmission with a 0.0 dB RF power-splitting penalty, enabling a theoretical peak rate of up to 160 QSOs/h (scaling to 320 QSOs/h in dual-carrier operation) while retaining the -21.0 dB SNR sensitivity of continuous-phase 8-GFSK transport. The protocol family also defines three extended profiles (LQ16, LQ4, and LQ2) tailored for extreme sensitivity, VHF/UHF contesting, and fast-burst channels.

</details>


### [59] [Measurement-Device Placement in MDI-QKD Networks with Minimal Trusted Relays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06603)
*Tianqu Luo,Ibrahim Arslantas,Sezin Ozturk,Qiaolun Zhang,Alberto Sebastián-Lombraña,Mehdi Bolourian,Jiaheng Xiong,Francesco Musumeci,Vicente Martín,Raouf Boutaba,Massimo Tornatore*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文将MDI-QKD网络中BSM枢纽的选址问题形式化为MHD问题，证明其NP难性并建立MILP模型，通过三个城域拓扑的数值评估揭示拓扑结构与可信中继需求的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDI-QKD通过将测量委托给不可信中继（BSM枢纽）消除探测器侧信道漏洞，但信道损耗仍限制链路距离，长距离业务依赖可信中继转发密钥。随着MDI-QKD走向城域部署，如何在既有光纤基础设施上放置BSM枢纽以最小化可信中继使用，成为关键的网络规划问题。

Method: 首先证明MHD问题为NP难，然后将其形式化为混合整数线性规划（MILP）。该模型首次捕捉MDI-QKD特有的结构特征：共享BSM枢纽、用户到枢纽的双链路路由以及损耗均衡约束，同时联合决策枢纽放置、用户-枢纽分配与可信中继需求分配，并考虑现实预算与容量约束及商用MDI-QKD部署的实践洞见。

Result: 在三个12至50节点的城域拓扑（真实地理距离）上进行数值评估：拓扑结构主导可信中继需求——仅靠枢纽放置即可在紧凑城市网格中消除全部可信中继；在稀疏拓扑中，放宽损耗均衡约束可在不增加基础设施成本的情况下消除可信中继使用的主要来源。可信中继首次出现的关键速率阈值随网络直径（任意节点对间最大光纤最短路径距离）单调变化。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构决定了可信中继需求，网络直径刻画了纯MDI-QKD部署的可行性边界，为城域MDI-QKD网络规划提供了结构化建模方法与部署指导。

Abstract: Measurement-Device-Independent Quantum Key Distribution (MDI-QKD) removes detector side-channel vulnerabilities by delegating measurements to an untrusted relay that, when shared by several user pairs, acts as a Bell-state measurement (BSM) hub. Channel loss still limits the reach of MDI-QKD links, so long-range services rely on trusted relays for key forwarding. As MDI-QKD moves toward metropolitan deployment, a key network-planning question arises: how should BSM hubs be placed on existing fiber infrastructure to minimize the use of trusted relays?
  In this work, we formalize this challenge as the MDI-QKD Hub Deployment (MHD) problem. We first prove that the MHD problem is NP-hard, and then formulate it as a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first formulation for MDI-QKD network planning that captures the structural features specific to MDI-QKD, namely a shared BSM hub, two-link user-to-hub routing, and loss-balancing constraints, while jointly determining hub placement, user-to-hub assignment, and trusted-relay demand allocation. Our solution accounts for realistic budget and capacity constraints, including practical insights from real-world deployments of commercial MDI-QKD solutions. Numerical evaluations on three metropolitan topologies (12 to 50 nodes) at realistic geographic distances show that topology structure governs trusted-relay demand: hub placement alone eliminates all trusted relays in compact urban meshes, while in sparse topologies, relaxing the loss-balancing constraint removes the dominant source of trusted-relay usage at no additional infrastructure cost. The key-rate threshold at which trusted relays first appear shifts monotonically with the network diameter, i.e., the largest shortest-path distance between any node pair in fiber kilometers, delineating the feasibility boundary of pure MDI-QKD deployment.

</details>


### [60] [Enabling Emergency Communication via Semantic Radar-Centric ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06801)
*Mohaimin Al Barat,Chaoyu Zhang,Hexuan Yu,Kai Zeng,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出以雷达为中心的语义ISAC框架，用矢量量化语义编码器压缩任务相关信息，并约束相控MIMO雷达于保守空谱配置子集，在几乎不改变波束图的前提下实现应急通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作战飞机等现代军事平台同时搭载雷达与专用通信电台，二者共址。在对抗环境中，专用通信链路易被干扰、遮挡或物理损毁，而雷达通常仍可正常工作。因此希望将幸存雷达孔径复用为应急通信信道。但雷达波形为感知而设计，任何信息加载都会扰动波束图并损害感知性能，比特越多感知损失越大。核心难题是在保持雷达主感知功能的同时实现应急通信。

Method: 提出语义雷达中心ISAC框架，包含两部分：(1) 学习型矢量量化（VQ）语义编码器，将任务相关信息压缩至数十到数百比特；(2) 宽带相控MIMO雷达，其空谱配置被限制在满足有界感知退化预算的保守子集内。

Result: 在SAR目标分类与文本分类任务上，该框架支持面向下游任务的可靠应急通信，同时使雷达波束图几乎保持不变。

Conclusion: 以语义压缩降低信息载荷、以受限空谱配置约束感知退化，可在雷达为中心的前提下兼顾应急通信与主感知功能，验证了雷达孔径复用为应急信道的可行性。

Abstract: Combat aircraft and other modern military platforms field two co-located RF assets: a radar for sensing and a dedicated radio for communication. In contested battlefield environments, however, the dedicated communication path is highly likely to be targeted and disrupted by jamming, blockage, or physical damage, while the radar remains fully operational. A natural fallback is to repurpose the surviving radar aperture as an emergency communication channel. The difficulty is that radar is purposely engineered for sensing rather than communication, so loading any information onto its waveform perturbs the beampattern and inevitably erodes sensing performance: the more bits we push, the more sensing we lose. We identify the core problem as enabling emergency communication via a radar-centric integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) link while preserving the radar's primary sensing function. Therefore, we propose a semantic radar-centric ISAC framework that pairs a learned vector-quantized semantic encoder, which compresses task-relevant content into a few tens to a few hundred bits, with a wideband phased-MIMO radar restricted to a conservative spatial-spectral configuration subset that respects a bounded sensing-degradation budget. Experiments on SAR target classification and text classification show that our framework supports reliable emergency communication for downstream tasks while keeping the radar beampattern almost unchanged.

</details>


### [61] [An FPGA-in-the-Loop Testbed for MU-MIMO OFDM Beamforming over Ray-Traced Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06812)
*Drew Schlesener,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种硬件在环（HIL）测试平台，将基于FPGA的OFDM波形与MU-MIMO波束成形实现与NVIDIA Sionna光线追踪信道模拟器耦合，使真实基站架构能在数字孪生传播环境中实时收发，从而在无需暗室或射频前端的情况下对FPGA波束成形逻辑进行可重复验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络吞吐需求持续增长，用户群体异构且高密度，需要低时延、能适应信道条件的波束成形算法。验证此类算法面临两难：空口测试平台成本高昂，而纯仿真环境缺乏真实硬件的时序与资源约束，导致算法设计与硬件可实现的部署之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 构建硬件在环测试平台：以FPGA实现OFDM波形与MU-MIMO波束成形，并与NVIDIA Sionna的光线追踪信道模拟器相耦合，形成数字孪生传播环境，使物理基站架构可实时收发。文中详述FPGA的OFDM收发流水线、FPGA与Sionna环境之间的同步与数据接口设计。

Result: 在AWGN与光线追踪信道条件下对信号质量进行了验证，表明该平台能够在真实、可控且可重复的信道条件下（含UE移动性和特定站点多径）评估运行于真实FPGA逻辑上的波束成形算法，无需暗室或实时射频前端。

Conclusion: 该HIL测试平台弥合了算法设计与硬件可实现部署之间的差距，可作为一种支持硬件验证波束成形研究的平台。

Abstract: Wireless networks face ever expanding throughput demands from heterogeneous, high-density user populations, requiring beamforming algorithms that adapt to channel conditions with low latency. Validating such algorithms requires either costly over-the-air testbeds or simulation environments that lack the timing and resource constraints of real hardware, leaving a gap between algorithm design and hardware-realizable deployment. This work presents a hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testbed that closes that gap by coupling an FPGA-based implementation of OFDM Waveforms with MU-MIMO beamforming to NVIDIA Sionna's ray-tracing channel simulator, enabling a physical base-station architecture to transmit and receive against a Sionna-rendered digital-twin propagation environment in real time. Unlike prior work that validates beamforming algorithms either purely in simulation or on full RF testbeds, this architecture allows beamforming logic running on actual FPGA fabric to be evaluated under realistic, controllable, and repeatable channel conditions, including UE mobility and site-specific multi-path, without requiring an anechoic chamber or live RF front end. We detail the FPGA OFDM transmit/receive pipeline, the synchronization and data interface between the FPGA and the Sionna environment, and validation of signal quality under AWGN and ray-traced channel conditions, establishing this testbed as a platform for hardware-validated beamforming research.

</details>


### [62] [ASTRA: Low-Overhead Runtime Architecture for STReam Adaptation in Video Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07020)
*Mahshid Ghasemi,Zoran Kostic,Javad Ghaderi,Gil Zussman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ASTRA——一种面向边缘实时视频分析的在线自适应运行时架构，通过黑盒方式支持多类分辨率/帧率自适应算法，在COSMOS测试床上验证其可在极低GPU开销下达到高系统可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频分析对智慧城市与云连接车辆控制至关重要，但以最高分辨率和帧率处理所有摄像头的视频在资源受限条件下不可行，且会恶化分析时延。因此需要根据网络状况与视频内容智能调整摄像头分辨率与帧率以优化性能。

Method: 提出ASTRA，一种低开销的边缘实时摄像头分析在线自适应运行时架构，可将各类在线算法作为黑盒执行。在真实NSF COSMOS测试床上部署，利用其街道级摄像头评估实时性能；并在真实网络条件下以多达八个仿真摄像头的综合视频数据集进行评测，同时以该架构评估多类自适应算法的实际表现。

Result: ASTRA可提供超过90%的系统可靠性（即满足精度与时延要求的概率），性能与最优离线性能偏差小于10%，每摄像头平均GPU利用率开销约为2%。

Conclusion: ASTRA能够以极低的资源开销实现高可靠、接近离线最优的在线视频分析自适应，验证了其架构在真实部署场景下的实用性与通用性。

Abstract: Real-time video analytics is crucial for smart city applications and cloud-connected vehicle control. To improve analytics accuracy, it is desirable to process the video at the highest resolution and frame rate. However, due to limited resources, streaming and processing video at the highest resolution and frame rate from all cameras is not feasible and adversely affects the analytics latency. Intelligent adaptation of cameras' resolutions and frame rates based on network conditions and the video content is crucial in order to optimize the performance. In this paper, we present ASTRA, a low-overhead runtime architecture for online adaptation of live camera analytics at the edge. ASTRA can execute various online algorithms as a black box. We deployed ASTRA in the realistic NSF COSMOS testbed and uniquely assessed its real-time performance using COSMOS' street-level cameras. We further evaluated ASTRA with up to eight emulated cameras by streaming a comprehensive video dataset under real-world network conditions. We used ASTRA's architecture to evaluate the practical performance of several classes of adaptation algorithms, including theoretical and empirical methods. The results indicate that ASTRA can provide system reliability (i.e., the probability of meeting accuracy and latency requirements) of more than 90% while maintaining performance within a deviation of less than 10% from optimal offline performance with average GPU utilization overhead of around 2% per camera.

</details>


### [63] [Ollama in the Wild: A Longitudinal Measurement of Exposed Ollama LLM Endpoints at Internet Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07115)
*Zuyao Xu,Xiang Li,Yuqi Qiu,Lu Sun*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 对Ollama暴露端点开展365天纵向测量，揭示其持续增长、高度集中且升级缓慢的结构性暴露面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自托管大模型服务正成为一类新型互联网服务，但公众对其在公网上的出现方式与演化过程了解甚少，缺乏纵向、系统性的观测证据。

Method: 2025年2月至2026年2月对11434端口（Ollama）暴露端点进行365天纵向测量：每日主动探测，结合GeoIP/ASN富化、PTR与443端口主机观测，并开展生存分析；累计362个观测日、约480万条IP×日观测记录、152,137个累计IP。

Result: 26.4%的IP仅出现单日；五个选定CVE中，处于修复前版本的IP仅0.43%–2.90%原地完成升级；前五国家/地区占加权观测的70%以上；云与托管服务商主导头部ASN。旧版本、常见模型选择、云及托管ASN、PTR类别与TLS证书模式全年持续可见。

Conclusion: 暴露的Ollama构成一种结构性暴露面：持续存在、不断增长且高度集中；其不安全的部署实践在云基础设施中反复出现，意味着服务商层面的缓解措施具有潜在覆盖效力。

Abstract: Self-hosted large language model (LLM) serving is emerging as a distinct category of Internet service, but we still know little about how these deployments appear and change on the public Internet. We present a 365-day longitudinal measurement of exposed Ollama endpoints (port 11434) from February 2025 to February 2026, combining daily active probing with GeoIP/ASN enrichment, PTR and port-443 host observations, and survival analysis. Across 362 observation days and approximately 4.8 million IP$\times$day observations, 26.4% of the 152,137 cumulative IPs appear for a single day; across five selected CVEs, only 0.43-2.90% of below-fix IPs upgraded in place; the top five countries/regions account for over 70% of weighted observations; and cloud and hosting providers dominate the top ASNs. These results characterize exposed Ollama as a structural exposure surface: persistent, growing, and heavily concentrated. At the same time, old versions, common model choices, cloud and hosting ASNs, PTR categories, and TLS certificate patterns remain visible across the year, indicating recurring insecure deployment practices in cloud infrastructure and the potential reach of provider-level mitigation.

</details>


### [64] [Det-5G: Closing the Determinism Gap in 5G-Advanced for Industrial Closed-Loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07386)
*Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Det-5G——以完整双向控制周期为调度对象的统一无线资源分配框架，通过下行/上行协调分配、自适应绑定传输、组导向下行通信与优化多用户上行调度，提升工业闭环控制中周期完成的确定性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G的uRLLC能力为工业无线连接带来机遇，但蜂窝网络在闭环控制中仍难普及：闭环控制不仅要求低时延高可靠，还要求命令/反馈的周期性交互在信道变化、重传、移动性和多设备竞争下仍能在可预测的时间界限内完成，而传统调度难以满足这种确定性需求。

Method: Det-5G将完整双向控制周期视为调度对象，在5G空口上联合采用上下行协调分配、自适应绑定传输、组导向下行通信以及优化的多用户上行调度；评估方法结合闭式分析与蒙特卡洛调度实验，并与动态授权调度、半持续调度/配置授权、固定主动重复等方案对比。

Result: 评估表明Det-5G提升了周期完成的预测性，在链路条件变化下仍维持目标可靠性，在多设备控制场景下的扩展性优于传统反应式调度，并且能够自适应使用无线资源，而非像固定重复那样持续按最坏情况预留资源。

Conclusion: 面向周期的调度是缩小确定性差距的务实方案，可推动5G在不同垂直领域用于闭环控制，并随5G-Advanced向6G演进持续适用。

Abstract: The ultra-reliable low-latency communication (uRLLC) capability of 5G has created significant opportunities for industrial wireless connectivity, yet widespread use of cellular networks for closed-loop control remains challenging. Closed-loop control requires more than low packet latency and high reliability: cyclic command/feedback exchanges must complete within predictable time bounds despite changing channel conditions, recovery transmissions, mobility, and multi-device contention. This paper introduces Deterministic-5G (Det-5G), a unified radio resource allocation framework for industrial closed-loop control. Det-5G treats the complete bidirectional control cycle as the scheduling object and combines coordinated downlink/uplink allocation, adaptive bundled transmissions, group-oriented downlink communication, and optimized multi-user uplink scheduling over 5G air-interface. Its performance is evaluated through a combination of closed-form analysis and Monte Carlo scheduling experiments, with comparisons against conventional dynamic grant-based scheduling, semi-persistent scheduling/configured grant operation, and fixed proactive repetition. The evaluation shows that Det-5G improves predictability of cycle completion, maintains the target reliability under changing link conditions, and scales more effectively to multi-device control than conventional reactive scheduling, while adapting radio resource use instead of continuously provisioning for the worst case as in fixed repetition. These characteristics make cycle-oriented scheduling a pragmatic solution for reducing the determinism gap that limits the use of 5G for closed-loop control in different verticals, especially as it evolves through 5G-Advanced toward 6G.

</details>


### [65] [Blockchain-based Proportional Fair Scheduling for Multi-Operator O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07473)
*Kun Huang,Xintong Ling,Meining Wu,Jiaheng Wang,Zhi Ding,Xiqi Gao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于区块链的比例公平调度（BC-PFS），通过智能合约实现O-RAN中可信的跨运营商协调与资源池化，并从理论上刻画其吞吐量性能与池化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN的开放与解耦促进了跨网络的资源共享与协调，产生了对高效且可信的跨运营商调度的新需求。然而，传统比例公平调度（PFS）缺乏在独立运营商之间建立信任的机制，无法胜任此类调度任务。

Method: 设计BC-PFS以支持O-RAN中跨运营商的可信网络间协调与资源池化；实现四类核心智能合约（注册、状态上报、调度、结算）并给出Solidity实现，以保证链上执行的可信性；建立解析框架，分别用概率方法与常微分方程（ODE）方法推导用户平均吞吐量，并给出简化的闭式解。

Result: 基于性能评估量化了BC-PFS通过可信跨运营商协作在O-RAN中实现的池化效应，并指出该效应随运营商网络数量与用户数量单调增长；仿真验证了理论分析，并展示了BC-PFS在O-RAN中的性能表现。

Conclusion: BC-PFS可弥补传统PFS在跨运营商可信调度方面的不足，实现可信的跨运营商协调与资源池化，且其池化效应随网络和用户规模单调增强。

Abstract: The openness and disaggregation of Open radio access network (O-RAN) facilitate resource sharing and coordination across networks, creating new demands for efficient and trustworthy cross-operator scheduling. However, such scheduling is beyond the scope and capability of conventional proportional fair scheduling (PFS), which lacks mechanisms for establishing trust among independent operators. To fulfill this gap, we propose the blockchain-based proportional fair scheduling (BC-PFS) that enables trustworthy inter-network coordination and resource pooling across operators in O-RAN. Specifically, we design four core smart contracts including registration, status reporting, scheduling, and settlement contracts with corresponding Solidity implementations to ensure trustworthy on-chain execution. Theoretically, to evaluate the BC-PFS performance, we develop an analytical framework to derive the user average throughput via both probabilistic and ordinary differential equation (ODE) approaches, and provide a simplified closed-form solution. Based on the above performance assessment, we quantify the pooling effect in O-RAN achieved through trustworthy cross-operator collaboration via BC-PFS, and point out that this effect grows monotonically in both the numbers of operator networks and users. Simulations validate the theoretical analysis and show the performance of the BC-PFS in O-RAN.

</details>


### [66] [Perception-Aware Joint Power and Sub-Band Allocation for 6G In-Body Subnetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07519)
*Samira Abdelrahman,Hossam Farag*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向XR的体内子网络（IBS）感知驱动联合功率控制与子带分配框架，将用户时延感知融入无线资源分配，通过Lyapunov漂移加惩罚求解，实现最高26%发射功率降低与密集部署下约60%功率节省，同时保持QoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G中IBS为沉浸式XR提供超短距低时延连接，但多IBS密集共存导致严重同信道干扰，需提高发射功率以满足XR严苛时延要求。现有干扰管理仅依据应用层QoS分配资源，忽略人类用户的感知局限，造成资源与功率浪费。

Method: 首先结合高斯混合模型（GMM）与监督学习构建时延感知阈值的统计模型；将学习到的感知模型嵌入随机无线资源分配问题，利用Lyapunov漂移加惩罚（drift-plus-penalty）重构，并通过低复杂度的逐时隙（per-slot）资源分配流程求解，实现感知驱动的联合功率控制与子带分配。

Result: 在真实体内与IBS间传播条件下的系统级仿真表明，所提方法显著提升无线资源效率：严苛时延要求下发射功率降低最高达26%，密集IBS部署中功率节省约60%，同时维持所需体验质量（QoE）。

Conclusion: 将人类时延感知纳入IBS无线资源分配可有效缓解同信道干扰并大幅降低发射功率，在保证QoE的前提下提升资源效率，为6G XR场景的IBS干扰管理提供了感知驱动的新范式。

Abstract: In-body subnetworks (IBSs) are expected to become a key enabler of immersive eXtended Reality (XR) services in sixth-generation (6G) networks by providing ultra-short-range, low-latency wireless connectivity around the human body. However, the dense coexistence of multiple IBSs leads to severe co-channel interference, requiring increased transmit power to satisfy the stringent latency requirements of XR applications. Existing interference management approaches allocate radio resources solely according to application-level Quality-of-Service (QoS) requirements, overlooking the perceptual limitations of human users. This paper proposes perception-aware joint power control and sub-band allocation framework that integrates users' delay perception into radio resource allocation for XR-oriented IBSs. A learning-based perception model is first developed by combining Gaussian mixture modeling (GMM) with supervised learning to develop a statistical model of the delay perception threshold. The learned perception model is then incorporated into a stochastic radio resource allocation problem, which is reformulated using a Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty and solved through a low-complexity per-slot resource allocation procedure. System-level simulations under realistic intra- and inter-IBS propagation conditions demonstrate that the proposed approach substantially improves radio resource efficiency, achieving up to 26% transmit power reduction under stringent latency requirements and approximately 60% power savings in dense IBS deployments, while maintaining the required Quality of Experience (QoE).

</details>


### [67] [How Bitcoin Forms Its Network: Peer-Table Sampling and Structural Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07609)
*Taki E. M. Abedesselam,Antonio Cruciani,Fabio Giacomelli,Lucianna Kiffer,Francesco Pasquale*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过爬取比特币与狗狗币P2P网络节点数据，统计peer表中IP地址分布、估算网络规模与churn率，并据此参数化仿真比特币网络，揭示其peer表维护规则导致非均匀重尾可见性，但整体网络仍保持良好连通与结构鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代加密货币P2P网络的全局结构在设计上被隐藏：节点仅知晓邻居并维护本地peer表，全局结构对任何节点和外部观察者均不可知。然而邻居间信息交换规则与爬虫查询机制相容，使得可通过爬虫探测peer表信息。作者希望借此理解节点peer表维护规则如何决定网络的全局结构与演化，并检验其对网络连通性与鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 1. 对比特币和狗狗币P2P网络节点进行爬取，收集peer表中IP地址分布数据；2. 基于爬取数据估算网络规模演化与churn率；3. 将上述估计值作为参数，建立比特币网络仿真模型；4. 分析仿真所得网络结构的演化特征。

Result: 爬取数据显示peer表中IP地址呈非均匀分布；据此估算了网络规模与churn率的时间演化。仿真结果表明，比特币的peer表维护规则会产生非均匀、重尾的可见性模式，但网络整体仍高度连通且结构鲁棒。

Conclusion: 比特币peer表的本地维护规则虽导致可见性分布高度不均（重尾），但并未损害网络的连通性与结构鲁棒性，二者可共存。

Abstract: The global structure of P2P networks underlying modern cryptocurrencies is hidden by design: each node only knows its neighbors and maintains a local \textit{peer table} of IP addresses. The peer table of a node can be seen as the node's ``local view'' of the set of nodes currently in the network: it is constantly updated with information collected from the neighbors and it is used by the node to establish new connections when needed. The maintenance rules of the nodes' peer tables determine the global structure of the network and its evolution. Even though the global structure is unknown to any node of the network as well as to any external observer, the rules for the exchange of information between neighbors are compatible with the design and use of network \textit{crawlers} that can query the nodes and extract some information about their peer tables.
  In this paper we first analyze the data we crawled from nodes of the Bitcoin and Dogecoin P2P networks, to collect information about the distribution of IP addresses in the peer tables and to estimate the evolution of network size and churn rate; we then use the estimates to set up the parameters for a simulation of the Bitcoin network and we analyze the evolution of the network structure that we get from the simulation. Overall, our results show that Bitcoin's peer-table maintenance rules induce non-uniform, heavy-tailed visibility patterns while nevertheless generating a well-connected and structurally robust network.

</details>


### [68] [Real-Time dApps for AI-RAN: Measured Interface Requirements for Inline PHY and Slot-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07805)
*Timothy O'Shea,Matthew Pennybacker,Andriy Kharchenko*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文质疑现有公共dApp框架统一采用"外部进程+指示/控制消息"边界实现分布式应用的做法，提出以dApp与DU的耦合方式作为设计轴（进程内C ABI 与基于SCTP的E3AP关联协议两端），并据此定位三类OCUDU dApp。基于39个经5G NR时序标定的AI-RAN用例审计表明，超过半数用例无法跨观察者边界；实测显示所有载体在空闲主机上均满足100us控制时延，但仅进程内路径在DU运行时仍能满足。据此释放出ABI（inline流、bounded control验证调用）与观察侧三种机制（lease、supervised ring、portable E3 client），并在单一空口小区上联合验证了三类共四个dApp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共dApp框架都将分布式应用实现为"外部进程接收指示并返回控制消息"的同一边界形式。该边界适合感知与咨询类负载，但无法表达必须在一个时隙内完成的神经接收机，也无法承载MAC正在等待的调度决策。论文因此追问：要让当前归在AI-RAN名下的用例真正以dApp形式实现，dApp接口在时延与带宽上必须提供什么。

Method: 将dApp与DU的耦合视为一条设计轴，其两端分别为DU进程内的C ABI与基于SCTP的协议标准化E3AP关联，并将三类OCUDU dApp沿该轴定位。构建包含39个运行时AI-RAN用例的审计语料库，按5G NR时序进行规模标定，判断各用例能否跨越观察者边界。在安静主机与空口实活小区两种条件下，测量各框架实际使用的机制。在单一over-the-air小区上联合验证覆盖全部三类的四个dApp。

Result: 审计显示超过半数用例无法跨越观察者边界：inline PHY工作因指示无返回同一时隙的路径，bounded control受负载下尾延迟影响。实测表明所有载体在空闲主机上均满足100us控制时延，而DU运行后仅进程内路径仍满足该时延。异步用例在轴上任意位置均保持可行。进程内放置为异步用例带来可问责的陈旧度（accountable staleness）与无消息传递的观察到决策路径。

Conclusion: 协议标准化一端仍作为一等选项保留，面向异步用例；进程内放置作为其补充，提供可问责的陈旧度与零消息观察-决策路径。测量结果衍生出所释放的接口设计：用于inline工作的stream、用于bounded control的validated call、以及观察侧在lease、supervised ring与portable E3 client之间的选择。

Abstract: Distributed applications (dApps) bring AI to the microsecond-to-millisecond band beside the 5G distributed unit (DU), but every public dApp framework realizes them the same way: an external process that receives an indication and returns a control message. That boundary is right for sensing and advisory workloads. It cannot express a neural receiver that must finish inside a slot, and it cannot hold a scheduling decision the MAC is waiting on. This paper asks what a dApp interface must deliver, in latency and bandwidth, for the AI-RAN use cases now filed under that name to be realized as dApps at all. It treats the coupling between a dApp and the DU as a design axis with two legitimate ends, a C ABI inside the DU process and a protocol-standard E3AP association over SCTP, and places the three OCUDU dApp classes on it. An audited corpus of 39 runtime AI-RAN use cases, sized by 5G NR timing, shows that more than half cannot cross the observer boundary: inline PHY work because an indication has no return path into the same slot, and bounded control because of the tail under load. Measuring the mechanisms each framework actually uses, on a quiet host and with a live cell on the air, shows that every carrier meets a 100 us control deadline on an idle host and that only the in-process paths still do once the DU is running. The asynchronous use cases remain feasible at every position, so the protocol-standard end is kept as a first-class option; what in-process placement adds for them is accountable staleness and an observation-to-decision path with no message on it. These measurements derive the released ABI, a stream for inline work, a validated call for bounded control, and a choice of lease, supervised ring, or portable E3 client for observation, and four dApps of all three classes are validated together on one over-the-air cell.

</details>


### [69] [The OCUDU dApp Platform: An Open Runtime and E3 Interface for Real-Time AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07843)
*Timothy O'Shea,Matthew Pennybacker,Andriy Kharchenko*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OCUDU dApp平台：一个开源运行时与E3接口，使经签名的AI-RAN应用能在生产级5G DU内部按三类时序契约（Class A/B/C）执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在3GPP NR 5G DU的10 ms以下时延带（链路自适应、逐时隙调度、信道估计、接收机）收益最大，但此前无开放平台允许独立构建的软件在其中运行，既有dApp框架仅作为导出流的外部观察者。

Method: 提出OCUDU dApp平台，包含开放运行时与E3接口，定义三类时序契约：Class A驻留于GPU接收链，Class B须在调度器100 µs准入时限内完成，Class C为永不阻塞的观察者且其结果供调度器消费。传统路径不被替代，所有权限均为类型化、经校验且由运营商限定。论文阐述运行时、内嵌E3代理与三个公开仓库的协作方式，展示dApp源码、签名包与生命周期状态机，定义模块编写所依据的契约，并说明统一管理面可服务于Python脚本、运营商控制台与LLM代理。

Result: 在带接入终端的GB10 gNB上，三类dApp（含树外神经均衡器）在真实小区中并行运行且零回退（no fallback），均衡器变体仅通过生命周期操作即可空口对比。文中报告了每个测量检查点及其条件与差距。

Conclusion: 平台、SDK及零硬件快速入门以BSD-3-Clause-Clear公开，作为OCUDU AI-RAN第二工作组预览版发布，面向上游并入OCUDU主线之前征集反馈、新用例与独立验证。

Abstract: Machine learning has shown its largest gains in the band below 10 ms inside a 3GPP new radio (NR) 5G distributed unit (DU): link adaptation, per-slot scheduling, channel estimation, and the receiver itself. No open platform has let independently built software run there. Prior dApp frameworks reached the band only as external observers of an export stream. This paper is a guided introduction to the OCUDU dApp platform, an open runtime and E3 interface under which signed AI-RAN applications execute inside a production DU under three timing contracts: resident on the GPU receive chain (Class A), inside the scheduler's 100 us admitted deadline (Class B), or as never-blocking observers whose results the scheduler consumes (Class C). The conventional path is never displaced, and every authority is typed, validated, and operator-bounded. The paper explains how the runtime, the embedded E3 agent, and the three public repositories fit together; shows a dApp's source, its signed package, and its lifecycle state machine; defines the contracts a module is written against; and shows how one management surface serves a Python script, an operator's console, and an LLM agent. On a GB10 gNB with attached handsets, dApps of all three classes, including an out-of-tree neural equalizer, ran together on a live cell without a single fallback, and equalizer variants were compared over the air by lifecycle operations alone. Every measured checkpoint is reported with its conditions and its gaps. Platform, SDK, and a zero-hardware quickstart are public under BSD-3-Clause-Clear as a preview release of the OCUDU AI-RAN Working Group 2, inviting feedback, new use cases, and independent vetting ahead of upstreaming into the OCUDU mainline.

</details>


### [70] [Movable Antennas Enabled Wireless Powered Networks: Principles and Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08042)
*Zhendong Li,Yiran Zheng,Tianyu Li,Zhou Su,Wen Chen,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了可移动天线（MA）赋能的无线供能网络（WPNs），梳理MA实现方式与WPNs原理，总结其优势、典型场景与使能技术，并通过案例分析验证能量收集性能提升，最后展望挑战与方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WPNs融合无线通信与能量传输，但受信道增益与能量波束成形效率限制；MA通过引入额外空间自由度动态调整天线位置，有望充分利用信道增益、优化能量波束成形，从而提升WPNs性能，故需系统梳理该新兴框架。

Method: 采用综述与案例分析相结合的方法：首先对MA的实现方式进行分类，并回顾WPNs基本原理；随后分析MA赋能WPNs的关键优势，讨论四类代表性应用场景与三项关键使能技术；最后通过案例研究评估MA对WPNs能量收集性能的提升。

Result: MA赋能WPNs在提升无线能量传输效率、实现灵活自适应波束成形、增强系统鲁棒性与抗干扰能力方面具有显著优势；案例研究表明，引入MA可有效改善WPNs的能量收集性能。

Conclusion: MA为WPNs提供了新的性能提升途径，本文明确了其优势、应用场景与使能技术，并指出未来挑战与研究方向，可为后续研究与工程实践提供参考。

Abstract: As an emerging framework, movable antenna (MA)-enabled wireless powered networks (WPNs) have attracted growing attention. WPNs integrate wireless communication and energy transfer. MA can dynamically adjust the position of antenna units by introducing additional spatial degrees of freedom, so as to make full use of channel gain, optimize the effect of energy beamforming, and further improve the performance of WPNs. In this article, we first classify the implementations of MA, and review the fundamental principles of WPNs. We then highlight the key advantages of MA-enabled WPNs in enhancing wireless power transfer efficiency, realizing flexible and adaptive beamforming, and improving system robustness and interference resilience. Furthermore, four representative application scenarios and three key enabling technologies are discussed. A case study is also presented to show the improvement of energy harvesting performance brought by MA for WPNs. Finally, we discuss the challenges and future directions of MA-enabled WPNs, aiming to provide reference for future research and practice.

</details>


### [71] [6SEVEN: System for EValuating IPv6 ENumeration algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08098)
*Chase Kanipe,Erik Rye,Dave Levin,Robert Beverly*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出6SEVEN框架，将IPv6目标生成算法（TGA）作为插件统一托管，并共享数据清洗、探测、去别名与结果统计组件，以解决TGA评估标准不一的问题。通过移植8种主流TGA并进行受控案例研究，发现性能差异很大程度源于种子集构成、去别名和统计流程等非算法因素，并揭示了产量与探索性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPv6地址空间庞大、稀疏且常为短时存在，发现活跃地址困难，社区已开发30多种TGA。但现有文献在输入种子、数据清洗和成功度量上各不相同，导致无法直接比较，亟需统一的评估框架。

Method: 提出6SEVEN可扩展框架：以插件形式托管TGA，并将其与共享的数据清洗、探测、去别名和结果统计组件相连接。将8种流行TGA移植为插件，并开展受控案例研究。

Result: 这些TGA的性能差异显著，且很大程度上取决于与主算法无关的因素，尤其是种子集构成、去别名和统计流程；同时观察到TGA性能中产量（yield）与探索性（exploration）等理想特性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 6SEVEN有望成为社区资源，推动当前与未来TGA研究的发展，并促进IPv6测量科学的进步。

Abstract: The vast, sparsely populated, and often ephemeral IPv6 address space makes discovering active addresses challenging. In response, the community has developed over thirty different IPv6 Target Generation Algorithms (TGAs). TGAs learn addressing structure from seed (training) datasets, build a representative model, and generate candidate addresses. Unfortunately, the existing literature employs a wide variety of input seeds, data cleansing, and metrics of success that prevent ready comparison. Toward making TGA evaluation consistent, we present 6SEVEN, an extensible framework that hosts TGAs as plugins and links them to shared data cleaning, probing, dealiasing, and result tabulation components.
  We port as 6SEVEN plugins eight popular TGAs and demonstrate a controlled case study. Our results show that the performance of these TGAs varies substantially due to factors independent of the main algorithm---especially the seed set composition, dealiasing, and tabulation procedures. We observe tradeoffs between desirable features of TGA performance, including yield and exploration. We envision 6SEVEN as an enabling community resource to advance the science of current and future TGAs, and, by extension, IPv6 measurement.

</details>


### [72] [Information-Entropy-Driven Fault Propagation Modeling for Probabilistic Network Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08143)
*Lusha Mo,Fengxiao Tang,Xiaonan Wang,Ming Zhao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出信息熵驱动的故障传播范式IEFP与条件扩散模型FEMNet，实现复杂故障场景下的概率化网络性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的性能预测器多聚焦正常运行状态，或将故障引发的拓扑与路由变化视为静态输入，通常只给出确定性点估计，忽略了故障传播动态与性能演化中的不确定性；预定义规则与纯数据驱动的传播模型缺乏对故障定义、传播机制与影响量化的统一表示，且条件扩散模型中的通用去噪器未能将故障传播纳入不确定性建模。

Method: 提出信息熵驱动的故障传播范式IEFP，用相对熵、互信息与转移熵刻画故障传播；设计故障感知图消息传递机制，使传播上下文调制网络表示学习；在此基础上构建FEMNet，将该机制作为条件扩散模型中定制化的去噪器，实现复杂故障场景下的概率化网络性能预测。

Result: 与最强基线相比，IEFP提升了故障预测性能，FEMNet同时降低了点预测与概率KPI预测的误差。

Conclusion: 通过信息熵驱动的传播建模与故障感知去噪器的结合，将故障传播动态引入不确定性建模，可有效提升复杂故障场景下网络性能预测的准确性与概率刻画能力。

Abstract: Network faults can trigger cascading effects that cause abrupt and nonstationary performance degradation. Existing learning-based performance predictors mainly focus on normal operation or treat fault-induced topology and routing changes as static inputs, and typically produce deterministic point estimates. They overlook fault-propagation dynamics and uncertainty in performance evolution. The predefined-rule and purely data-driven propagation models lack a unified representation of fault definition, propagation mechanism, and impact quantification. Additionally, generic denoisers in conditional diffusion models fail to incorporate fault propagation into uncertainty modeling. To address these limitations, we propose an information-entropy-driven fault propagation paradigm (IEFP) that characterizes fault propagation via relative entropy, mutual information and transfer entropy. We then design a fault-aware graph message-passing mechanism that propagation contexts modulate network representation learning. We further develop FEMNet, which employs this mechanism as a tailored denoiser within a conditional diffusion model to enable probabilistic network performance prediction under complex fault scenarios. Compared with the strongest baselines, IEFP improves fault-prediction performance, while FEMNet reduces errors in both point and probabilistic KPI prediction.

</details>


### [73] [QoS-Aware RACH Preamble Slicing via Quota-Projected Branching Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08199)
*Jiulin Guo,Jiahan Xu,Jiashuo Zhang,Heng Yang,Yizhen Sun,Yutong Xie,Shanshan Li,Zhenyu Liu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出QP-BD3QN-RACH，用配额投影分支深度强化学习在混合2RA/4RA随机接入中自适应分配RACH前导预算，在五档负载下相对四个对比方法取得一致正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QoS感知随机接入需要在异构业务与接入流程间自适应分配有限的RACH前导预算，而混合两步骤（2RA）与四步骤（4RA）竞争型随机接入的预算分配存在组合动作空间大与预算约束严格的双重难题。

Method: 提出QP-BD3QN-RACH：四个动作分支分别对应时延敏感与时延容忍的2RA/4RA前导池；采用分支型dueling Double DQN输出各池专属乘子，再经确定性配额投影转为保持前导总预算不变的非负整数分配。每分支5个动作，以20个分支-动作输出表示625个投影前分支-动作元组。

Result: 评估涵盖五档到达负载、标称种子42下的跨方法对比、六种子敏感性分析及针对性消融。在五档负载网格上，相对四个对比方法的平均方向对齐差值均为正：成功/碰撞为5.74至8.21个百分点，回退为1.23至1.92个百分点，阻塞为0.35至0.68个百分点，成功接入时延为0.128至0.456个决策间隔。逐负载结果呈指标依赖的权衡，在中等与过载条件下尤为明显。

Conclusion: 分支动作表示与配额投影相结合，可在严格前导预算约束下高效处理混合2RA/4RA随机接入的联合分配问题；所提方法在多种负载与对比方法下总体优于基线，但性能增益随负载与指标存在权衡。

Abstract: Quality-of-service (QoS)-aware random access requires adaptive allocation of a finite random access channel (RACH) preamble budget across heterogeneous traffic and access procedures. This paper proposes QP-BD3QN-RACH, a quota-projected branching deep reinforcement learning controller for mixed two-step (2RA) and four-step (4RA) contention-based random access. Four action branches correspond to the delay-sensitive and delay-tolerant 2RA/4RA preamble pools. A branching dueling Double DQN selects pool-specific multipliers, and deterministic quota projection converts them to nonnegative integer allocations that preserve the preamble budget. With five actions per branch, the controller represents 625 pre-projection branch-action tuples using 20 branch-action outputs. Evaluation covers five arrival loads, cross-method comparison under nominal seed 42, six-seed sensitivity of QP-BD3QN-RACH, and targeted ablations. Across the five-load grid, its mean direction-aligned differences relative to four comparators are positive: 5.74 to 8.21 percentage points for success/collision, 1.23 to 1.92 percentage points for fallback, 0.35 to 0.68 percentage points for blocking, and 0.128 to 0.456 decision intervals for successful-access delay. Load-wise results exhibit metric-dependent tradeoffs, particularly under intermediate and overload conditions.

</details>


### [74] [Toward Fully Autonomous 6G Networks: AI-driven Operational Efficiency and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08426)
*David Reiss,Oriol Sallent,Miguel Catalan-Cid,Daniel Camps-Mur*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI原生RAN与NaaS生态的融合，提出基于Agentic的编排框架以支持意图策略解析，实现自主6G RAN管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络复杂度因异构服务激增而显著上升，NaaS范式在加速创新的同时对网络高效运营提出挑战；未来网络需内生AI以应对复杂性。

Method: 提出一种Agentic-based编排框架，能够解析基于意图的策略，将外部NaaS请求与内部网络管理策略进行整合。

Result: 该框架成为连接外部NaaS请求与内部网络管理策略的关键机制，支撑自主化6G RAN管理。

Conclusion: AI原生RAN与NaaS生态的融合具备可行性，Agentic编排框架是赋能自主6G RAN管理的关键路径。

Abstract: Mobile networks evolution is characterized by a substantial increase in system complexity, driven by the need to accommodate a growing number of heterogeneous services on top of the digital infrastructure. This growth in service accommodation is expected to accelerate with the adoption of the Network as a Service (NaaS) paradigm, which has emerged as a promising approach to accelerate network innovation while enabling new revenue streams for operators. Although it is fundamental to abstract network capabilities for third-party developers, it poses significant challenges in terms of efficient network operation. To address this increased complexity, future mobile networks are envisioned to be inherently Artificial Intelligence (AI)-native. In particular, the integration of AI within the Radio Access Network (RAN) becomes a key enabler for optimizing operation, energy consumption, and autonomous network control. In this context, this research explores the convergence of AI-native RAN and NaaS ecosystems to enable autonomous 6G RAN management. We propose an Agentic-based orchestration framework capable of interpreting intent-based policies. The proposed framework becomes key to integrate external NaaS requests with internal network management policies.

</details>


### [75] [AI-Native Orchestration in the 6G Continuum: Evolving Operator Platforms with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08441)
*Claudia Carballo González,Hatim Chergui,Sergio Giménez-Antón,Mohammadreza Mosahebfard,Juan Sebastián Camargo,Pouria Sayyad Khodashenas,Vasileios Theodorou,Christos Verikoukis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文面向6G云-边-IoT一体化场景，在GSMA OP架构与ETSI OOP开源参考实现基础上，提出一种智能体（Agentic）驱动的情报扩展方案，通过AI原生编排层、持久化服务上下文、基于CAMARA API的闭环控制、DeMAS监测告警系统及去中心化智能体协商协议，实现意图驱动的跨域资源优化与自治冲突消解，并以URLLC与eMBB共存用例验证其在满足严格SLA的同时提升基础设施能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络向云-边-IoT连续体演进，跨分布式计算与网络域的自治编排成为关键；未来6G服务跨越多个管理与运营域，联邦化是实现泛在、超低时延服务连续性的必要条件。GSMA OP虽提供多运营商联邦与网络能力开放的架构蓝图，ETSI SDG OOP提供开源参考实现，但现有框架受限于无状态API交互且缺乏原生智能，因而需要AI原生机制支撑意图驱动自动化与闭环管理。

Method: 提出面向GSMA OP架构的Agentic驱动情报扩展，以OOP为参考框架；构建AI原生编排层，由自治智能体管理持久化服务上下文，并通过CAMARA API实现闭环控制；在OOP栈中集成声明式监测与告警系统（DeMAS），并建立去中心化智能体协商协议，从而支持实时、意图驱动的资源优化与跨联邦域的自治冲突消解。

Result: 通过一个涉及URLLC与eMBB共存的代表性6G用例进行验证，表明基于智能体的OP框架能够自主协调严格的SLA，同时提升基础设施能效。

Conclusion: 研究结果为跨域网络即服务（NaaS）模型提供了可扩展蓝图，使标准化能力开放与6G自治需求相协调。

Abstract: As Sixth-Generation (6G) networks evolve towards a seamless Cloud-Edge-Internet of Things (IoT) continuum, autonomous orchestration across distributed compute and network domains becomes critical. Future 6G services will span multiple administrative and operator domains, making federation essential for ubiquitous, ultra-low-latency service continuity beyond individual footprints. This complexity demands AI-native mechanisms supporting intent-driven automation and closed-loop management. While the GSMA Operator Platform (OP) provides the architectural blueprint for multi-operator federation and network capability exposure, and the ETSI Software Development Group OpenOP (SDG OOP) offers a primary open-source reference implementation, current frameworks are limited by stateless API interactions and lack native intelligence. This paper proposes an Agentic-driven Intelligence extension for the GSMA OP architecture, using the OOP as the reference framework. We introduce an AI-native orchestration layer where autonomous agents manage persistent service contexts and enable closed-loop control via CAMARA APIs. By integrating a Declarative Monitoring and Alerting System (DeMAS) into the OOP stack and establishing a decentralised agent negotiation protocol, the proposed architecture enables real-time, intent-driven resource optimisation and autonomous cross-domain conflict resolution across federated domains. We validate our approach through a representative 6G use case involving Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) and enhanced Mobile Broadband (eMBB) coexistence, demonstrating that an agentic OP framework autonomously reconciles stringent Service Level Agreements (SLAs) while enhancing infrastructure energy efficiency. Our findings establish a scalable blueprint for cross-domain Network-as-a-Service (NaaS) models that align standardised exposure with 6G autonomous requirements.

</details>


### [76] [CleanCity-BinSense: An IoT-Enabled Smart Waste Management System with Configurable Real-Time Fill Monitoring and Nearest-Neighbor Route Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08527)
*Mohammad Adnan Kabir,Intifad Muhammad Sayeed*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出 CleanCity-BinSense：面向资源受限城市的低成本端到端物联网智能垃圾管理系统，通过太阳能传感节点实时监测垃圾桶填充度，并基于可配置标定参数与轻量最近邻算法实现需求驱动的收运路线规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中城市普遍采用固定时间表收运路线，导致垃圾桶溢出与燃油浪费，需要低成本、可扩展、对基础设施依赖低的实时监测与需求驱动收运方案。

Method: 系统由太阳能供电传感节点与超声波传感器组成，实时监测填充度；采用基于 FULL_DISTANCE 与 EMPTY_DISTANCE 两个标定参数的可配置感知模型，并以免修改固件的方式适配不同尺寸与几何形状的桶体；填充百分比通过几何可配置的线性归一化算法计算，并经硬件实验验证（MAE 0.38 cm，处于厂商容差内）；数据经 Wi-Fi 上传至集中式 Web 平台，提供面向管理员、操作员与司机的角色化仪表盘及公开实时桶状态地图；路线规划采用基于 SQL Server 空间函数的轻量最近邻算法生成邻近性收运路线，计算开销低。

Result: 硬件实验填充度测量平均绝对误差 MAE 为 0.38 cm，在传感器厂商容差范围内；系统端到端平均时延 5.3 秒，主要受感测间隔主导而非网络开销；典型城市收运区域路线生成耗时低于 100 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该低成本、可配置、低基础设施依赖的智能垃圾管理系统在达卡等资源受限环境的异构城市垃圾网络中具有可行性。

Abstract: CleanCity-BinSense addresses inefficiencies in urban waste management in developing cities, where fixed-schedule collection routes lead to overflowing bins and wasted fuel. This paper presents CleanCity-BinSense, a low-cost, end-to-end IoT-enabled smart waste management system for scalable real-time waste monitoring and demand-driven collection. The system integrates a solar-powered sensor node with an ultrasonic sensor for real-time bin fill-level monitoring. A key contribution is a configurable sensing model based on two calibration parameters, FULL_DISTANCE and EMPTY_DISTANCE, enabling deployment across bins of varying sizes and geometries without firmware modification. Fill percentage is computed using a geometry-configurable linear normalization algorithm, validated through hardware experiments with a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.38 cm, within the manufacturer-specified sensor tolerance. Sensor readings are transmitted via Wi-Fi to a centralized web platform providing role-based dashboards for administrators, operators, and drivers, along with a public real-time bin-status map. The system also incorporates a lightweight nearest-neighbor route planning algorithm using SQL Server's spatial function to generate proximity-based collection routes with low computational overhead. Experimental evaluation shows an average end-to-end system latency of 5.3 seconds, dominated by the sensing interval rather than network overhead, while route generation for typical urban collection zones completes in under 100 ms. These results demonstrate the feasibility of a low-cost, configurable, infrastructure-light smart waste management system for heterogeneous urban waste networks in resource-constrained environments such as Dhaka, Bangladesh.

</details>

### [77] [Recursive overlap Bernoulli distributions and an entropy concavity conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05546)
*Jörg Neunhäuserer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一类由带重叠的左右嵌入递归生成的有限概率分布族，它介于经典二项分布与非重叠伯努利乘积分布之间，并给出了期望、方差及高阶矩生成函数的显式公式，同时提出并部分验证了关于Shannon熵凹性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二项分布与非重叠伯努利乘积分布是两类重要的离散概率分布，但二者之间缺乏一个统一的连续过渡框架；作者希望借助带重叠的左右嵌入构造，填补这一空白并刻画其矩与熵性质。

Method: 利用带重叠的左右嵌入（left and right embeddings with overlaps）递归生成一族有限概率分布；推导期望、方差和高阶矩生成函数的显式表达式；通过解析证明与符号计算相结合的方式研究Shannon熵的凹性。

Result: 构造出的分布族在经典二项分布与非重叠伯努利乘积分布之间实现插值；获得期望、方差及高阶矩生成函数的显式公式；证明熵凹性猜想在两个极端情形下成立，并通过大量重叠序列的符号计算得到支持。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了一个统一插值二项分布与伯努利乘积分布的概率分布框架，其矩性质有显式刻画，而Shannon熵的凹性猜想在两端情形成立并有数值证据支持，为后续一般性证明留下开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce a family of recursively generated finite probability distributions obtained from left and right embeddings with overlaps. The construction interpolates between the classical binomial distribution and the non-overlapping Bernoulli product distribution. We derive explicit formulas for the expectation, variance, and the generating function of higher moments, and formulate a conjecture asserting that the Shannon entropy is concave. The conjecture is proved in the two extremal cases and supported by symbolic computations for numerous overlap sequences.

</details>


### [78] [A Nuclear-Norm Lower Bound for Dithered Scalar Quantization of Matrix Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05641)
*Piyush Sao,Narasinga Miniskar,Pedro Valero-Lara,Keita Teranishi,Sudip Seal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究量化矩阵乘法 C=AB 的误差最小化问题，通过保持乘积不变的内基变换与外正交旋转优化因子范围与量化步长，在独立零均值抖动噪声下证明了误差下界并给出达到该界的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量量化因子的舍入误差规模取决于其行列的最大绝对值（范围），而范围决定量化网格步长。作者希望在保持乘积 C 不变的前提下，通过变换改变因子范围与网格步长以降低量化误差，并刻画全规范（full-gauge）最优性及保持行列索引的代价。

Method: 在无界格上的独立零均值可减抖动噪声下，优化保持乘积的变换：内可逆基变换与外正交旋转。推导输出端主导期望平方误差下界，并构造 SVD 对齐的 Hadamard 方案与 SVD 对齐的 DCT 方案；无外旋转时用 Gram 矩阵平衡最小化分解能量，用有限集展平逼近界；对 2 的幂次 K 用条件期望确定 Hadamard 符号。

Result: 证明下界 E_lead ≥ (c_A+c_B)/K · ||AB||_*^2（K 为内维，c_A,c_B 为归一化噪声方差，||·||_* 为核范数）。该界是紧的：当存在 K 阶 Hadamard 矩阵（含所有 2 的幂）时，SVD 对齐 Hadamard 构造达到下确界；SVD 对齐 DCT 构造对所有 K 在二倍因子内。无外旋转时 Gram 矩阵平衡最小化分解能量，有限集展平在 Clog(K(m+n)) 内达到界。对 2 的幂次 K，条件期望在 O((m+n)K^2) 精确实数运算内确定 Hadamard 符号。合成实验验证两种构造并展示正则化与条件数之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了全规范最优解，并量化了保持行、列索引所付出的代价；Hadamard 与 DCT 构造分别实现紧界与近似紧界，Gram 平衡与有限集展平在受限情形下给出有效方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing error in quantized matrix multiplication $C=AB$. Scalar quantization of the factors introduces rounding errors whose scale depends on the maximum absolute entries -- the ranges -- of their rows and columns. These ranges determine the quantization grid steps. To reduce the error, we optimize over product-preserving transformations that alter the factor ranges and grid steps without changing $C$. Specifically, we seek the smallest leading expected squared error over invertible inner changes of basis and orthogonal outer rotations. Under independent, zero-mean subtractive dither noise on an unbounded lattice, we prove the output-only bound $E_{\rm lead} \ge (c_A+c_B)/K \Vert AB\Vert_*^2$, where $K$ is the inner dimension, $c_A$ and $c_B$ are normalized noise variances, and $\Vert AB\Vert_*$ is the nuclear norm. The bound is tight: an SVD-aligned Hadamard construction attains the infimum whenever a Hadamard matrix of order $K$ exists, including every power of two, while an SVD-aligned DCT construction is within a factor of two for every $K$. Without outer rotations, Gram-matrix balancing minimizes factorization energy, and finite-set flattening achieves the bound within $C\log(K(m+n))$. For power-of-two $K$, conditional expectations deterministically select the Hadamard signs in $O((m+n)K^2)$ exact-real operations. Synthetic experiments verify both constructions and illustrate the tradeoff between regularization and conditioning. These results characterize the full-gauge optimum and quantify the cost of preserving row and column indices.

</details>


### [79] [Moments of crosscorrelation demerit factors of binary sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05771)
*Daniel J. Katz,Harmony M. Vargas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究二元序列对的非周期互相关劣因子（demerit factor）在均匀分布下的统计分布，推导其各阶中心矩的精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低非周期互相关的序列族可用于多用户异步通信系统与MIMO雷达设计，因此需要理解随机二元序列对的互相关劣因子分布。

Method: 对所有长度ℓ的2^{2ℓ}对二元序列赋予均匀概率测度，建立求解任意正整数p阶中心矩关于ℓ的精确公式的方法；手动计算方差与三阶中心矩，用计算机辅助计算四至六阶中心矩。

Result: 获得p=2至6阶中心矩的精确公式，并证明当p≥2且ℓ≥3时所有中心矩严格为正；同时验证了Sarwate关于均值为1的结论。

Conclusion: 该理论方法系统刻画了二元序列对互相关劣因子的矩分布，为相关序列设计提供了统计依据。

Abstract: Families of sequences with low mutual aperiodic crosscorrelation assist the design of systems for multi-user asynchronous communications and multiple-input multiple-output radar. The crosscorrelation demerit factor of a pair of sequences is the sum of the squared magnitudes of their crosscorrelation values at every shift when the sequences are normalized to unit Euclidean norm, and the merit factor is the reciprocal of the demerit factor. For each positive integer $\ell$, we endow the $2^{2 \ell}$ pairs of binary sequences of length $\ell$ with uniform probability measure and study the distribution of their crosscorrelation demerit factors. Sarwate showed that the mean value is always $1$ regardless of length $\ell$. We develop a method for finding an exact formula for the $p$th central moment (for any positive integer $p$) as a function of $\ell$. Formulae for the variance and third central moment ($p=2$ and $3$) are then obtained by hand calculations, while the fourth through sixth central moments are obtained by computer-assisted calculations. Our theory also shows that all the central moments must be strictly positive for $p\geq 2$ and $\ell \geq 3$.

</details>
