### [80] [Algorithmic List Decoding at Capacity and Optimal Proximity Gaps for Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05870)
*Fernando Granha Jeronimo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为素数域上、任意给定评估集的普通 Reed–Solomon 码建立了统一的 hidden-derivative 框架，同时实现列表解码与相互相关一致性（MCA），并得到与域大小 q 无关的列表上界与精确支撑 MCA 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多针对特定评估集或依赖域大小，缺乏对素数域上任意评估集的统一处理；同时列表解码的列表规模与 MCA 误差往往随 q 增长。本文希望去除对 q 的依赖并统一列表解码与 MCA 的分析。

Method: 基于 Brakensiek–Chen–Putterman–Zhang–Zheng 的 hidden-derivative 构造，在插值阶段进行重新参数化与优化；使用 Kopparty 算法做微分根枚举；并证明一个 specialization-safe 微分方程可被常维数簇覆盖，其累积次数为多项式级，且仅在多项式多个参数值处失效。将这些簇与完整一致支撑对应的方程相交，得到结论。

Result: 对任意固定松弛 γ>0、充分大的码长 n、素数 q≥n、维数 1≤k≤(1−γ)n，确定性算法在 q^{O_γ(1)} 时间内找出相对距离 1−k/n−γ 内的全部码字，最终列表规模为 n^{O_γ(1)}，与 q 无关；结论可推广到有界输入列表恢复（常数额外依赖输入列表界）。对固定曲线次数 ℓ，曲线上至多 n^{O_{γ,ℓ}(1)} 个参数存在近邻码字，其精确一致支撑不是系数词的最大联合解释支撑；对直线情形给出 MCA 误差 n^{O_γ(1)}/q，且无 proximity loss。

Conclusion: 该 hidden-derivative 统一框架在素数域、任意评估集下同时给出域大小无关的列表解码上界与精确支撑 MCA 界，将列表解码与 MCA 的证明技术统一起来。

Abstract: We give a unified hidden-derivative framework for list decoding and mutual correlated agreement of ordinary Reed--Solomon codes over prime fields, on arbitrary prescribed evaluation sets. For every fixed slack $γ>0$, every sufficiently large block length $n$, every prime $q\ge n$, and every dimension $1\le k\le(1-γ)n$, a deterministic algorithm finds all codewords within relative distance $1-k/n-γ$ in $q^{O_γ(1)}$ time. The final list has size $n^{O_γ(1)}$, independently of $q$. Both statements extend to bounded-input-list recovery, with constants depending additionally on the input-list bound.
  For every fixed curve degree $\ell$, at most $n^{O_{γ,\ell}(1)}$ parameters on a curve $f_0+zf_1+\cdots+z^\ell f_\ell$ admit a nearby codeword whose exact agreement support is not a maximal jointly explained support of the coefficient words. For lines this gives MCA error $n^{O_γ(1)}/q$, with no proximity loss.
  The interpolation stage reparameterizes and optimizes the hidden-derivative construction of Brakensiek, Chen, Putterman, Zhang, and Zheng; differential root enumeration uses Kopparty's algorithm. We then prove that a specialization-safe differential equation has a cover by constant-dimensional varieties of polynomial cumulative degree, outside polynomially many parameter values. Intersecting these varieties with equations from the full agreement support yields both the field-size-independent list bound and exact-support MCA.

</details>


### [81] [Cadence: Error-Bounded Lossy Compression of Demand Time Series with a Time-Series Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06008)
*Roberto Tacconelli*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: Cadence：结合330M参数时序基础模型（TimesFM-3）与自适应算术编码的误差有界有损压缩器，保证逐样本误差不超过τ，在多个真实语料上显著优于经典预测器与下采样，并报告了多项负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向数值时间序列的压缩需求，探索时序基础模型能否替代经典线性预测器以提升压缩率。作者首先指出一个负面约束：无损编码下基础模型的收益仅为对数级，$1.51\times$的预测精度优势只换来0.60 bit（20.28 bit中）与+0.03%中位增益，因此设计空间被限制在误差有界有损编码这一唯一能逃逸该瓶颈的路径。

Method: 将Google TimesFM-3与自适应算术编码器配对，采用上下文建模的二值化自适应区间编码器；一旦预测落入误差带τ，残差索引为零，样本近乎零成本。同时处理工程确定性问题：预测在不同批大小下非位一致，且无任何PyTorch配置可修复，故将组大小与执行设备写入容器格式。评测在49条EIA-930负荷序列、50条MTA客流序列（2026）上进行，并以SDRBench做域外证伪测试。

Result: 自适应区间编码器在真实索引上比xz/zstd优9.7%（15/15）；EIA-930上较六种经典预测器最优者提升13.3%；MTA上提升28.3%，297个序列-容差对中位21.4%且全部获胜；等尺寸下最坏情况误差比下采样紧28–56倍；端到端在支付上下文引导开销后，从六个月小时级数据的6.8%提升至渐近15.1%。SDRBench上符合理论预期失败：中位-0.8%，27对中0获胜。

Conclusion: 误差有界有损编码是基础模型在时序压缩中发挥作用的唯一有效切入点，Cadence在真实领域语料上全面领先，但对域外数据（SDRBench）失效，验证了领域定位与理论预测；另有三项负面结果与八项撤回主张被完整报告。

Abstract: We present Cadence, an error-bounded lossy compressor for numeric time series pairing a 330M-parameter time-series foundation model (Google TimesFM-3) with an adaptive arithmetic coder, guaranteeing $|\hat{x}_t-x_t|\leτ$ on every sample. One negative result constrains the design space: for lossless coding a foundation model is worth nothing, because bits saved are logarithmic in predictor accuracy, $Δb=\log_2(\mathrm{MAE_{old}}/\mathrm{MAE_{new}})$. So the $1.51\times$ advantage TimesFM-3 holds over a 32-tap linear predictor buys 0.60 bits of 20.28, a median gain of +0.03%. Error-bounded coding escapes this at one point: once a forecast lands inside the band the residual index is zero and the sample nearly free. Cadence contributes: (1) an adaptive range coder with context-modelled binarization, beating xz/zstd on real indices by 9.7% (15/15) and reversing a finding from a general-purpose back end; (2) a determinism result -- predictions are not bit-identical across batch sizes, and no PyTorch configuration repairs this, forcing group size and execution device into the container format; and (3) domain localization on corpora postdating any plausible training cutoff.
  On 49 EIA-930 balancing-authority demand series (2026) Cadence gains 13.3% over the best of six classical predictors, and 28.3% on 50 MTA ridership series (2026): 21.4% median over 297 series-tolerance pairs, winning all 297. Against downsampling, what time-series databases deploy for retention, its guaranteed worst-case error is $28$--$56\times$ tighter at equal size. End-to-end, once the context bootstrap is paid for, gains run from 6.8% at six months of hourly data to 15.1% asymptotically. Attempting to falsify the domain claim on SDRBench, theory predicts failure and delivers: -0.8% median, 0 of 27 pairs gaining. Three further negative results and eight retracted claims are reported in full.

</details>


### [82] [Fundamental Limits of Adaptive Beamforming Under Finite Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06136)
*Zhiyong Cheng,Shengyao Chen,Di Song*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将自适应波束形成器因有限训练导致的SINR损失刻画为一个不可避免的极小极大下界问题，给出其上的一阶系数表达，并确定其在两类复高斯模型中的取值：点源场景下为C_θ≤K，无结构协方差下为N-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限训练样本会降低自适应波束形成器的输出信干噪比（SINR），但其中多少损失是原理上不可避免的尚不清楚。作者希望在波束形成中建立类似谱估计中Cramér–Rao界的最优性基准，从而界定这一损失的根本极限。

Method: 首先给出SINR损失关于clairvoyant MVDR权重误差的精确恒等式，并将其表示为有界函数；基于此在所有可测、数据依赖的波束形成规则（含偏差与不规则规则）上建立局部渐近极小极大下界，一阶系数为tr(M J_eff^{-1})，其中J_eff描述训练数据信息，M度量输出SINR敏感性；随后通过匹配构造在两类复高斯模型中确定该系数：N阵元ULA含K个点干扰（2K+1≤N）场景，以及无结构协方差矩阵场景（SMI）。

Result: 在N阵元均匀线阵含K个不同点干扰且2K+1≤N时，数据驱动的split one-step波束形成器在固定紧致规则参数集的所有内点场景达到系数C_θ≤K；在无约束协方差矩阵下，样本矩阵求逆（SMI）依经典Reed–Mallett–Brennan定律达到系数N-1。二者之差量化了有限源结构的一阶价值。文中还给出依赖干扰功率与阵列几何的几何公式、数值结果，以及弱源边界附近的有限样本偏离。

Conclusion: 有限训练造成的SINR损失存在可精确刻画的极小极大极限，其最低一阶系数由tr(M J_eff^{-1})给出；点源结构可显著降低该系数（≤K），而无结构协方差下达到N-1。该结果可作为波束形成的Cramér–Rao型基准，并揭示了源结构与几何对最优性能的影响。

Abstract: Finite training reduces the output signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) of an adaptive beamformer, and a natural question is how much of this loss is unavoidable. This paper determines this question, providing a beamforming counterpart of the Cramér--Rao bound in spectral estimation. An exact identity expresses the SINR loss as a bounded function of the error in the clairvoyant minimum-variance distortionless-response (MVDR) weight. It yields a local asymptotic minimax lower bound over all measurable data-dependent beamforming rules, including biased and irregular rules. The first-order coefficient is $\tr(\Mb\Jb_{\rm eff}^{-1})$, where $\Jb_{\rm eff}$ describes the information in the training data and $\Mb$ measures the sensitivity of the output SINR. Matching constructions determine this coefficient in two complex-Gaussian models. For an $N$-sensor uniform linear array with $K$ distinct point interferers and $2K+1\le N$, a data-driven split one-step beamformer attains the coefficient $C_θ\le K$ at every interior scene of a fixed compact regular parameter set. For unrestricted covariance matrices, sample matrix inversion (SMI) attains the coefficient $N-1$ through the classical Reed--Mallett--Brennan law. The difference quantifies the first-order value of finite-source structure. Geometric formulas and numerical results describe the dependence on interference power and array geometry, and the finite-sample departure near a weak-source boundary.

</details>


### [83] [Rank and classification of $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06244)
*Dipak K. Bhunia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文确定了所有容许三元组 $(t_1,t_2,t_3)$ 对应的 $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性 Hadamard 码 $H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$ 的秩，给出显式闭式公式，并结合已知的核维数对该码族进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-可加码是 $\mathbb{Z}_2^{α_1}\times\mathbb{Z}_4^{α_2}\times\mathbb{Z}_8^{α_3}$ 的子群，其 Gray 映射像给出 $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性 Hadamard 码。此前已知该类码的递归构造、线性性及核（kernel），但对应码 $H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$ 的秩（rank）没有闭式表达，因而无法仅用秩与核维数对码族进行完整分类。

Method: 对每个容许三元组确定 $rank(H^{t_1,t_2,t_3})$ 并导出显式闭式公式。证明思路是：将码的二进制坐标函数视为消息二进制位的布尔函数，计算这些坐标函数张成空间的维数。随后利用秩公式与已知核维数，以这两个不变量对码族进行分类，并考察与 $\mathbb{Z}_4$、$\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$、$\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性 Hadamard 码的重合情形。

Result: 得到 $rank(H^{t_1,t_2,t_3})$ 的闭式公式。同长度且同时共享秩与核维数这两个不变量的不同类型对仅为 $\bigl(H^{1,2,t-6},H^{2,0,t-5}\bigr)$（$t\geq7$）与 $\bigl(H^{2,2,t-9},H^{3,0,t-8}\bigr)$（$t\geq10$）。长度为 $2^t$ 的码中不同 $(r,k)$ 对的数量为：$\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor$（$3\leq t\leq6$），$\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor-1$（$7\leq t\leq9$），$\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor-2$（$t\geq10$）。与 $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$-、$\mathbb{Z}_4$-线性码恰好有一个无限族重合，即 $H^{2,1,t-7}$ 与 $H^{5,t-9}$（$t\geq10$）；与 $\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性码恰好有五个族重合。

Conclusion: 秩与核维数这两个不变量能够完全分类该码族当且仅当 $3\leq t\leq6$；当 $t\geq7$ 时存在（且仅有）上述少量无法区分的类型对，因而仅凭秩与核维数不能完全区分。

Abstract: The $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-additive codes are subgroups of $\mathbb{Z}_2^{α_1}\times\mathbb{Z}_4^{α_2}\times\mathbb{Z}_8^{α_3}$. A $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard code is a Hadamard code which is the Gray map image of such a code. A recursive construction of $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-additive Hadamard codes $\mathcal H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$, with all $α_i\neq0$, $t_1\geq1$, $t_2\geq0$, and $t_3\geq1$, is known, as are the linearity and kernel of the corresponding codes $H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$ of length $2^t$. Their rank, however, was not known in closed form.
  In this paper, we determine $rank(H^{t_1,t_2,t_3})$ for every admissible triple and obtain an explicit closed formula. The proof computes the dimension of the space spanned by the binary coordinate functions of the code, viewed as Boolean functions of the binary digits of the message. We then use the rank formula, together with the known kernel dimension, to classify the family as far as these two invariants allow. The only pairs of distinct types of the same length sharing both invariants are $\bigl(H^{1,2,t-6},H^{2,0,t-5}\bigr)$ for $t\geq7$ and $\bigl(H^{2,2,t-9},H^{3,0,t-8}\bigr)$ for $t\geq10$. Consequently, the number of distinct pairs $(r,k)$ among the codes of length $2^t$ is $\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor$ for $3\leq t\leq6$, $\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor-1$ for $7\leq t\leq9$, and $\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor-2$ for $t\geq10$. Thus, rank and kernel dimension classify the family completely if and only if $3\leq t\leq6$. We also determine all coincidences with the $\mathbb{Z}_4$-, $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4$-, and $\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard codes of the same length: there is exactly one infinite family with the first two, namely $H^{2,1,t-7}$ and $H^{5,t-9}$ for $t\geq10$, and exactly five families with the $\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear codes.

</details>


### [84] [Scaling Bayesian Bandit Encoding with Shared Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06293)
*Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将贝叶斯赌博机编码器（BBE）的选择组件扩展到1008种码与GRAND译码器配置，通过离线学习共享性能模式并在线更新权重进行配置选择，在训练选出的六配置短名单上比独立学习降低33.5%累积效用损失，且剪枝共享学习器在信道变化后更快达到近优选择标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信系统需随信道条件变化选择差错保护与译码努力程度。BBE利用接收端反馈学习传输配置选择，而GRAND译码通过猜测错误模式工作。作者希望将BBE的选择组件扩展到大量码与译码器配置，并解决大规模配置下的学习效率问题。

Method: 将BBE选择组件扩展到1008种码与译码器配置；离线学习配置间的共享性能模式，在线更新其权重；译码器噪声模型保持固定；采用训练选出的短名单并进行剪枝，与独立学习（相同折扣）进行对比。

Result: 在六配置训练短名单上，共享学习相对独立学习将累积效用损失降低33.5%；一个固定的训练选出配置可匹敌搜索完整目录的共享学习器；信道变化后，剪枝共享学习器在88.5%的事件中于2000个包内首次满足近优选择标准，而相同折扣下剪枝独立学习仅为54.2%。

Conclusion: 结果支持将共享与剪枝结合用于配置选择，但所测码在严重噪声下丢包率仍然较高。

Abstract: A communication system must choose error protection and decoding effort as channel conditions change. A Bayesian bandit encoder (BBE) uses receiver feedback to learn which transmission configuration to select. We study a receiver that decodes by guessing error patterns, using Guessing Random Additive Noise Decoding (GRAND). We extend BBE's selection component to 1,008 code and decoder configurations by learning shared performance patterns offline and updating their weights online. Decoder noise models remain fixed. On a six-configuration training-selected shortlist, sharing reduces accumulated utility loss by 33.5% relative to independent learning. A fixed training-selected configuration matches the shared learner that searches the full catalog. After channel changes, the pruned shared learner first meets a near-optimal selection criterion in 88.5% of events by 2,000 packets, compared with 54.2% for pruned independent learning with the same discounting. The results support combining sharing and pruning for configuration selection, although packet losses remain high for the tested codes under severe noise.

</details>


### [85] [A Graph Foundation Model for Large-Scale MIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06361)
*Xingyu Zhou,Le Liang,Hao Ye,Jing Zhang,Chao-Kai Wen,Xiao Li,Shi Jin,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向大规模MIMO检测的无线原生图基础模型（GFM），通过物理信息混合架构融合消息传递网络与图Transformer，并结合预训练、参数高效微调与专家混合机制，在精度、配置通用性和跨场景迁移上优于经典与数据驱动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO检测是现代无线网络的基础，但受限于性能-复杂度权衡。现有检测器（无论经典方法还是学习型方法）往往在可扩展性或跨异构场景的泛化能力上存在不足。作者希望突破这些限制，构建可扩展、可泛化的通用检测方案。

Method: 提出无线原生图基础模型（GFM）。其采用物理信息混合架构：将消息传递神经网络（MPNN）的局部相关性提取能力与图Transformer的全局注意力相结合，并编码来自期望传播（EP）算法的物理干扰模式。通过大规模预训练学习可在天线维度与信道条件间扩展的通用检测映射；下游采用参数高效微调（PEFT）快速适配特定非理想系统场景；推理阶段嵌入专家混合（MoE）机制，动态激活必要子模块以提升效率。

Result: 评估表明，该GFM在多种具有挑战性的零样本与少样本条件下，在检测精度、配置通用性和跨场景迁移能力方面，持续优于经典检测器和先进的数据驱动基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将图基础模型范式引入大规模MIMO检测，借助物理先验与图学习的融合、预训练-微调流程及动态专家路由，实现了兼具可扩展性、泛化性和推理效率的检测器，验证了无线原生基础模型在多场景部署中的潜力。

Abstract: Large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) detection is fundamental to modern wireless networks but constrained by performance-complexity trade-offs. Existing detectors, whether classical or learning-based, often fall short in either scalability or generalizability across heterogeneous scenarios. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a wireless-native graph foundation model (GFM) tailored for large-scale MIMO detection. The proposed GFM employs a physics-informed hybrid architecture, integrating the local correlation extraction of message passing neural networks with the global attention of graph Transformers, encoding the physical interference patterns from the expectation propagation algorithm. Via extensive pre-training, this synergy enables the learning of a general-purpose detection mapping scalable across antenna dimensions and channel conditions. For rapid downstream deployment, parameter-efficient fine-tuning is leveraged to adapt the GFM to specific non-ideal system regimes with minimal overhead. To enhance inference efficiency, a mixture-of-experts mechanism is embedded at downstream deployment to dynamically activate only the necessary sub-modules. Evaluations show that the proposed GFM consistently outperforms classical detectors and advanced data-driven baselines in accuracy, configuration generality, and cross-scenario transferability across various challenging zero-shot and few-shot conditions.

</details>


### [86] [Optimal-Access Cooperative MSR Codes: Parity-Check Matrix Construction And a Unified Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06372)
*Yaqian Zhang,Jingke Xu,Ya-Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对最优访问协作MSR码，提出直接显式构造与通用变换框架两种方法，将子包化水平由δ^{C(n,h)}降至δ^m，显著减少子包化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作MSR码可在保持MDS最小存储的同时实现任意h≥2个节点擦除的最优带宽协作修复，但已有最优访问协作MSR码的子包化水平过高（δ^{C(n,h)}），带来较大的实现与IO开销，因此需要降低子包化水平。

Method: 1）直接显式构造：通过设计校验矩阵，反复使用两个关键校验矩阵作为构建块来构造最优访问协作MSR码；2）通用变换框架：从任意[n+d-k,d] MDS标量码出发，系统性地施加两种基本变换导出最终的协作MSR码。

Result: 两种方法均得到(n,k,ℓ=δ^m)最优访问协作MSR码，其中δ=d-k+h，m=C(n,h)-⌊n/δ⌋(C(δ,h)-1)。相比现有技术水平（ℓ=δ^{C(n,h)}），子包化ℓ减少了1/δ^{⌊n/δ⌋(C(δ,h)-1)}的比例。此外，先前部分最优访问协作MSR码结构及h=1的最优访问MSR码可作为该变换构造的特例；所有构造均建立在大小≥n+d-k的有限域上。

Conclusion: 本文通过直接构造与通用变换框架有效降低了最优访问协作MSR码的子包化水平，且统一包含了若干已有码结构为特例，构造基于线性大小有限域，具有较好的实用性与理论统一性。

Abstract: Cooperative MSR codes are a kind of storage codes which enable optimal-bandwidth repair of any $h\geq2$ node erasures in a cooperative way, while retaining the minimum storage as an $[n,k]$ MDS code. Each code coordinate (node) is assumed to store an array of $\ell$ symbols, where $\ell$ is termed as sub-packetization. To address the disk IO (input/output) capability, a cooperative MSR code is said to have optimal-access property, if during node repair, the amount of data accessed at each helper node meets a lower bound on this quantity.
  In this paper, we focus on reducing the sub-packetization level of optimal-access cooperative MSR codes. We propose new constructions of optimal-access cooperative MSR codes through two methods. At first, we propose a direct explicit construction by designing its parity-check matrix. Such parity-check matrix is built by repeatedly employing two crucial parity-check matrices as building blocks. Secondly, we propose a generic transformation framework. Starting from an arbitrary $[n+d-k,d]$ MDS scalar code, one can derive a final cooperative MSR code by systematically applying two basic transformations. Both approaches yield $(n,k,\ell=δ^m)$ optimal-access cooperative MSR codes with $δ=d-k+h$ and $m=\binom{n}{h}-\lfloor\frac{n}δ\rfloor(\binomδ{h}-1)$. Compared with the state of the art (with $\ell=δ^{\binom{n}{h}}$), the derived codes can reduce the sub-packetization $\ell$ by a fraction of $1/δ^{\lfloor\frac{n}δ\rfloor(\binomδ{h}-1)}$, where $δ=d-k+h$. Moreover, we also show that some previous code structures of optimal-access cooperative MSR codes and optimal-access MSR codes with $h=1$ are included as special cases of our transformation construction. At last, we note that all of the constructions are built over a finite field of linear size $\geq n+d-k$.

</details>


### [87] [From Tsallis to KL: Convergence and Error Estimates for Tsallis-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06432)
*Takeshi Suguro,Toshiaki Yachimura*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究非负有界连续代价下熵正则化最优传输的Tsallis-to-KL极限，给出精确变分重构、信息投影的存在唯一性、Γ-收敛与O(q-1)误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画从Tsallis正则化到经典KL正则化的过渡，并厘清熵正则化与信息投影之间的关系；在Tsallis情形下，正则化传输问题与信息投影问题并不完全重合，需要量化二者差异。

Method: 固定正则化参数ε>0，将Tsallis正则化最优传输表述为到耦合集上的Tsallis信息投影的精确变分重构，分离出显式修正项；证明Tsallis信息投影的存在唯一性；在窄拓扑下证明q↓1时功能量的Γ-收敛及唯一极小元的窄收敛；并推导误差估计。

Result: 建立Tsallis信息投影的存在唯一性；证明Tsallis正则化泛函在窄拓扑下Γ-收敛到KL正则化泛函，且唯一极小元窄收敛；对正则化最优传输值与相应信息投影值给出显式O(q-1)误差估计，适用于1<q≤2。

Conclusion: 结果量化了Tsallis正则化到经典KL设定的过渡，明确了1<q≤2时熵正则化与信息投影的关系，并解释了Tsallis情形下正则化传输问题与信息投影问题不精确重合的原因。

Abstract: We study the Tsallis-to-Kullback--Leibler (KL) limit for entropy-regularized optimal transport with nonnegative bounded continuous costs. Fixing the regularization parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, we first derive an exact variational reformulation of Tsallis-regularized optimal transport in terms of the Tsallis information projection onto the set of couplings. The formula isolates an explicit correction term and thereby explains why, unlike in the KL case, the regularized transport problem and the corresponding information projection problem do not coincide exactly. We also establish existence and uniqueness for the Tsallis information projection. We then prove, with respect to the narrow topology, the $Γ$-convergence of the Tsallis-regularized functionals to the KL-regularized functional as $q\downarrow1$, together with narrow convergence of their unique minimizers. Finally, we obtain explicit error estimates of order $O(q-1)$ for both the regularized optimal transport values and the associated information projection values. These results quantify the passage from Tsallis regularization to the classical KL setting and clarify the relation between entropic regularization and information projection for $1 < q \leq 2$.

</details>


### [88] [Random Algebraic Geometry Codes Approach the Half-Singleton Bound for Insertions and Deletions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06441)
*Zhihao Guan,Hengjia Wei*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将线性插入删除码的概率分析推广到代数几何（AG）码，证明随机 AG 码在更小字母表下能逼近 half-Singleton 界，并利用 Hermitian 曲线与 García-Stichtenoth 塔突破域尺寸随码长线性增长的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: half-Singleton 界指出 $[n,k]_q$ 线性码最多可纠正 $n-2k+1$ 个插入删除错误，随机 Reed-Solomon 码虽能逼近该界，但要求域大小 $q$ 随码长 $n$ 线性增长。本文旨在突破这一域尺寸障碍，在更小字母表上构造近最优的插入删除纠错码。

Method: 将一般线性插入删除码的概率分析扩展到代数几何码，利用具有多有理点曲线的 AG 码进行随机构造；具体采用一般光滑完备曲线、Hermitian 曲线以及渐近最优的 García-Stichtenoth 塔。

Result: (1) 对固定亏格的一般光滑完备曲线，随机 AG 码以高概率在 $q=\Theta(n)$ 的线性尺寸域上可纠正 $(1-\varepsilon)n-2k$ 个插入删除错误；(2) 利用 Hermitian 曲线，在 $q=\Theta(n^{2/3})$ 的亚线性域上达到该最优性，突破线性域尺寸障碍；(3) 利用渐近最优 García-Stichtenoth 塔，证明存在随机 AG 码以高概率在 $q=2^{O_R(1/\varepsilon^2)}$（与 $n$ 无关）的域上逼近 half-Singleton 界。

Conclusion: 随机代数几何码可在远小于随机 Reed-Solomon 码所需的域尺寸下逼近 half-Singleton 界；多有理点曲线特别是 Hermitian 曲线和 García-Stichtenoth 塔，使近最优插入删除纠错码能够在亚线性甚至与码长无关的域上存在。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the performance of algebraic geometry (AG) codes against adversarial insertion-deletion (insdel) errors. The half-Singleton bound states that an $[n,k]_q$ linear code can correct at most $n-2k+1$ insdel errors. It was recently proven that random Reed-Solomon codes approach this bound. However, these constructions require the field size $q$ to grow linearly with the code length $n$. We overcome this barrier by extending the probabilistic analysis of general linear insdel codes to AG codes. We demonstrate that curves with many rational points allow for nearly optimal codes over significantly smaller alphabets.
  We prove the following main asymptotic results: (1) For general smooth complete curves of fixed genus, random AG codes are nearly optimal, that is, they can correct $(1-\varepsilon)n-2k$ insdel errors with high probability over linear-sized fields ($q=Θ(n)$). (2) By utilizing Hermitian curves, we achieve this optimality over sublinear fields of size $q=Θ(n^{2/3})$, breaking the linear field size barrier. (3) Using asymptotically optimal García-Stichtenoth towers, we prove the existence of random AG codes that approach the half-Singleton bound with high probability over fields of size $q=2^{O_R(1/\varepsilon^2)}$, independent of $n$.

</details>


### [89] [UAV Fluid-Antenna Channel Acquisition under Intra-Scan Channel Aging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06537)
*Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Liang Wu,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机流体天线系统中顺序探测带来的信息-新鲜度权衡，提出联合优化探测位置与停止时机的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序探测可提供额外空间信息，但会延迟传输，导致早先的信道观测老化，从而形成信息量与信息新鲜度之间的权衡。

Method: 采用动态Karhunen-Loève估计器将异步测量与传输状态对齐；用协方差信息增益选择可行的探测位置；引入年龄折扣恒等式与"再探一个时隙"局部条件，刻画协方差层面的信息-新鲜度平衡。

Result: 配对仿真表明：时间对齐可恢复静态堆叠所丢失的大部分下尾可靠性；协方差感知探测在数值上排名最高；在最高测试移动性下，优化后的单时隙探测与双时隙方案在统计上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 在所评估的调度族内，移动性增加使竞争工作区域向更短扫描偏移，支持联合设计探测位置与探测时长。

Abstract: Sequential sounding in UAV fluid-antenna systems (FASs) provides additional spatial information but delays transmission, causing earlier channel observations to age. This paper addresses the resulting information--freshness tradeoff by jointly determining where to probe and when to stop under blockwise hardware constraints. A dynamic Karhunen--Loève estimator aligns asynchronous measurements with the transmission state, covariance information gain selects feasible probe locations, and an age-discount identity with a local one-more-slot condition characterizes the covariance-level balance between information and freshness. Paired simulations show that temporal alignment recovers most of the lower-tail reliability lost by static stacking, covariance-aware probing ranks highest numerically among the evaluated policies, and optimized one-slot sounding becomes statistically competitive with two-slot alternatives at the highest tested mobility. Within the evaluated schedule family, increasing mobility shifts the competitive operating region toward shorter scans, supporting joint probe-placement and sounding-duration design.

</details>


### [90] [The list size of random linear codes at capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06570)
*Shashwat Silas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文确定了任意素数幂 $q$ 下均匀随机线性码的最小列表大小 $L^*(C,\rho)$，其值约为 $\lceil h_q(\rho)/\varepsilon\rceil$（除去分式部分极小的例外情形），从而填补了 $q\ge 3$ 时缺少正确常数上界的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机线性码的列表可解码性研究中，$L^*=\Theta_{q,\rho}(1/\varepsilon)$ 早已知道，但常数精确值未知。已有工作证明了 $q=2$ 时的上界并将 $L^*$ 锁定在三个连续整数内，而 $q\ge 3$ 时缺少具有正确常数的上界，本文旨在对所有素数幂 $q$ 精确确定 $L^*$。

Method: 借助 Mosheiff 等人关于随机线性码的阈值刻画，将上下界归约为对单一量 $V(q,L,\rho)$ 的双侧估计。上界利用新证明的稀疏随机向量在成对非比例线性约束下的熵不等式，并结合 Erdős–Rado 向日葵引理；下界则对 Guruswami 等人引入的分布进行精确分析。

Result: 对所有素数幂 $q$，令 $\zeta=h_q(\rho)/\varepsilon$，对充分小的 $\varepsilon$，以概率 $1-o(1)$ 有 $L^*(C,\rho)=\lceil\zeta\rceil$，除非 $\zeta$ 的分式部分不超过 $q^{-\Omega_{q,\rho}(\zeta)}$，此时 $L^*(C,\rho)$ 为 $\lfloor\zeta\rfloor$ 或 $\lfloor\zeta\rfloor+1$。同时证明对充分大的 $L$：$h_q(\rho)(1+1/L)-q^{-\Omega_{q,\rho}(L)}\le V(q,L,\rho)\le h_q(\rho)(1+1/L)$。

Conclusion: 本文完全确定了任意素数幂 $q$ 下随机线性码的 $L^*(C,\rho)$，纠正并推广了此前仅对 $q=2$ 成立的结果，建立了关于 $V(q,L,\rho)$ 的紧双侧估计，并引入了稀疏随机向量熵不等式这一新工具。

Abstract: Let $C \le \mathbb{F}_q^n$ be a uniformly random $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear code of rate $1 - h_q(ρ) - \varepsilon$, and let $L^*(C,ρ)$ be the least $L$ such that every Hamming ball of relative radius $ρ$ contains at most $L$ codewords of $C$. That $L^* = Θ_{q,ρ}(1/\varepsilon)$ has been known since work of Guruswami, Håstad and Kopparty and of Guruswami and Narayanan. Guruswami, Li, Mosheiff, Resch, Silas and Wootters proved that the constant in front of $1/\varepsilon$ is at least $h_q(ρ)$ for all $q$, along with an upper bound special to $q = 2$ which narrowed $L^*$ to within three consecutive integers in that case. But for $q \ge 3$ no upper bound with the correct constant was known.
  We determine $L^*$ for every prime power $q$. Let $ζ:= h_q(ρ)/\varepsilon$. For every sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, with probability $1-o(1)$ over the choice of $C$, $$L^*(C,ρ) = \lceil ζ\rceil,$$ unless the fractional part of $ζ$ is at most $q^{-Ω_{q,ρ}(ζ)}$, in which case $L^*(C,ρ)$ is $\lfloor ζ\rfloor$ or $\lfloor ζ\rfloor + 1$.
  By the threshold characterization of random linear codes due to Mosheiff, Resch, Ron-Zewi, Silas and Wootters, both bounds reduce to a two-sided estimate of a single quantity $V(q,L,ρ)$, where $1-V(q,L,ρ)$ is the threshold rate for $(ρ,L)$-list-decodability. We prove for all large $L$: $$h_q(ρ)(1 + 1/L) - q^{-Ω_{q,ρ}(L)} \le V(q,L,ρ) \le h_q(ρ)(1 + 1/L).$$ The upper bound rests on a new entropy inequality for sparse random vectors under pairwise non-proportional linear constraints, proved with the Erdős-Rado sunflower lemma. The lower bound is an exact analysis of the distribution introduced by Guruswami, Li, Mosheiff, Resch, Silas and Wootters.

</details>


### [91] [Constrained Capacity for Faster-than-Nyquist Signaling in Frequency-Selective Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06639)
*Zichao Zhang,Melda Yuksel,Gokhan M. Guvensen,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了DFT预编码FTN信号在频率选择性信道下的有限字母容量，给出了并行特征信道分解与可达信息速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTN（超奈奎斯特）信号在频率选择性信道中会引入严重的ISI，且实际系统采用有限字母输入，其可达速率与约束容量难以刻画。

Method: 借助循环前缀（CP）与循环后缀（CS），将FTN与多径引起的ISI分解为并行特征信道，其增益由折叠FTN频谱与信道频率响应共同决定；据此推导有限字母星座的约束容量，并针对无CP/CS的DFT预编码FTN给出失配译码可达信息速率。

Result: 得到有限字母输入下的约束容量表达式，并定量刻画了非理想对角化（无CP/CS）导致的有限块长度速率损失。

Conclusion: 即便在失配译码条件下，FTN相较奈奎斯特传输仍有显著速率提升。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the constrained capacity of discrete Fourier transform (DFT)-precoded faster-than-Nyquist (FTN) signaling over frequency-selective channels with finite-alphabet inputs. With a cyclic prefix (CP) and cyclic suffix (CS), the FTN and multipath induced intersymbol interference (ISI) is decomposed into parallel eigenchannels, whose gains are jointly determined by the folded FTN spectrum and the channel frequency response. Based on this decomposition, we derive the constrained capacity for finite-alphabet constellations and formulate a mismatched decoding achievable information rate for DFT-precoded FTN signaling without CP/CS, quantifying the finite-block rate loss caused by imperfect diagonalization. We find that even under mismatched decoding, FTN significantly improves upon Nyquist transmission.

</details>


### [92] [Improved Degree Tables for Secure Distributed Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06789)
*John Byrne,Rafael G. L. D'Oliveira,Michael Tait*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出构造度表的周期间隙框架，统一了GASP_r、GASP_{r,s}、DOG_{r,s}三类方案，并导出SHIFT_{r,s}与COVER_r两种新构造，在多种情形下优于现有方案，并证明了整数度表恢复阈值的新下界及平衡情形下的渐近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分布式矩阵乘法中，用户借助服务器计算两矩阵乘积，需保证任意T个合谋服务器无法获知任一矩阵信息。度表是构造此类多项式码的组合工具，是GASP_r、GASP_{r,s}、DOG_{r,s}等先进方案的基础，但缺乏统一框架与更优构造。

Method: 引入周期间隙（periodic-gap）框架构造度表，将GASP_r、GASP_{r,s}、DOG_{r,s}作为特例纳入；基于该框架提出SHIFT_{r,s}与COVER_r两种新构造；确定其精确恢复阈值；并对普通整数度表的恢复阈值证明新下界，在平衡情形（划分参数与安全参数相等）下将下界加强至与SHIFT_{r,s}在下低阶项意义下匹配。

Result: 给出SHIFT_{r,s}与COVER_r的精确恢复阈值，并表明其在许多情形下优于当前最优方案；证明整数度表恢复阈值的新下界；在平衡情形下加强该下界，使其与SHIFT_{r,s}在低阶项意义下一致，说明SHIFT_{r,s}在度表构造类中渐近最优。

Conclusion: 周期间隙框架统一并推广了现有度表构造，新构造SHIFT_{r,s}与COVER_r在广泛参数下提升恢复阈值性能，且SHIFT_{r,s}在平衡情形下渐近最优，为安全分布式矩阵乘法的度表设计提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In secure distributed matrix multiplication, a user wishes to compute the product of two matrices with the assistance of servers, in such a way that any $T$ colluding servers learn nothing about either matrix. Degree tables are a combinatorial tool for constructing polynomial codes for this problem and underlie several state-of-the-art schemes, including $\mathsf{GASP}_r$, $\mathsf{GASP}_{r,s}$, and $\mathsf{DOG}_{r,s}$. We introduce a periodic-gap framework for constructing degree tables that contains these three families as special cases and leads to two new constructions, $\mathsf{SHIFT}_{r,s}$ and $\mathsf{COVER}_r$. We determine their exact recovery thresholds and show that, in many cases, they outperform the current state of the art. We also prove a new lower bound on the recovery threshold of ordinary integer degree tables. In the balanced case, in which the partitioning parameters and the security parameter are equal, we sharpen this bound to match $\mathsf{SHIFT}_{r,s}$ up to lower-order terms, showing that it is asymptotically optimal among degree-table constructions.

</details>


### [93] [Quantum Advantage in Multiple Access Wiretap Channels with Entangled Transmitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06808)
*Hassan ZivariFard,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究经典多址窃听信道（MAC-WTC）中发射端共享纠缠对安全通信的增益，给出可达速率区域、保密容量的正则化表达式、单字母上界，并通过伪心灵感应博弈证明纠缠严格优于经典相关随机性；同时建立了含纠缠发射端MAC的强软覆盖引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格语义安全准则下，发射端间共享纠缠能否为经典多址窃听信道带来超越经典相关随机性的保密容量增益，并推广无纠缠系统的既有结论。

Method: 针对含纠缠发射端的MAC-WTC，在严格语义安全准则下推导可达速率区域与保密容量正则化表达式；建立保密容量的单字母上界；并构造含纠缠发射端MAC输出的强软覆盖引理。

Result: 得到了MAC-WTC的可达速率区域与保密容量正则化表达式，以及其单字母上界；证明纠缠相较仅共享经典相关随机性可严格增大保密容量，并以伪心灵感应博弈实例予以说明；给出了含纠缠发射端MAC的新强软覆盖引理。

Conclusion: 纠缠可严格提升MAC-WTC的保密容量，所提结果推广了无纠缠系统的现有结论。

Abstract: We investigate secure communication over a classical multiple-access wiretap channel (MAC-WTC), specifically exploring the benefit of shared entanglement between the transmitters. Under the strict semantic security criterion, we derive an achievable rate region and a regularized expression for the secrecy capacity. We further establish a single-letter upper bound on the secure capacity of the MAC-WTC with entangled transmitters. Our results demonstrate that entanglement strictly enlarges the secrecy capacity of MAC-WTC compared to sharing only classical correlated randomness, a finding we illustrate using a pseudo-telepathy game example. Finally, we establish new strong soft-covering lemmas for the output statistics of multipleaccess channels (MACs) with entangled transmitters. Our results generalize existing results for non-entangled systems.

</details>


### [94] [Equivalence of Fixed-Rank and Rank-One Even-Order Symmetric Tensor Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06971)
*Ruba Hussen Morsi,Anas A. Rahman*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将有限秩尖峰Wigner模型中"秩一等价"结论推广到有限秩偶阶对称张量分解，并通过信息论恒等式与副本对称性把多维变分公式化为一维形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作证明：在Bayes最优设置下，当信号为中心i.i.d.条目时，有限秩尖峰Wigner模型的自由熵大尺寸极限与秩一情形相同。本文旨在检验并推广这一"秩一等价"现象到有限秩偶阶对称张量分解，同时对该结论所需假设给出更自然的表述。

Method: 沿用矩阵情形的信息论恒等式与副本对称（replica symmetry）论证，将已知的极限自由熵多维变分公式化简为其一维类比。关键难点在于副本对称势中包含变分参数的Hadamard（逐元素）幂而非平方，因此需调整矩阵情形中基于特征值的方法。

Result: 证明秩一等价结果在有限秩、偶阶、对称张量分解情形下依然成立；并给出前作中作为该结果必要条件的假设的一个自然重新表述。

Conclusion: 秩一等价现象不仅限于矩阵情形，可自然延伸至偶阶对称张量分解，从而扩展了尖峰模型自由熵极限的理论框架。

Abstract: In the recent work of Barbier, Ko, and the second present author on sublinear-rank symmetric matrix factorization [Math. Stat. Learn. 9 (2026), 1-68], a key result is that, in the Bayes-optimal setting, the large-size limit of the free entropy of the finite-rank spiked Wigner model is the same as in the rank-one case when the signal has centered i.i.d. entries. In this paper, we show that this rank-one equivalence result extends to the case of finite-rank, even-order, symmetric tensor factorization. Moreover, we give a natural reformulation of a hypothesis that was stated in the aforementioned work to be necessary for this result. As in the matrix case, we use information-theoretic identities and replica symmetry to reduce a known multi-dimensional variational formula for the limiting free entropy to its one-dimensional analog. The novelty stems from the fact that said formula involves a replica symmetric potential containing Hadamard (entrywise) powers, rather than squares, of the matrix-valued variational parameter, so the eigenvalue-based approach used in the matrix case must be adjusted.

</details>


### [95] [Spatial-Code-Domain Grouped Index Modulation: Fluid-Antenna-Assisted System Design and BER Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07087)
*Peng Zhang,Jian Dang,Yao Ge,Miaowen Wen,Ziyang Liu,Liang Wu,Zaichen Zhang,Yudong Yao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向流体天线(FAS)辅助收发机的码域分组索引调制(CGIM)及其空间-码域扩展(SCGIM)，并推导了误码率(BER)理论分析、设计了多种检测器，仿真验证了方案在BER与吞吐量上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统(FAS)能在紧凑孔径内提供可重构的空间资源，但如何在FAS辅助链路中高效利用码域与空间域联合承载信息、并降低检测复杂度，仍缺乏系统性的调制与检测方案及理论性能分析。

Method: 提出CGIM：将正交扩频码划分为多个子集，联合映射到同相/正交索引与星座符号；在Rx-FAS辅助SIMO链路中采用分组解扩分离正交码组以实现并行检测，并通过接收端口选择获得空间分集。进一步提出SCGIM：将交织的Tx-FA端口子集与码子集关联，Tx-FAS承载空间索引信息、Rx-FAS提供选择分集，构成MIMO链路。为SCGIM设计ML、分阶段贪心(GD)与跨域索引消息传递(CD-IMPD)检测器，后者在无环因子图上交换软信息，仅需一次内向与一次外向消息传递。对CGIM，基于解扩域观测的联合判决区域推导BER，并在Rayleigh、Nakagami-m及AWGN信道下按Rx-FAS选择增益分布取平均；对SCGIM，基于全对联合界，利用选择增益密度比与指数倾斜二次型Laplace变换推导平均BER近似。

Result: 仿真结果验证了BER理论分析的正确性；所提方案相较对比的索引调制(IM)方案具有更低的BER和更高的吞吐量；CD-IMPD在检测复杂度更低的同时，BER性能接近ML检测。

Conclusion: CGIM与SCGIM有效结合码域与空间域索引调制，充分利用FAS的可重构空间资源提升频谱效率与可靠性；所提BER分析方法与CD-IMPD检测器在性能与复杂度之间取得了良好折中。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FASs) provide reconfigurable spatial resources within compact apertures. In this paper, we introduce code-domain grouped index modulation (CGIM) and its spatial-code-domain extension, termed SCGIM, for FA-assisted transceivers. CGIM partitions the available orthogonal spreading codes into multiple subsets and jointly maps information onto their in-phase and quadrature indices and constellation symbols. In an Rx-FAS-assisted single-input multiple-output (SIMO) link, group-wise despreading separates the orthogonal code groups for parallel detection, while receive-port selection provides spatial diversity. SCGIM further associates interleaved Tx-FA port subsets with the code subsets, with the Tx-FAS conveying spatial-index information and the Rx-FAS providing selection diversity in a multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) link. For SCGIM, we develop maximum-likelihood (ML), staged greedy (GD), and cross-domain index message-passing (CD-IMPD) detectors. CD-IMPD exchanges soft information over a cycle-free factor graph to account for the coupling between the spatial and code indices, requiring only one inward and one outward message pass. For CGIM, the BER is derived from the joint decision regions of the despread-domain observations and averaged over the Rx-FAS selected-gain distribution under Rayleigh, Nakagami-m, and additive white Gaussian noise channels. For SCGIM, an average-BER approximation is derived from a full-pair union bound using the selected-gain density ratio and exponentially tilted quadratic-form Laplace transforms. Simulation results validate the BER analysis and show that the proposed schemes achieve lower BER and higher throughput than the considered IM schemes, while CD-IMPD achieves near-ML BER performance with lower detection complexity.

</details>


### [96] [Conditional Fisher-Information Central Limit Theorems under Log-Concavity with Information-Theoretic Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07150)
*Tong Ye,Liu-Quan Yao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在任意固定维度、对数凹假设下建立了Fisher信息意义下的条件中心极限定理，证明条件标准化和经白化后的平均条件Fisher信息在某一卷积层级有限时收敛到维度，并给出条件相对熵、条件熵收敛及信息论应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CLT的收敛多以分布函数或相对熵刻画；本文旨在以Fisher信息这一更强、更局部的度量建立条件版本的中心极限定理，并刻画条件相对Fisher信息与高斯基准的差距，为条件独立结构下的信息论问题（如低功率输入、Gaussian信令的互信息缺口）提供定量工具。

Method: 在固定维度对数凹条件下：(1) 对条件中心化标准化和，先用平均条件协方差白化，证明平均条件Fisher信息收敛到维度当且仅当其在某一卷积层级有限；(2) 给出基于高斯平滑、尾部受控类上Fisher积二阶连续性定理的独立标量证明；(3) 对原始和，证明平均Fisher信息矩阵在算子范数下收敛到逆平均条件协方差；(4) 借助高斯对数Sobolev不等式将条件相对Fisher信息消失转化为条件相对熵与条件熵收敛。

Result: 条件标准化白化后平均条件Fisher信息收敛到维度，充要条件为其在某一卷积层级有限；标量情形作为一维特例成立；原始和的平均Fisher信息矩阵按算子范数收敛到逆平均条件协方差；条件相对Fisher信息关于极限高斯律消失，进而条件相对熵与条件熵收敛；低功率有限星座输入的一阶条件互信息斜率收敛到高斯噪声基准，Gaussian信令下互信息与基准的缺口由条件相对Fisher亏量控制并渐近消失。

Conclusion: Fisher信息可有效刻画条件CLT，条件相对Fisher信息的收敛足以推出条件相对熵与条件熵的收敛，并直接导出低功率输入与Gaussian信令两类信息论基准结果。

Abstract: We establish conditional central limit theorems in Fisher information under log-concavity in every fixed dimension. For conditionally centered normalized sums, after whitening by the averaged conditional covariance, the averaged conditional Fisher information converges to the dimension if and only if it is finite at one convolution level. The scalar criterion follows as the one-dimensional case; we also provide an independent scalar proof based on a second-order continuity theorem for Fisher production on Gaussian-smoothed, tail-controlled classes. For the original sums, the averaged Fisher information matrix converges in operator norm to the inverse averaged conditional covariance. Consequently, the conditional relative Fisher information with respect to the limiting Gaussian law vanishes, and the Gaussian logarithmic Sobolev inequality yields convergence in conditional relative entropy and conditional entropy. We give two operational consequences. For any fixed finite-constellation low-power input, the first-order conditional mutual-information slope converges to the Gaussian-noise benchmark. For Gaussian signaling at any fixed signal covariance, the mutual-information gap from that benchmark is bounded by the conditional relative Fisher deficit and hence vanishes asymptotically.

</details>


### [97] [Cyclic Codes of Length 7_p^s over F_p^m + uF_p^m : Characterization, Duals, and Applications to Quantum and LCD Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07181)
*Payel Chandra,Kalyan Hansda*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究环 R_2 上长度 7p^s 的循环码，利用 Φ_7(x) 的不可约性做直和分解，分类 7-cyclotomic 码并给出基数、对偶生成元，构造量子码与 LCD 码判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环 F_{p^m}+uF_{p^m}（u^2=0）上循环码结构已有研究，但长度含因子 7 的情形因 Φ_7(x) 的分解行为而有特殊结构；作者希望在 p≡3,5 (mod 7) 且 gcd(m,6)=1 时借助 Φ_7(x) 不可约，系统刻画此类码并拓展其量子与 LCD 应用。

Method: 在 R_2=F_{p^m}+uF_{p^m} 上取长度 7p^s；由 Φ_7(x) 在 F_{p^m} 上不可约，将循环码 C 分解为 C=C_1⊕C_2，其中 C_1 长 p^s、C_2 为长 6p^s 的 7-cyclotomic 码；按生成元将 7-cyclotomic 码分为四类互斥型，用剩余码与挠子码计算精确基数，并显式给出欧几里得对偶码 C^⊥ 的生成元；最后用 CSS 框架构造量子稳定子码，并给出 LCD 码的参数判据。

Result: 得到循环码的直和分解结构；给出四类 7-cyclotomic 码的分类及精确基数公式；确定欧几里得对偶码的显式生成元；构造出新的量子稳定子码族，并建立线性码为 LCD 码的参数判据。

Conclusion: 在 Φ_7(x) 不可约的条件下，R_2 上长度 7p^s 的循环码可完全由 C_1 与 7-cyclotomic 码 C_2 的结构刻画，分类与计数结果可直接用于构造量子稳定子码和判定 LCD 码。

Abstract: Let $R_2 = \mathbb{F}_{p^m} + u\mathbb{F}_{p^m}$ ($u^2 = 0$), where $p$ is an odd prime and $m, s \in \mathbb{N}$. For $p \equiv 3, 5 \pmod 7$ with $\gcd(m, 6) = 1$, the cyclotomic polynomial $Φ_7(x)$ is irreducible over $\mathbb{F}_{p^m}$. This yields a direct sum decomposition $C = C_1 \oplus C_2$ for any cyclic code $C$ of length $7p^s$ over $R_2$, where $C_1$ has length $p^s$ and $C_2$ is a $7$-cyclotomic code of length $6p^s$. We classify $7$-cyclotomic codes into four disjoint generator-based types and calculate exact cardinalities using residue and torsion subcodes. Furthermore, explicit generators for the Euclidean dual codes $C^\perp$ are determined. As operational applications of these classified codes, we construct new families of quantum stabilizer codes via the CSS framework and establish parameter criteria for linear codes with complementary duals (LCD codes).

</details>


### [98] [A Fundamental Limit in Decentralized Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07479)
*Marco Carpentiero,Felice Scala,Vincenzo Matta,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文证明去中心化决策存在不可消除的性能损失，并给出该损失与图距离及似然比矩母函数相关的闭式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化估计已被证明可达到最优集中式性能，但作者质疑该结论是否适用于去中心化决策，旨在揭示去中心化决策与分类交互作用下的根本性能极限。

Method: 针对按网络图连接、仅能与邻居局部交换信息并处理流式观测的多个智能体，分析其迭代决策过程，推导最优去中心化决策策略错误概率相对最优集中式分类器的不可约损失，并建立闭式解析关系：损失由节点间图距离与刻画决策问题的似然比矩母函数共同决定。随后将所得关系应用于不同网络拓扑与推断问题。

Result: 证明最优去中心化决策策略的错误概率存在相对最优集中式分类器的不可约损失；给出损失与图距离、似然比矩母函数相互作用的闭式关系；刻画常见网络拓扑下损失随网络规模的缩放律，显示错误概率可相差数量级；并揭示信息性智能体与非信息性智能体之间相对图距离对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 去中心化决策不同于去中心化估计，无法达到集中式最优性能，其性能受“去中心化—分类”耦合根本限制；该极限可通过图距离与似然比矩母函数的解析关系定量刻画，并随拓扑与推断问题呈现显著差异。

Abstract: In decentralized decision-making, several agents connected according to a network graph aim at solving a classification problem by collecting streaming observations. Due to decentralization, they run an iterative algorithm where, at each iteration, they can only exchange information locally with their neighbors. While decentralized estimation solutions have been shown to match the performance of optimal centralized systems, we show here that surprisingly this conclusion does not hold for decentralized decision-making. Specifically, we prove that the error probability for the best decentralized decision strategy exhibits an irreducible loss with respect to the optimal centralized classifier. This result establishes a fundamental limit for the performance of any decentralized decision strategy. We obtain an analytical relation showing that this limit is related to the interplay between decentralization and classification. The first aspect appears through the distances between the nodes in the graph, while the second aspect plays through the moment generating functions of the likelihood ratios that describe the decision problem. By applying the derived closed-form relation to different network topologies and inference problems, we observe some interesting and perhaps unexpected behavior emerging. In particular, we characterize the scaling law (with the network size) for the loss over popular network topologies, showing that the error probabilities might differ by orders of magnitude; and we examine how performance is affected by the relative distance between informative and uninformative agents over the graph.

</details>


### [99] [Resource-Constrained Semantic-Aware Remote Estimation with Overlapping Sensor Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07563)
*Bowen Sun,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究多有限状态马尔可夫源在K个重叠覆盖传感器下的语义感知远程估计，建模为平均代价约束MDP，证明传输资源函数秩≤K，拉格朗日仅依赖K个有效传输代价，最优约束解可用至多K+1个确定性策略-循环类分量表示，并给出分段仿射凹拉格朗日值函数与投影对偶次梯度上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器共享时分多址上行链路，且在传输可靠性、交付时延与传输预算上存在差异，需要在全局和单传感器传输频率约束下联合调度源与监测传感器，以最小化长期平均驱动误差，兼顾语义感知与资源受限的远程估计需求。

Method: 将问题形式化为有限平均代价约束马尔可夫决策过程；分析传输资源函数秩性质；利用拉格朗日对偶，将约束问题化简为仅含K个有效传输代价的形式；证明最优解的确定性策略-循环类分量结构；刻画分段仿射凹拉格朗日值函数，并在显式有界乘子集上推导投影对偶次梯度上升算法。

Result: 证明传输资源函数秩至多为K，全局约束仍可能压缩可行域；拉格朗日仅依赖K个有效传输代价；最优约束解可由至多K+1个确定性策略-循环类分量表示；给出分段仿射凹值函数结构；数值结果展示值函数形态、代表性实例中策略随机化的必要性，以及全局与单传感器传输预算间的交互作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为多源多传感器语义感知远程估计提供了一种基于约束MDP与对偶分解的调度优化框架，揭示了秩结构带来的降维优势，并说明在多约束耦合下可能需要策略随机化，为资源受限估计系统的调度设计提供理论依据与算法支撑。

Abstract: We study semantic-aware remote estimation of multiple finite-state Markov sources observed by K sensors with overlapping coverage. The sensors share a time-division multiple-access uplink and differ in transmission reliability, delivery delay, and transmission budget. In each slot, the scheduler jointly selects a source and one of its monitoring sensors, or remains idle, to minimize the long-run average cost of actuation error subject to global and per-sensor transmission-frequency constraints. We formulate this problem as a finite average-cost constrained Markov decision process. We show that the transmission resource functions have rank at most K, although the global constraint may still restrict the feasible region. Consequently, the Lagrangian depends only on K effective transmission costs, and an optimal constrained solution can be represented using at most K+1 deterministic policy-recurrent-class components. We further characterize the piecewise-affine concave Lagrangian value function and derive projected dual subgradient ascent over an explicitly bounded multiplier set. Numerical results illustrate the value-function structure, the need for policy randomization in a representative instance, and the interaction between global and per-sensor transmission budgets.

</details>


### [100] [A Note on Binary Quadratic Systems and their relation to complexity theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07769)
*Gabriele Radici,Massimiliano Sala*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明：在 $\mathbb F_2$ 上，变量数与方程数相等的多元二次方程组恰有一个解的系统数严格多于无解的系统数，且二者之比被 $(1+1/(2^n-1))$ 控制；该结论推广到次数不超过 $d$ 的多项式函数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定 $\mathbb F_2$ 上多元二次方程组是否有解是经典 NP 完全问题，对平方系统亦然，且是现代后量子密码学的基石之一。已知无解语言 $\cup_{n\ge2}\MQ_0(n)$ 为 coNP 完全，恰一解语言 $\cup_{n\ge2}\MQ_1(n)$ 属于 DP，且渐近地 $|\MQ_1(n)|/|\MQ_0(n)|\to1$。作者希望给出有限 $n$ 的显式定量界，刻画 $n$ 较小时两类系统计数之间的精细差距。

Method: 作者将 $(\FF_2)^n$ 视为 $Q_d$ 的赋值拟阵的基集，用特征多项式表达 $\alpha_0$ 与 $\alpha_1$；再借助 Whitney 型符号反转对合，证明能使 $\alpha_1-\alpha_0$ 低于 $\alpha_1/2^n$ 的项仅来自某个拟阵 port 的元素。这些元素被识别为 Reed--Muller 码 $\RM(n-d-1,n)=\RM(d,n)^\perp$ 的最小支撑字；最终估计由 MacWilliams 恒等式、最小距离界 $2^{d+1}$ 以及该码的偶重量结构导出。

Result: 证明了显式有限 $n$ 界：$|\MQ_0(n)|<|\MQ_1(n)|\le(1+1/(2^n-1))|\MQ_0(n)|$。更一般地，设 $\alpha_k$ 为 $(Q_d)^n$ 中恰有 $k$ 个解的平方系统数，则对 $2\le d\le n$ 有 $\alpha_0<\alpha_1\le(1+1/(2^n-1))\alpha_0$。

Conclusion: 通过拟阵与编码论方法的结合，建立了 $\mathbb F_2$ 上平方多项式系统无解与恰一解计数之间的显式比值上界，推广了此前仅渐近成立的结果，并为相关计数问题提供了新的组合与编码论工具。

Abstract: Deciding whether a system of multivariate quadratic equations over $\mathbb F_2$ has a solution is a classical NP-complete problem, and remains so for square systems, with as many equations as variables. The hardness of this problem is one of the cornerstones of nowadays post-quantum cryptography. Let $\MQ_0(n)$ and $\MQ_1(n)$ denote the sets of square quadratic systems in $n$ variables having respectively no solutions and exactly one solution. $\cup_{n\geq 2} \MQ_0(n)$ is a coNP-complete language, while $\cup_{n\geq 2} \MQ_1(n)$ lies in DP. It is known that $\lim_{n\to \infty} |\MQ_1(n)|/|\MQ_0(n)|=1$. Here we prove the explicit finite-$n$ bounds \[ |\MQ_0(n)|<|\MQ_1(n)| \le \left(1+\frac{1}{2^n-1}\right)|\MQ_0(n)|, \]
  More generally, let $Q_d$ be the space of polynomial functions $(\FF_2)^n\to\mathbb F_2$ of degree at most $d$, and let $α_k$ count square systems in $(Q_d)^n$ having exactly $k$ solutions. Then \[ α_0<α_1 \le \left(1+\frac{1}{2^n-1}\right)α_0\,, \qquad 2\le d\le n \,. \] The proof combines matroid and coding-theoretic methods. We interpret $(\FF_2)^n$ as the ground set of the evaluation matroid of $Q_d$, express $α_0$ and $α_1$ through characteristic polynomials, and use a Whitney-type sign-reversing involution to show that the only terms that can push $α_1-α_0$ below $α_1/2^n$ come from the elements of a matroid port. These are identified with minimal-support words of the Reed--Muller code $\RM(n-d-1,n)=\RM(d,n)^\perp$; the required estimate then follows from the MacWilliams identity, the minimum-distance bound $2^{d+1}$, and the even-weight structure of the code.

</details>


### [101] [Exact Degrees of Freedom of Spatially Sparse MIMO Channels Without Prior CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07926)
*Yifeng Xiong,Weijiang Zhao,Fan Liu,Shi Jin,Jianhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了发射端与接收端配置非均匀线阵、无先验CSI的点对点块状无记忆信道的自由度：对固定K条传播路径，当满足天线数与块长条件时，DoF为K=1时的1-1/T以及K≥2时的K(1-3/(2T))，并给出ISAC中的DoF权衡应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DoF研究多依赖均匀线阵或已知CSI等理想假设，缺乏对无先验CSI、任意非均匀线阵配置下块状衰落信道基本极限的刻画；同时ISAC等新兴场景需要明确无CSI时的DoF权衡基准。

Method: 建立点对点块状无记忆信道模型：Tx/Rx分别配置Nt、Nr个元素位置固定、已知且互异的NULA，ULA为特例；每块长度T内连续AoA、AoD及独立复高斯路径增益重新抽取，收发仅知状态分布而不知当前实现；参考符号计入T且其能量纳入功率约束。在此模型下推导DoF的可达性与逆定理，并将结果用于ISAC的DoF权衡分析；对更一般阵列结构给出可达性结果。

Result: 在Nr≥K+1、Nt≥max{K,2}、T≥K条件下，DoF为：K=1时为1-1/T；K≥2时为K(1-3/(2T))。结果进一步应用于ISAC的DoF权衡分析；对更一般阵列结构得到可达性结果，而一般逆定理仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 论文精确刻画了无先验CSI、非均匀线阵、块状衰落条件下点对点信道的DoF，揭示了路径数K与块长T对DoF的影响规律，并为ISAC权衡分析提供理论支撑；一般阵列结构的逆定理尚未解决。

Abstract: We characterize the degree of freedom (DoF) of a point-to-point blockwise memoryless channel without prior channel state information (CSI), with a fixed number $K$ of propagation paths, where the transmitter (Tx) and the receiver (Rx) are equipped with nonuniform linear arrays (NULAs) of $N_t$ and $N_r$ antennas, respectively. The positions of array elements are fixed, known, pairwise distinct, and need not be equally spaced. The uniform linear array (ULA) is a special case. In each block of length $T$, the continuous angles of arrival (AoAs), angles of departure (AoDs), and independent complex Gaussian path gains are redrawn. Both Tx and Rx know the state distributions but are not given the current realizations before transmission. The receiver may estimate the channel from reference signals or decode without explicit channel estimation, with reference symbols counted in $T$ and their energy counted against the power constraint. Under the aforementioned model, we show that the DoF is $1-\frac{1}{T}$ for $K=1$, and $K(1-\frac{3}{2T})$ for $K \geq 2$, when $N_r\ge K+1$, $N_t\ge\max\{K,2\}$, and $T\ge K$. The analytical results are further demonstrated by their applications to the DoF tradeoff analysis in integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). For more general array structures, an achievability result is established, while the converse remains open in general.

</details>


### [102] [Algorithmic List Decoding of Reed-Solomon Codes up to Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08005)
*Joshua Brakensiek,Yeyuan Chen,Aaron Putterman,Zihan Zhang,Kai Zhe Zheng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文给出素数域上Reed-Solomon码的确定性多项式时间列表译码算法，对任意赋值集与任意常数码率均逼近列表译码容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reed-Solomon码的列表译码已有重要进展，但此前结果多依赖随机化方法，或仅对特定赋值集、特定码率有效，难以同时达到列表译码容量。本文旨在消除这些限制，实现确定性、广泛适用且逼近容量的算法。

Method: 提出确定性多项式时间列表译码算法，针对素数域上的Reed-Solomon码，在任意赋值集与任意常数码率下进行译码，并证明其输出列表规模逼近列表译码容量所允许的界。

Result: 算法在多项式时间内运行，且无论赋值集如何、码率为何（只要为常数），都能达到接近列表译码容量的纠错半径，即半径约为 1 − R（R为码率），显著优于经典唯一译码半径 (1−R)/2。

Conclusion: 该工作为Reed-Solomon码提供了统一且确定性的列表译码方案，突破了此前对赋值集与码率的限制，使列表译码容量在素数域情形下可被高效逼近。

Abstract: We give a deterministic polynomial-time list-decoding algorithm for Reed-Solomon codes over prime fields that approaches list-decoding capacity for every evaluation set and every constant rate.

</details>


### [103] [Function Tables for Secure Distributed Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08154)
*Rafael G. L. D'Oliveira,Giulia Gaggero,Arturo Jaramillo Gil,Hiram H. López,Cecilia Martínez-Reyes,Divyesh Vaghasiya*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文引入“函数表”表示法统一刻画安全分布式矩阵乘法（SDMM）中工人响应系数函数，将隐私转化为秩条件、可译性转化为线性无关条件，从而对一般线性编码/解码模型给出更紧的逆定理，并精确确定 $T=1$ 的最优工人数及若干下界与域可行性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 SDMM 结果多依赖结构化系数函数（度表、循环加法表、代数几何构造），逆定理仅适用于特定家族，无法约束所有线性方案，故需要一个无结构假设的统一表示来获得普适且更紧的界限。

Method: 提出函数表：外积划分下含 $K$ 行块、$L$ 列块、抗 $T$ 合谋，用逐元素系数函数表示工人响应；隐私等价于数据与掩码系数的秩条件，可译性等价于目标项模冗余空间的线性无关性；在一般线性编码/线性解码模型下推导逆定理。

Result: $T=1$ 时最优值为 $q\ge3$ 下的 $KL+K+L$，$\mathbb{F}_2$ 上为 $KL+K+L+1$；一般 $T$ 证明 $N\ge KL+K+L$ 与 $N\ge\max\{K,L\}+T$（无需掩码 MDS 假设），前者在 $\min\{K,L\}\ge2T$ 时强于已知界 $KL+\max\{K,L\}+2T-1$；域可行性等价于 $[\max\{K,L\}+T,T]$ 线性 MDS 码存在，且存在时 Cartesian 构造达 $N=(K+T)(L+T)$；$T=2$ 时 $q\ge\max\{K,L\}+1$ 充分必要，并在 $KL+K+L\mid q-1$ 时给出 $N=KL+K+L+2$ 的射影直线构造。

Conclusion: 函数表提供了一个不依赖结构化系数族的通用框架，使逆定理覆盖所有线性 SDMM 方案，并给出更紧的工人数下界与精确的域可行性刻画；$T=2$ 的射影直线构造在 $K,L\ge2$ 时达到已知最优工人数，且仅需阶为 $KL+K+L$ 的元素。

Abstract: We introduce function tables, an entrywise representation of the coefficient functions that appear in the worker responses of a secure distributed matrix multiplication (SDMM) scheme. We work under the outer-product partition, with $K$ row blocks, $L$ column blocks, and privacy against any $T$ colluding workers, in the general model of linear encoding and linear decoding. In this representation, privacy is a rank condition on the data and mask coefficients, and decodability is linear independence of the desired entries modulo the nuisance space. Degree tables, cyclic-addition tables, and algebraic-geometry constructions are the special cases obtained by restricting the coefficient functions to a structured family; we impose no such restriction, so our converses bind every linear scheme. For $T=1$, we determine the exact optimum over every finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$: it is $KL+K+L$ when $q\geq3$, and $KL+K+L+1$ over $\mathbb{F}_2$, where the identity $z^2=z$ forces one more worker. For arbitrary $T$, we prove $N\geq KL+K+L$ and $N\geq\max\{K,L\}+T$ with no MDS hypothesis on the masks; the first is stronger than the previously known bound $KL+\max\{K,L\}+2T-1$ whenever $\min\{K,L\}\geq2T$. We then reduce field feasibility exactly to MDS existence: a scheme exists over $\mathbb{F}_q$ if and only if an $[\max\{K,L\}+T,T]$ linear MDS code does, and whenever it does, a Cartesian construction attains $N=(K+T)(L+T)$ over that same field. For $T=2$ this makes $q\geq\max\{K,L\}+1$ necessary and sufficient, and we give a projective-line construction with $N=KL+K+L+2$ whenever $KL+K+L$ divides $q-1$; for $K,L\geq2$ it matches the best known worker count while requiring only an element of order $KL+K+L$.

</details>


### [104] [Asymptotically good binary triorthogonal codes and higher-level transversal gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08203)
*Rodrigo San-José*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了从第三层起每个Clifford层级上的渐近好二进制CSS码，支持对角横向门，并给出首个渐近好的二进制三正交码族及常数开销T态块蒸馏方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford层级高层级的二进制CSS码是容错量子计算实现非Clifford门（如T门）的关键资源，但此前缺乏渐近好且支持对角横向门的二进制码族。

Method: 基于二进制扩域上的代数几何码，通过字母表约化将扩域上的正交性条件转化为二进制重叠条件，构造各层级的对角横向门码；并给出无需后续纠错的更强构造；采用基于纠错的蒸馏协议。

Result: 从第三层起每个层级均构造出渐近好的二进制CSS码，获得首个渐近好的二进制三正交码族；更强构造参数较差但无需后续纠正；在足够弱的输入噪声和理想稳定子操作下，T门码族实现常数开销T态块蒸馏。

Conclusion: 通过代数几何码与字母表约化，实现了全层级对角横向门的渐近好二进制CSS码，特别是首个渐近好二进制三正交码，并借纠错蒸馏协议达成常数开销T态块蒸馏。

Abstract: For each level of the Clifford hierarchy from the third level onward, we construct explicit asymptotically good binary CSS codes supporting diagonal transversal gates in that level. We achieve this using algebraic geometry codes over binary extension fields and performing an alphabet reduction that translates orthogonality conditions over the extension field to binary overlap conditions. In particular, we obtain the first asymptotically good family of binary triorthogonal codes. We also give a stronger construction with worse parameters for which no subsequent correction is required. Finally, adapting an error-correction-based distillation protocol, our binary $T$-gate families yield constant-overhead $T$-state block distillation under sufficiently weak input noise and ideal stabilizer operations.

</details>


### [105] [Finite-Modal Realization and Operator-Norm Convergence of a Source-to-Observation Electromagnetic Scattering Green Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08229)
*Zhukang Wang,Da Li,Ruifeng Li,Jinyan Ma,Jiarun Hu,Jiahui Wang,Anqi Xia,Tengjiao Wang,Said Mikki,Er-Ping Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文将多散射环境中的源-观测算符建模为连续源/观测空间上的散射格林算符，给出精确迹空间分解与有限阶VSWF实现，证明算子范数收敛并保证奇异值一致收敛，同时构建保持非零奇异值的有限度量核，通过全波基准与近共振双球实验揭示相邻阶一致性可能掩盖主导高阶集体通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多查询电磁预测与通信模式分析常依赖可复用的源-观测算符，但实际多散射模型中算符仅由有限个角模表示；在选定激励下的一致性或相邻截断阶之间的一致性，并不能保证整个映射的一致精度，也不能保证其奇异通道的可靠性。因此需要一个具有收敛性与度量一致性的有限模表示框架来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 在固定的连续源空间与观测空间上，将环境诱导响应表述为散射格林算符，并推导精确的迹空间分解以重构Maxwell散射场。针对固定的两两不相交包围迹球与良态集体问题，证明嵌套VSWF实现按算子范数收敛。给出结构性界，将外部模尾与集体预解敏感性分离。构造一个有限度量核，在谱问题中保持每个有限阶算符的非零奇异值，并重构匹配的正交归一源-场通道，且不引入外部支撑离散自由度。用全波基准验证有限阶实现。

Result: 算子范数收敛保证奇异值的一致收敛。全波基准验证了有限阶实现。受控近共振双球研究表明：相邻阶一致性可能先于主导高阶集体方向的分辨而出现；只有部分内部放大体现在外部可及增益中；共振会将一对不同的高阶通道提升到原本保留的低阶族之上。

Conclusion: 该框架为多散射源-观测算符及其可及通道提供了收敛且度量一致的有限模表示，说明仅凭相邻阶一致性不足以判定算符可靠性，必须结合算子范数收敛与奇异通道分析。

Abstract: Source-to-observation operators provide reusable environment-level descriptions for multi-query electromagnetic (EM) prediction and communication-mode analysis. However, in practical multiple-scattering models, these operators are represented with finitely many angular modes, and agreement for selected excitations or between successive truncation orders does not establish uniform accuracy of the full map or reliability of its singular channels. To close this gap, we formulate the environment-induced response as a scattering Green operator on fixed continuous source and observation spaces and derive an exact trace-space factorization that reconstructs the Maxwell scattered field. For fixed, pairwise-disjoint enclosing trace spheres and a well-posed collective problem, nested vector spherical wave function (VSWF) realizations converge in operator norm. A structural bound separates external modal tails from collective-resolvent sensitivity, and operator-norm convergence guarantees uniform convergence of the singular values. We further construct a finite metric core that preserves the nonzero singular values of each finite-order operator and reconstructs matched orthonormal source--field channels without introducing external-support discretization degrees of freedom (DoF) into the spectral problem. Full-wave benchmarks verify the finite-order implementation. A controlled near-resonant two-sphere study shows that adjacent-order agreement can precede resolution of the dominant high-order collective direction. It further shows that only part of the internal amplification appears in externally accessible gains and that resonance promotes a distinct high-order channel pair above an otherwise preserved low-order family. The resulting framework provides a convergent, metric-consistent finite-modal representation of multiple-scattering source-to-observation operators and their accessible channels.

</details>


### [106] [Variational Bayesian Data Detection for Multiuser MIMO Systems Corrupted by Phase Noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08252)
*Toan-Van Nguyen,Duy H. N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对上行多用户MIMO中每根天线独立振荡器导致的相位噪声问题，提出基于变分贝叶斯的联合相位噪声估计与数据检测框架，通过将发射端相位噪声吸收进发送信号并采用von Mises先验，得到闭式共轭后验更新，实现低复杂度且可扩展的高性能检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上行多用户MIMO系统中，收发天线各配备独立振荡器，产生互不相关的乘性相位噪声，严重恶化系统性能。现有接收端补偿算法要么依赖线性化近似、在严重相位噪声下精度不足，要么计算复杂度随天线数呈难以承受的增长，亟需兼顾精度与可扩展性的方案。

Method: 提出变分贝叶斯（VB）框架，用于上行MIMO的相位噪声估计与数据检测联合处理。将噪声统计量视为隐变量；通过把发射端相位噪声吸收进发送信号，把由此构成的复合变量作为推断目标进行问题重构；在von Mises先验下，该重构得到精确的闭式共轭后验更新，并据此推导出改进检测器，在低复杂度下取得更优性能。

Result: 仿真表明，在广泛的信道条件、调制阶数和相位噪声强度范围内，所提VB算法的符号错误率均低于自干扰白化（SIW）算法及传统相位噪声无感知检测器，同时计算复杂度对大规摸MIMO部署保持良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提变分贝叶斯联合估计与检测框架在精度、复杂度与可扩展性之间取得良好折中，是大规模上行多用户MIMO系统中抑制相位噪声的有效方案。

Abstract: Phase noise (PN), arising from imperfect local oscillators, introduces multiplicative distortions that degrade the performance of communication systems. In uplink multiuser multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, this impairment is further compounded by the presence of independent oscillators at each transmit and receive antenna, each contributing an uncorrelated noise component. Existing PN compensation algorithms at the receiver either rely on linearization approximations that lose accuracy under severe PN conditions, or incur computational complexity that scales prohibitively with the number of antennas. To address these limitations, we propose a variational Bayes (VB) framework for joint PN estimation and data detection in uplink MIMO systems. We develop VB-based detectors that treat noise statistics as latent variables, and reformulate the inference problem by absorbing the transmitter PN into the transmitted signal, treating the resulting composite variable as the inference target. Under von Mises priors, this reformulation yields exact closed-form conjugate posterior updates, from which we derive an improved detector achieving superior performance at low complexity. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed VB algorithm achieves lower symbol error rates than the Self-Interference Whitening (SIW) algorithm and conventional phase-noise-unaware detectors across a wide range of channel conditions, modulation orders, and PN severities, while remaining computationally scalable to large MIMO deployments.

</details>


### [107] [Physics of Information Geometry - Part I: Principle of Least Action on the Probability Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08285)
*C. Emre Koksal,Deniz Sargun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最小作用量框架，用于描述概率分布在受限增量变化下从平衡态演化到指定非平衡态的过程，并以信息投影与相对熵的勾股结构为核心，构造贪心路径、给出闭式刻画与有限步性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何统一理解概率分布的演化过程，使“从平衡态到非平衡态”的转变同时具有几何意义与物理意义，并降低大跨度状态转移的动力学代价，是本文的核心动机。作者希望借助最小作用量与信息几何，将非平衡自由能、信息动力学代价和能量守恒式关系纳入同一框架。

Method: 以Gibbs分布作为平衡参考，将概率单纯形的几何赋予物理含义：到平衡态的距离对应非平衡自由能，相邻分布间变化对应信息动力学代价。利用相对熵的勾股结构，采用序贯信息投影选择中间分布；据此构造贪心最小作用量路径，并借助Lambert W函数给出每个投影的闭式刻画；进一步通过重塑底层Gibbs参考来纳入状态依赖代价，将路径惩罚解释为有效能量景观的修正。

Result: 序贯信息投影选择的中间分布能够降低大步转移的动力学代价；建立了路径动力学支出与到达目标分布所积累自由能之间的类能量守恒关系；构造了贪心最小作用量路径；给出了每个信息投影的Lambert W闭式表征；获得了有限步性能保证；并表明状态依赖代价可自然嵌入框架。

Conclusion: 该工作通过最小作用量、信息几何与非平衡热力学的统一视角，刻画了概率分布的演化，揭示了自由能与信息动力学代价之间的守恒式联系，并为路径设计与状态依赖代价建模提供了可解释的物理框架。

Abstract: We develop a least-action framework for describing how a probability distribution can evolve from an equilibrium state to a prescribed nonequilibrium state under constrained incremental changes. Taking a Gibbs distribution as the equilibrium reference, the framework gives a direct physical meaning to the geometry of the probability simplex: distance from equilibrium corresponds to nonequilibrium free energy, while changes between successive distributions carry an informational kinetic cost. The Pythagorean structure of relative entropy then provides the central insight of the work. It shows that intermediate distributions chosen via sequential information projections can reduce the kinetic cost of large transitions and establishes an energy-conservation-like relation between the kinetic expenditure along a path and the free energy accumulated in reaching the target distribution. Motivated by this geometry, we construct a greedy least-action path through successive information projections, obtain a closed-form characterization of each projection through the Lambert W function, and establish a finite-step performance guarantee. We further show that state-dependent costs can be incorporated naturally by reshaping the underlying Gibbs reference, providing a thermodynamic interpretation of path penalties as modifications of the effective energy landscape. Together, these results provide a unified view of distributional evolution through least action, information geometry, and nonequilibrium thermodynamics.

</details>


### [108] [Convolutional Codes from Cyclic Codes with Guaranteed Free and Local Minimum Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08296)
*Khaled Abdel-Ghaffar,Daniel J. Costello,Juane Li,Shu Lin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于奇长度循环码的代数构造方法，可构造具有保证自由距离和局部最小距离的卷积码，无需计算机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积码通常依赖计算机搜索来确定自由距离和局部最小距离，缺乏可保证距离的理论构造方法。本文旨在提出一种简单有效的代数方法，在无需计算机搜索的前提下构造具有保证自由距离和局部最小距离的卷积码。

Method: 对任意正整数 r 与 t（1 ≤ r < t），利用满足包含条件 C₀ ⊃ C₁ ⊃ ... ⊃ C_{r-1} 的 r 个同长度 n 循环码构成码链，构造速率 r/t 的卷积码。该卷积码由限定在宽度为 n 的对角带内的半无限相同局部码链组成，每个局部码由码链中的 r 个循环码构成，是母码 C₀ 的特殊局部化子码。

Result: 所构造卷积码的每个局部码的最小距离 d_local 由码链中母码 C₀ 的最小距离 d₀ 下界保证，从而获得保证的自由距离和局部最小距离。

Conclusion: 该卷积码的局部结构使其能够基于母码 C₀ 设计的奇偶校验矩阵，采用滑动窗口译码方案进行译码。

Abstract: This paper presents an algebraic method to construct convolutional codes with guaranteed \emph{free and local minimum distances} without limit based on cyclic codes of odd lengths. The constructions are simple but effective, and no computer search is needed. For any two positive integers $r$ and $t$ with $1 \leq r < t$, a rate-$r/t$ convolutional code $\mathcal{C}_{\text{convol}}$ can be constructed by using a chain of $r$ cyclic codes $\mathcal{C}_0, \mathcal{C}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{C}_{r-1}$ of the same length $n$ which satisfy the inclusion condition, $\mathcal{C}_0 \supset \mathcal{C}_1 \supset \ldots \supset \mathcal{C}_{r-1}$. Such a convolutional code $\mathcal{C}_{\text{convol}}$ is composed of a \emph{semi-infinite chain of identical local codes} confined in a diagonal band of width $n$. Each local code $\mathcal{C}_{\text{local}}$ of $\mathcal{C}_{\text{convol}}$ is formed from the $r$ cyclic codes in the code chain and is a specially localized subcode of the \emph{mother code} $\mathcal{C}_0$ in the code chain. The minimum distance $d_{\text{local}}$ of each local code of $\mathcal{C}_{\text{convol}}$ is lower bounded by the minimum distance $d_0$ of the mother code $\mathcal{C}_0$ in the code chain. The local structure of $\mathcal{C}_{\text{convol}}$ allows it to be decoded based on a designed parity-check matrix of the mother code $\mathcal{C}_0$ using a sliding window decoding scheme.

</details>


### [109] [Non-Coherent Over-the-Air Federated Learning: Protocol, Convergence, and Device Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08312)
*Haifeng Wen,Nicolò Michelusi,Osvaldo Simeone,Yang Yang,Hong Xing*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出非相干空中联邦学习协议NCAirFL，通过二进制抖动、非相干检测与长期误差反馈，摆脱对瞬时CSI的依赖，并给出收敛性保证与联合设备调度/功率控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干AirFL依赖精确CSI、严格时频同步与频繁收发校准等苛刻PHY条件，难以满足且带来大量通信与计算开销，需设计免瞬时CSI的聚合方案以缓解RAN的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出宽带单天线MAC下的NCAirFL协议，利用二进制抖动（binary dithering）、无偏非相干检测与长期误差反馈实现模拟模型聚合；针对一般光滑非凸目标与恒定学习率推导收敛界；基于设备调度条件下单轮目标下降下界构建代理目标函数，联合优化设备选择与功率控制。

Result: 理论收敛界达到与通信理想FedAvg同阶的O(1/√T)速率；MNIST与CIFAR-10实验表明NCAirFL学习性能接近FedAvg，所提设备调度策略显著加速收敛。

Conclusion: NCAirFL在不依赖瞬时CSI的前提下实现了与理想FedAvg相当的收敛速率与实际学习性能，并通过联合设备选择与功率控制提升了数据与无线资源异构下的通信效率。

Abstract: To mitigate the scalability bottleneck in the radio access network (RAN) in federated edge learning (FEEL), over-the-air federated learning (AirFL) exploits waveform superposition over multiple-access channels (MACs) for analog model aggregation. However, coherent AirFL typically relies on stringent PHY-layer conditions such as accurate channel state information (CSI), tight time/frequency synchronization, and frequent transceiver calibration for signal alignment. However, these requirements, if not impossible to be met, incur substantial communication and computation overhead. In this paper, we propose a non-coherent AirFL (NCAirFL) protocol over a broadband single-antenna MAC, leveraging binary dithering, unbiased non-coherent detection, and long-term error feedback to waive the need for instantaneous CSI. For NCAirFL with general smooth non-convex objectives and a constant learning rate, we establish a convergence bound achieving the convergence rate in the same order of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ as communication-ideal FedAvg, where $T$ is the total number of communication rounds. To further improve communication efficiency under data and wireless resource heterogeneity, we also derive a lower bound on the expected single-round objective decrease in the global loss conditioned on device scheduling, building upon which a surrogate objective function is obtained for jointly optimal device selection and power control. Experimental results on MNIST and CIFAR-10 corroborate that NCAirFL achieves learning performance close to FedAvg in practical settings, with the proposed device scheduling policy substantially accelerating convergence.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum Matrix-Product Codes: CSS-T Characterization and Maximality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08520)
*Delio Jaramillo-Velez,Alessandro Neri,Adway Patra,Diego Ruano,Flavio Salizzoni*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将 CSS-T 码的 Schur 平方代数刻画推广到 (u|u+v) 构造，并在循环成分码情形下用定义的分圆集给出显式刻画，从而构造出新的、更长的 CSS-T 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSS-T 码因支持横向 T 门而对容错量子计算重要，其定义依赖满足特定 Schur 平方代数条件的嵌套经典二进制线性码。已有代数刻画未覆盖 (u|u+v) 这一传播规则，限制了可构造码的长度与数量。

Method: 将 CSS-T 码关于 Schur 平方的代数刻画推广至由两个成分码构成的矩阵乘积码，即 (u|u+v) 构造；针对循环成分码，用定义分圆集（cyclotomic sets）给出显式刻画，推广循环码的既有结果。

Result: 建立了 (u|u+v) 构造下 CSS-T 条件的代数刻画，并在循环成分码情形得到基于分圆集的显式判别准则，扩展了循环码的已有结论。

Conclusion: 该框架能够构造新的、更长的 CSS-T 码，为横向 T 门的容错量子计算提供更多候选码。

Abstract: CSS-T codes are quantum error-correcting codes that play an important role in fault-tolerant quantum computation, as they help mitigate the proliferation of errors. They admit a transversal T-gate and are defined from a pair of nested classical binary linear codes satisfying a specific algebraic condition expressed in terms of their Schur square. We extend this algebraic characterization to the propagation rule known as the $(u \mid u+v)$-construction, that is, the matrix-product code constructed from two constituent codes. Moreover, for cyclic constituent codes, we provide an explicit characterization in terms of the defining cyclotomic sets, which extends existing results for cyclic codes. This framework allows the construction of new and longer CSS-T codes.

</details>


### [111] [The Fusion Frame Phase Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08531)
*Haixia Liu,Bing Gao,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究融合框架相位恢复问题，测量矩阵为Haar测度下i.i.d.秩r正交投影，建立了投影矩阵集上函数的集中不等式，并证明两阶段初始化的梯度下降在r=O(1)时以O(d log²d)测量复杂度线性收敛至目标信号（相差全局相位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相位恢复算法的理论分析多依赖i.i.d.高斯或次高斯随机测量，缺乏针对融合框架（测量为秩r正交投影）场景的理论保证，因此需要为该类问题建立相应的概率工具与收敛性分析。

Method: 针对Haar测度抽取的i.i.d.秩r正交投影采样矩阵，首先推导秩r正交投影矩阵集上函数的集中不等式；随后以此为工具，结合两阶段初始化策略，分析梯度下降算法在融合框架相位恢复中的收敛行为，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 所提集中不等式为融合框架相位恢复的理论分析提供支撑；在此基础上证明梯度下降配合两阶段初始化可实现线性收敛，收敛到目标信号（至多相差全局相位），测量复杂度为O(d log²d)（当秩r=O(1)），数值结果验证了该收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文为融合框架相位恢复建立了秩r正交投影上的集中不等式，并据此给出梯度下降的线性收敛保证及O(d log²d)测量复杂度，拓展了相位恢复理论分析从高斯测量到融合框架测量的适用范围。

Abstract: The phase retrieval problem involves reconstructing a function or signal solely from the magnitude of linear measurements. Most theoretical analyses of phase retrieval algorithms rely on i.i.d. Gaussian random measurements or sub-Gaussian random measurements. In this paper, our focus is on the fusion frame phase retrieval problem, where the sampling matrices are i.i.d. rank-$r$ orthogonal projections drawn from the Haar measure. We present concentration inequalities for functions on the set of rank-$r$ orthogonal projection matrices. These inequalities are crucial for the theoretical analysis of the fusion frame phase retrieval problem. Based on these inequalities, we demonstrate that gradient descent, combined with a two-stage initialization, achieves linear convergence to the target signal up to a global phase with a measurement complexity of $O(d\log^2 d)$ when the rank $r = O(1)$. We verify this convergence through numerical results.

</details>


### [112] [Sequential Lossy Compression With Causal Conditional Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08611)
*Photios A. Stavrou,Zixuan He*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究因果条件感知准则下的序贯有损压缩，针对一阶马尔可夫源建立有限时域非预期率失真感知函数（NRDPF），给出单次变长和率的下上界，并对时变标量高斯-马尔可夫源证明高斯最优性并获得闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有损压缩多关注率失真或率失真感知，但缺乏在因果（非预期）条件下、以比较相同重构历史下源分布与重构分布为准则的序贯压缩理论刻画，需建立相应函数与界。

Method: 对一阶马尔可夫源，构建带逐阶段约束的有限时域非预期率失真感知函数（NRDPF）；利用加强的强函数表示引理（SFRL）与公共随机性，推导最小变长和率的单次下界与上界；对逐点MSE与条件平方Wasserstein-2保真度下的时变标量高斯-马尔可夫源，证明高斯最优性并给出对数方差刻画。

Result: 建立了一阶马尔可夫源NRDPF的单次变长和率下界与上界；对时变标量高斯-马尔可夫源得到闭式解，该解在感知无约束时退化为经典高斯NRDF，在源平稳无记忆时退化为经典高斯RDPF。

Conclusion: 所提框架统一并推广了非预期率失真与率失真感知理论，给出可计算的闭式解，为因果条件感知约束下的序贯有损压缩提供理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study sequential lossy compression under a causal conditional perception criterion comparing source and reconstruction distributions given the same reconstruction history. For first-order Markov sources, we formulate the finite-horizon nonanticipative rate-distortion-perception function (NRDPF) with stagewise constraints and establish one-shot lower and upper bounds on the minimum variable-length sum rate using a strengthened strong functional-representation lemma (SFRL) and common randomness. For time-varying scalar Gauss--Markov sources under pointwise mean-squared error (MSE) and conditional squared Wasserstein-$2$ fidelity, we prove Gaussian optimality, derive a log-variance characterization, and obtain a closed-form solution that recovers the classical Gaussian nonanticipative rate-distortion function (NRDF) when perception is unconstrained and the classical Gaussian RDPF when the source is stationary and memoryless.

</details>


### [113] [Hybrid Continuous DoA Estimation with Shared-Radius Co-Prime Circular Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08827)
*Keyvan Aghababaiyan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种共半径互质圆阵结构及混合连续恢复框架，实现3D空间高分辨率连续二维DoA估计，联合估计方位角与俯仰角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有圆阵DoA估计多依赖相位模式变换将圆阵映射为虚拟线阵，存在离散网格失配限制与高计算复杂度（特征值分解）；密集均匀阵存在互耦泄漏问题。需要一种直接在物理空间域工作、无网格失配且低复杂度的高分辨率二维连续DoA估计方法。

Method: 设计共半径互质圆阵：两个天线数互质、共享同一半径R的均匀圆子阵，结构本身抑制互耦泄漏。提出混合连续恢复框架，直接在物理空间域工作，结合快速离散粗网格搜索与群智能连续精细化阶段，绕过网格失配并避免特征值分解。利用Niven定理进行严格理论分析，证明真实信源方向的空间唯一性，解决相位模糊。

Result: 仿真表明，相比均匀配置，该混合方案在低信噪比下具有更优分辨率和更低RMSE，在高信噪比下渐近收敛至理论Cramer-Rao界（CRB）。

Conclusion: 共半径互质圆阵结合混合连续恢复框架，可在3D空间实现高分辨率、低复杂度、无网格失配的二维连续DoA估计，并在低SNR下优于均匀阵、高SNR下逼近CRB。

Abstract: This paper proposes a shared-radius co-prime circular array for high-resolution, continuous 2D Direction-of-Arrival (DoA) estimation in 3D space, jointly estimating azimuth and elevation angles. The proposed architecture consists of two uniform circular sub-arrays with co-prime antenna counts sharing a common radius RR, a design that intrinsically suppresses mutual coupling leakage compared to dense uniform arrays. Unlike existing works that rely on complex phase-mode transformations to map circular structures to virtual linear arrays, we introduce a hybrid continuous-recovery framework operating directly in the physical spatial domain. By integrating a fast, discrete coarse-grid search with a swarm-intelligence continuous refinement stage, the proposed method completely bypasses discrete grid-mismatch limitations and computationally expensive eigenvalue decompositions. A rigorous theoretical analysis using Nivens Theorem establishes the spatial uniqueness of the true source direction, effectively resolving phase ambiguities. Simulation results demonstrate that this hybrid scheme achieves superior resolution and lower Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) at low Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNR) compared to uniform configurations, while asymptotically converging to the theoretical Cramer-Rao Bound (CRB) at high SNRs.

</details>


### [114] [Linear Programming Bounds for LCD Codes via Gauss Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08662)
*Ming-Hsuan Kang,Maosheng Xiong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明 q∈{2,3} 时线性码为 LCD 当且仅当其权重枚举子的某个单位根值幅度为 q^{k/2}，并将该值的相位（二元情形还含宇称型）转化为权重分布的精确线性约束，嵌入高斯-相位线性规划，在二元与三元范围内系统强化 Hamming LCD 松弛，二元情形相对混合联合权重枚举子 LP 取得 4 处严格改进，均以有理可行性见证与整数 Farkas 证书验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性互补对偶（LCD）码存在性界通常依赖 Hamming/MacWilliams 线性规划松弛，这种松弛只使用码与对偶码的普通重量分布，算术信息不足，导致界不够紧。作者希望把 LCD 这一算术条件（而非仅组合条件）纳入 LP 中，从而在不显著增大规划规模的前提下收紧上界。

Method: 首先刻画 LCD 的权重枚举子判据：对 q∈{2,3}，k 维 F_q 线性码为 LCD，当且仅当其权重枚举子在某个单位根处取值幅度恰为 q^{k/2}。继而将该值的相位（二元时结合宇称型）转换为对重量分布的精确线性约束，构建"高斯-相位线性规划"：只使用码及其对偶的普通重量分布，在标准 Hamming/MacWilliams 约束之外仅增加常数规模的 branch 方程。

Result: 在经审计的二元与三元范围内计算，显示该规划对 Hamming LCD 松弛有系统性强化。二元情形下，与已有的混合联合权重枚举子 LP 比较，得到 4 处严格改进，每处使基准上界降低 1。每一处严格比较均用有理可行性见证和整数 Farkas 证书精确验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将 LCD 的算术判据（单位根取值幅度）转化为常数规模的线性约束，构造出规模接近标准 Hamming LP 却携带额外算术信息的高斯-相位 LP，可系统性收紧 LCD 码存在性上界；其严格改进具备可精确验证的证书，为 LCD 码界提供了更精细且可复现的线性规划方法。

Abstract: For $q\in\set{2,3}$, we show that a $k$-dimensional linear code over the finite field $\F_q$ of order $q$ is linear complementary dual (LCD) exactly when one root-of-unity value of its weight enumerator has magnitude $q^{k/2}$. We convert the phase of this value, together with the parity type in the binary case, into exact linear constraints on the weight distribution and incorporate them into a Gauss-phase linear program. The resulting program uses only the ordinary weight distributions of the code and its dual and adds only a constant-size set of branch equations to the usual Hamming/MacWilliams constraints, so it remains close in size to the standard Hamming LP while retaining additional arithmetic information. Computations over the audited binary and ternary ranges show systematic strengthening of the Hamming LCD relaxation. In the binary case, comparison with the established mixed joint-weight-enumerator LP yields four strict improvements, lowering the benchmark upper bound by one in each case. Each strict comparison is verified exactly by rational feasibility witnesses and integer Farkas certificates.

</details>


### [115] [A Note on Scaling in Randomly Rotated Quantization and Its Connection to the CDEF +1 Pythagorean Relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08759)
*Uri Erez*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文指出随机旋转量化中的重建缩放系数与经典CDEF公式中的Wiener系数和无偏系数存在对应关系，并澄清其有限维、依赖于具体实现的性质以及大维极限下的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于随机旋转的量化方案近期重新受到关注，尤其是MMSE与无偏重建缩放的作用，但尚缺乏与经典统计信号处理和通信理论结果的明确联系与解释。

Method: 将EDEN系列工作所用两种重建缩放解释为经典CDEF中Wiener与无偏系数的有限维、依赖于旋转实现的对应物；在有限块长下分析CDEF +1关系的逐点成立性及其旋转平均后的失效；并通过大维极限分析随机旋转向量经验坐标统计趋于i.i.d.高斯、旋转相关量集中，从而恢复经典SNR关系。

Result: 有限块长下，CDEF +1关系对每个旋转实现逐点成立，表现为精确的几何（勾股）恒等式，但旋转平均后不再成立；当维度趋于无穷时，经典关系SNR_MMSE = SNR_MMSE,U + 1得以恢复。此外，EDEN的Haar旋转形式对每个有限维都保证精确的条件无偏性，强于CDEF的二阶无偏性。

Conclusion: 随机旋转量化与经典CDEF/Wiener理论存在自然对应，但EDEN在有限维下提供了更强的精确条件无偏性；随机旋转在量化中兼具坐标近似高斯化与跨量化分支重建误差去相关两种不同作用。

Abstract: Quantization schemes based on randomized rotations have recently received renewed attention, including the roles of MMSE and unbiased reconstruction scalings. In this note, we point out the connection to classical results in statistical signal processing and communication theory. Specifically, the two reconstruction scales used in the EDEN line of work admit a natural interpretation as finite-dimensional, realization-dependent counterparts of the Wiener and unbiased coefficients in the classical CDEF formulation. At finite blocklength, the CDEF +1 relation holds pointwise for each rotation realization as an exact geometric (Pythagorean) identity, but does not hold after averaging the distortions over the rotation. The classical SNR relation $\sf{SNR}_{\rm MMSE}=\sf{SNR}_{\rm MMSE,U}+1$ is recovered as $d\to\infty$: once the overall scale is handled separately, the empirical coordinate statistics of a randomly rotated vector approach their i.i.d. Gaussian counterparts, and the rotation-dependent quantities concentrate. Importantly, EDEN goes beyond this classical correspondence: for every finite $d$, its Haar-rotation formulation guarantees exact conditional unbiasedness, a stronger property than the second-order notion of unbiasedness in CDEF. We further comment on two distinct roles random rotations play in quantization: one is approximate Gaussianization of the coordinates; the other is decorrelation of reconstruction errors across quantization branches.

</details>


### [116] [The Weight Spectrum of the Affine Grassmann Code $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08784)
*Prasant Singh,Rohit Yadav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Grassmannian $G_{3,6}$ 的仿射开胞${\mathbb A}^9$ 得到的仿射Grassmann码 $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$，通过按非零系数出现的最大子式尺寸对码字分类，确定了所有可能的Hamming重量及每个重量对应的码字数目，从而得到完整的重量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射Grassmann码的重量分布是编码理论中的重要课题，而 $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$ 的完整重量谱此前尚未确定，需要一种系统的方法来刻画码字结构并计算其重量分布。

Method: 将码字表示为一般 $3\times3$ 矩阵所有尺寸子式的线性组合，并依据非零系数出现的最大子式尺寸对码字进行分类；利用该分类逐一确定码字可能的Hamming重量。

Result: 确定了 $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$ 码字所有可能的Hamming重量，并对每个重量计算了达到该重量的码字数目，从而给出了完整的重量谱。

Conclusion: 通过基于最大子式尺寸的码字分类，可有效导出仿射Grassmann码 $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$ 的完整重量谱，为相关码的重量分布研究提供了完整结果。

Abstract: In this article, we consider the affine Grassmann code $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$, obtained from the affine open cell ${\mathbb A}^9$ of the Grassmannian $G_{3,6}$. We exploit the representation of codewords as linear combinations of minors of all sizes of a generic $3\times3$ matrix and classify them according to the largest size of a minor occurring with a nonzero coefficient. Using this classification, we determine all possible Hamming weights of codewords of $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$ and, for each weight, compute the number of codewords attaining that weight. Consequently, we obtain the complete weight spectrum of the affine Grassmann code $C^{\mathbb A}(3,6)$.

</details>


### [117] [On Weighted Mathai-Haubold Entropy Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08889)
*Oindrali Das,Siddhartha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出加权Mathai-Haubold熵及其剩余与过去版本，构建基于该度量的老化类，讨论相关不等式，并给出非参数估计量及其性质，通过蒙特卡洛模拟以偏差和均方误差评估估计量性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵度量（如Mathai-Haubold熵）缺乏加权形式的推广，且其剩余与过去版本在可靠性、寿命分析与老化研究中尚未被系统刻画；需要更灵活的度量来区分不同寿命分布及刻画老化特征。

Method: 提出加权Mathai-Haubold熵及对应的剩余熵与过去熵；基于新的剩余与过去熵度量建立老化类；证明与三种度量相关的若干不等式；引入非参数估计量并研究其性质；利用蒙特卡洛模拟，以偏差与均方误差评估估计量表现。

Result: 建立了加权Mathai-Haubold熵、剩余熵与过去熵的理论性质与相关不等式；给出基于新度量的老化类刻画；非参数估计量性质得到分析，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟以偏差和均方误差量化其性能。

Conclusion: 加权Mathai-Haubold熵及其剩余、过去形式为寿命分布比较与老化分析提供了有效工具，配套的非参数估计方法在模拟中表现可评估，可应用于可靠性工程与生存分析。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose weighted Mathai-Haubold entropy along with their residual and past versions and study their properties. We develop aging classes based on the weighted Mathai-Haubold residual and past entropy measures. Also, some inequalities related to the three proposed measures are discussed. Non-parametric estimators of the proposed measures are introduced and their properties are investigated. Performance of this estimator is evaluated by means of bias and mean squared error using Monte-Carlo simulations.

</details>
